NAMING CONVENTION — LEVEL 4 / ANNEXURES

This document owns the canonical naming framework.

Authoritative naming layers:
  1. stable architecture/type/registry identity;
  2. canonical qualified name;
  3. typed binding target kind;
  4. display name;
  5. aliases;

Normative registry/binding maps use stable IDs or canonical qualified names. Display aliases cannot create
authority, ownership, dependencies or semantic identity. See REG-ARCH-NAME-001 and NAME-001..008.

DEFINITION OWNERSHIP
  Level 1 Human Design             : plain-language meaning; no recurring canonical-definition ownership.
  Level 2 System & Flow Design     : architecture/flow contracts and explanation-completeness metadata.
  Level 3 Detailed Technical       : owner-qualified state/functions/executable/profile contracts.
  Specialist Theories/Architectures: complete [TH-*] theory and [RA-*] named reference-architecture semantics.
  Level 4 Annexures                : definitions, aliases, registries, SchemaIDs, invariants, proofs/tests,
  audit; concise version history is the final section.

GCCR-1 — GARDEN COMPACT CANONICAL REPRESENTATION

Status: NORMATIVE SOURCE ENCODING for mechanically structured canonical material.
Purpose: reduce permanent source size/token overhead without semantic elision.

Records:
  @SC|Name|SchemaID|-or-field-payload      Technical owner-level schema/field contract.
  @CAT|SchemaID|Class|Name|Context         Annexure Artifact Catalogue entry; full SchemaID remains literal.
  @D|CanonicalID|NormativeText             Sole owner-definition occurrence for that canonical definition ID.
  @N|StructuralOrNormativeText             Retained non-definition text, including summaries/references/continuations.

Rules:
  - Full canonical IDs remain literal; GCCR-1 has no hidden prefix state or omitted identifier fragments.
  - `|` is structural only in GCCR records; payload punctuation and ordering remain normative as before.
  - ID-to-definition separator punctuation replaced by the @D record boundary is structural, not semantic.
  - A canonical definition ID appears in @D exactly once at its owner location; repeated summaries/references use @N.
  - Display whitespace, decorative separator lines and line wrapping are non-semantic and may be regenerated.
  - GCCR expansion is deterministic; generated pretty/readable views are DERIVED_VIEW and non-authoritative.
  - No LLM is trusted to invent omitted semantics. Expansion reconstructs from these records only.
  - GCCR compaction is not DEV-AI source elision: every canonical semantic token remains in source.
  - Long GCCR records are exempt from the ordinary 120-character human-reader wrapping convention.

BOOK-STYLE GLOSSARY — DEFERRED GENERATED READER VIEW

STATUS
  PLACEHOLDER — generate for the final reader/design edition; not part of the current normative semantic source.

GENERATION RULE
  The glossary is a non-authoritative DERIVED_VIEW generated from the current accepted H/S/T/THA/A owner contracts,
  [A-DICT], [A-CATALOG], [A-REG], [A-INVARIANTS] and [A-PROOF] under the accepted DesignEpoch/source roots.

  It may be regenerated whenever needed without changing canonical Garden semantics. Generated wording/order may vary
  unless a future pinned deterministic GlossaryGenerationProfile requires byte-identical output. Canonical identities,
  meanings, authority, epistemic status and references MUST resolve to the accepted source owners.

FINAL-DESIGN REQUIREMENT
  Before publication of a final reader edition, generate an alphabetical glossary/index with source-bound owner
  references and verify that every entry resolves without ambiguity. The generated glossary MUST remain a reader view
  and MUST NOT become an independent definition, registry, authority, proof, evidence or DesignEpoch source.

CURRENT BODY
  OMITTED INTENTIONALLY TO REDUCE DUPLICATION. No normative definition is removed by this omission.

SemanticAnchor: [A-REG]

REG-DEGRADATION-001 — Pre-Authorized Degradation Profile Registry
Binds DegradationPlan to ordered states, resource thresholds, transition guards, hysteresis, sheddable/non-sheddable
functions, authority/assurance ceilings, qualified fallback, recovery evidence, DesignEpoch and provenance.
Resource failure never creates permission to shed Constitution, K0P, HSA or other applicable non-sheddable safeguards.

REG-HARDWARE-ASSURANCE-001 — Hardware Assurance Capability Registry
Binds HardwareAssuranceCapabilityAttestation to exact hardware, firmware/microcode, driver, runtime/accelerator and
attestation roots; observable telemetry/introspection/isolation capabilities; freshness/invalidators; and maximum
assurance/authority class supported by that evidence. Capability attestation never creates legitimate authority.

REG-FUNCTION-001 physical-cognitive additions:
  execute_degradation_state; resolve_crossmodal_conflict; attest_hardware_capability.

REG-FUNCTION-001 physical-cognitive amended existing contracts:
  execute_sub_epoch_adaptation; handle_novelty; resolve_common_sense_frame; assess_value_intent;
  assess_terminal_ego; audit_mechanistic_behavior; execute_sensorimotor_loop.

REG-XCUT-001 additionally binds physical-cognitive hardening across SEA/NRS/CSR/VIP/TED/MI/EC, AAP/Safety,
critical continuity, Knowledge, Proof, Security, Runtime/Hardware and the two new registries.

REGISTRY ANNEXURE — CANONICAL REGISTRIES, CATALOGUES & BINDING MAPS

CURRENT ANNEXURE STATUS
This companion file contains the current Technical Dictionary, canonical Registry Annexure, complete Artifact
Catalogue and machine-projection generation contract, Annexure A, Annexure B and Annexure C.

This release does not duplicate qualified deterministic field/layout expansions in authoritative human source.
The current
SchemaID catalogue and semantic field contracts remain SOURCE. Concrete machine layouts become GENERATED only when a
MachineProjectionReceipt satisfies DEV-AI-026; otherwise the affected material is SOURCE or MIXED.

The HUMAN DESIGN, SYSTEM & FLOW DESIGN, DETAILED TECHNICAL SPECIFICATION and this ANNEXURES file share one canonical
current document release and one DesignEpoch candidate binding.

Registry definitions are centralized here. Technical Design sections contain RegistryRefs; the Human Design uses
plain-language cross-references. Each REG identifier is unique and
authoritative for its static registry/catalogue/binding-map content. Operational algorithms, proofs, policies, and
domain semantics remain in their substantive sections or dedicated annexures.

REG-FUNCTION-001 — Function Contract Registry
Every executable function resolves one FunctionContract containing semantic input/output types, closed effect vector,
AuthorityRequirement/consent where applicable, FailureModeSet/result algebra, dependencies/invalidators, resource and
termination bounds, DesignEpoch/environment/knowledge bindings, provenance and explanation trace.

REG-RESULT-ALG-001 — Typed Result Algebra Registry
Bool is admitted only for total deterministic predicates over a complete TRUE/FALSE semantic domain. Epistemic, proof,
safety, authority, policy, compliance, migration, distributed-finality, lifecycle and other non-binary operations use
owner-qualified result algebras preserving every applicable terminal state.

REG-PARAM-001 — Qualified Profile Parameter Registry
Operational constants are typed as FORMAL_BOUND, MEASURED_VALUE, ENGINEERING_TARGET or PROFILE_PARAMETER and bind owner,
scope, unit/type, evidence/source, version, validity, uncertainty where applicable and invalidators.

REG-EMERGENCY-ADMISSION-001 — Emergency Admission Contract Registry
Binds HazardPredicate, evidence/sensor/environment/calibration state, pre-authorized legitimate authority, authenticity,
scope/expiry, SafeActionSet, K0P/SafetyEnvelope, consent/delegation where applicable, fallback, recovery and audit.

REG-THEORY-EQ-001 — Theory Equation Contract Registry
Binds equation variables/types, dimensions/units, domain or graph/state space, operators/frames, assumptions, boundary/
initial conditions, stochastic semantics, conservation/source terms, numerical representation, validity and falsifiers.

REG-HUMAN-INPUT-001 — Human Input Boundary Registry
HumanInput = EpistemicContribution × AuthorityContribution. Epistemic reliability may be calibrated; valid consent,
rights and lawful authority remain separate and cannot be down-weighted by predictive track record/model confidence.

REG-PHYS-001 — GSL-PHYSICS Profile / Telemetry Assimilation Registry
Owns the current v1.1 quantity/unit/frame/calibration/model/simulation/conservation foundation and the
TelemetryTemporalBinding, PhysicalTwin and DataAssimilationProfile generated refinements. Each entry binds admitted
measurement types, units/frames, source and clock provenance, event/ingest time semantics, synchronization uncertainty,
latency/jitter bounds, sequence/causal ordering, duplicate/gap/late/reorder policy, observability/identifiability,
model/filter/update behavior, uncertainty propagation, event-loop/liveness semantics, invalidators and prevalidated
fallback. A registry entry is not empirical validation or controller qualification.

Canonical identifier normalization:
  legacy GSL-PHYSICS v1.1 ratification labels PHY-027..030 -> PHYS-027..030.
The exact profile-scoped mapping preserves semantic identity. Bare PHY-* is retired and is not accepted as a canonical
machine-facing invariant namespace. PHYS-001..020 retain their existing identities and meanings.

SemanticAnchor: [A-RIGHTS]
REG-RIGHTS-001 — Human Rights Registry Status
  ACTIVE                : RIGHT-001..RIGHT-025 — substantively ratified rights.
  RESERVED / NON-ACTIVE : RIGHT-026 — no predicate, veto, authority, obligation, permission or enforcement effect.
  ON_HOLD / NON-ACTIVE  : RIGHT-027 — no predicate, veto, authority, obligation, permission or enforcement effect.
  Activation of a non-active slot requires explicit constitutional ratification of a substantive definition.

REG-THEORY-ARCH-001 — Theory–Architecture Binding Registry
  SRT : GardenCore.Semantics.GSL; Capability.Knowledge; Engine.Reason; Engine.Research; Engine.Dependency;
        Domain.Applied.Space; Capability.State.TimeService.
  VSM v2.0 : HumanCore.Identity; HumanCore.Privacy; Capability.Compute.Runtime; Capability.Knowledge;
        Capability.Knowledge.Curation; Capability.State.TimeService; Capability.State.EventService;
        Capability.Resilience.Continuity; Domain.Mathematics; Engine.Research.
        Conditional: Algebra.Bridge; TFT; MAC where representation/transport/swarm semantics apply.
  RPM v2.0 : Capability.Compute.Runtime; Capability.Compute.Execution; Capability.State.TimeService;
        Capability.State.EventService; Engine.Reason; Engine.Policy; Capability.Trust.Safety;
        Capability.Authority.Governance; Domain.Mathematics.
        Conditional: VSM; PPM; TFT; MAC; Algebra.Bridge; applicable DomainRef.
  PPM v2.0 : Engine.Reason; Engine.Evolve; Engine.Policy; Capability.Compute.Runtime;
        Capability.Compute.Execution; Capability.State.TimeService; Capability.Trust.Safety;
        Capability.Knowledge; Domain.Mathematics.
        Conditional: RPM; VSM; TFT; FET; MAC; Algebra.Bridge; applicable DomainRef.
  TFT v1.1 : Domain.Mathematics; Engine.Research; Engine.Reason; Capability.Infrastructure.Network;
        Capability.Infrastructure.Fabric; Capability.Infrastructure.Hardware; Capability.State.TimeService;
        Domain.Applied.Energy; Domain.Computing.Quantum; applicable physical-domain DomainRef.
        Physical applications additionally bind GSL-PHYSICS and applicable physical-domain owners.
  FET : Capability.Trust.Safety; Capability.Resilience.Emergency; Capability.Compute.Execution;
        Capability.Infrastructure.Hardware; Capability.Compute.Runtime; Capability.Trust.Security;
        Capability.State.EventService; Capability.Resilience.Continuity.
  SCT : Engine.Proof; Capability.Knowledge; Engine.Reason; Engine.Research; Engine.Search; Engine.Explanation;
        Engine.Dependency; Engine.Compliance; Capability.Authority.Governance.

This central TheoryArchitectureBinding registry is authoritative. Binding targets resolve through
REG-ARCH-NAME-001/BindingTargetRef. Human/display aliases may be generated from these qualified targets but never
become independent binding keys.

Every applicable architecture object declares its bindings; every theory has implementing architecture objects; an
architecture object or phenomenon may legitimately have no applicable binding.

Unified cross-theory role: layered reality constrains state; persistent state carries continuity; reactive/proactive
regulation govern response and prevention; TFT models flow/transport; FET supplies emergency reflex semantics; SCT
supplies epistemic/coherence discipline. Any unifying equation remains a Garden model under declared assumptions, not
proof of universal physics.

REG-NAME-001 — Canonical Public Naming Registry Layered Reality : Stratified Reality Theory / SRT. Persistent State :
  Valence Self Model / VSM. Reactive Regulation : Reactive Persistent Machine / RPM. Proactive Regulation : Proactive
  Persistent Machine / PPM. Transport Phenomena : Transport Field Theory / TFT. Emergency Response : Functional
  Emergency Theory / FET. Structural Coherence : Structural Coherence Theory / SCT. Public display names are ordinary
  and intuitive; acronyms/technical names remain stable lineage and machine aliases. Display-name change never changes
  semantic identity, theory status or evidence.

REG-TYPE-001 — Canonical Type Resolution Registry Every generated machine type resolves to a GSL primitive, generic
  constructor, Core Object, generated artifact type, canonical enum/alias family, or owner-qualified imported type with
  explicit version binding. An unresolved type is a compile/conformance failure. Generic Ref<T> is the sole canonical
  reference constructor.

GSL-PHYSICS v1.1 TYPE-RESOLUTION
These are generated refinements or result types of the current physical profile; they do not create new GSL primitives
or additional unqualified SchemaID definitions.

  MeasurementRef
    Generated alias Ref<Measurement<T>> preserving the concrete measurement type parameter.

  TelemetryTemporalBinding
    Generated record binding event/observation time, ingest/processing time, clock domain/synchronization uncertainty,
    sequence/causal metadata, latency/jitter, reorder/watermark policy, late/duplicate/gap class and provenance.

  DataAssimilationProfile
    Generated profile record for admitted measurements, units/frames, calibration/environment, temporal order,
    observability/identifiability, model/filter/update semantics, uncertainty, event-loop behavior and fallback.

  PhysicalTwin
    Owner-qualified refinement of DigitalTwin. Bare DigitalTwin remains owner-resolved; this refinement does not create
    a third unqualified DigitalTwin SchemaID.

  TelemetryAdmissionResult
    ADMIT_IN_EVENT_TIME_ORDER | ADMIT_WITH_DECLARED_APPROXIMATION | BUFFER_UNTIL_WATERMARK | DUPLICATE_IGNORED |
    LATE_DATA_CORRECTION_REQUIRED | GAP_DETECTED | QUARANTINED | REJECTED | NON_IDENTIFIABLE | UNKNOWN | STALE |
    BLOCKED.

  PhysicalDataAssimilationResult
    Generated total result record binding telemetry dispositions, pre/post twin roots, state/uncertainty/residual delta,
    identifiability, invalidation, event-loop/fallback disposition and provenance.

PHYSICAL-COGNITIVE HARDENING TYPE-RESOLUTION
These refinements/enums are canonical generated type families under REG-TYPE-001. They are not SchemaID-bearing
artifacts and create no new GSL primitive.

  IntrospectionSideEffectClass
    NON_INVASIVE_TELEMETRY | BOUNDED_INLINE_PROBE | INVASIVE_INTROSPECTION |
    DESTRUCTIVE_OR_PERTURBATIVE_ANALYSIS.

    NORMAL | RESOURCE_CONSTRAINED | ASSURANCE_DEGRADED | SAFETY_PRESERVATION |
    MINIMUM_RISK | AUTHORITY_REVOKED.

  CrossModalConflictDisposition
    NO_MATERIAL_CONFLICT | MODALITY_DISAGREEMENT | COMMON_MODE_SUSPECTED |
    MODEL_SENSOR_CONFLICT | FALLBACK_REQUIRED | UNKNOWN.

Human phrases such as "sensor conflict" are display descriptions only. They do not create a separate SENSOR_CONFLICT
state or enum. The owner-qualified CrossModalConflictDisposition values above are canonical for machine projection.

BRIDGE TYPE-RESOLUTION
Bridge introduces no new GSL primitive. The following names resolve through the existing type-resolution order.

  Scalar
    Generated mathematical semantic scalar. Implementation representation is bound through Represented<T>;
    Scalar is not a universal raw Float.

  DimensionlessScalar
    Refinement of Scalar with dimension = 1.

  Count
    Non-negative integer/count refinement.

  SimilarityThreshold
    Dimensionless scalar threshold used only through QualifiedProfileParameter where operational.

  IndexSpace
    Generated mathematical index-space descriptor with stable identity, ordering/index semantics and validity scope.

  OrderedIndexSet<T>
    Ordered finite/indexable set over T.

  Axis / SemanticLabel
    Generated identifiers/labels used only with explicit AxisSemantics/DimensionSemantics bindings.

  DimensionSemantics
    Generated record describing one represented semantic dimension, including meaning and applicable unit/frame/basis.

  AxisSemantics
    Generated record binding axis identity/order, index space, semantic label and applicable dimension semantics.

  SparsityProfile / QuantizationProfile
    Generated representation profiles with explicit algorithm/version, approximation/error and validity semantics.

  SpectralAnalysisMethod
    Generated method record identifying exact/symbolic/validated-numerical/iterative spectral analysis, solver/config,
    error bounds, assumptions and provenance. A method record is not a proof by itself.

  RepresentationKind
    DENSE_VECTOR | DENSE_MATRIX | DENSE_TENSOR | CSR | CSC | BLOCK_SPARSE | LOW_RANK | FACTORIZED |
    GRAPH_OPERATOR | IMPLICIT | EMBEDDING | OTHER_QUALIFIED.

  ComputeTargetKind
    CPU | GPU | NPU | SPARSE_ACCELERATOR | DISTRIBUTED | QUANTUM.

  ComputeTargetBinding
    Generated sum/reference binding to the concrete eligible hardware/profile/architecture target selected for the
    ComputePlan. The binding carries actual device/profile/version identity; target kind alone is insufficient.

  SchedulingConstraints
    Generated refinement of the current scheduling contract, compatible with ScheduledWork/Runtime admission semantics.

  OperatorSignature
    Generated stable semantic/operator signature binding operator contract, domain/codomain, representation plan and
    relevant version/validity information.

  TokenID
    Generated stable tokenizer-vocabulary-bound token identifier; it has no model-independent semantic meaning.

  LibraryVersion
    Generated name/version/hash binding for a library or kernel dependency used by a compiled operator realization.

  InvalidationTrigger / InvalidationCondition
    Generated typed conditions identifying dependency, model, environment, representation, hardware, compiler,
    precision, policy or DesignEpoch changes that stale/revalidate an optimized artifact.

  ResourceRecurrence
    Generated mathematical recurrence record over typed Resource values with assumptions, bounds and provenance.

  RankingFunction
    Generated specialization of TransformFunction whose FunctionContract binds the required well-founded measure/order.
    Bridge technical schemas may use Ref<TransformFunction>; the ranking-function role is carried by that contract.

  ApproximationContract
    Generated record declaring EXACT | APPROXIMATE | LOSSY semantics, error/loss bounds, correspondence obligations,
    validity domain and fallback behavior.

  ResourceUsage
    Generated runtime measurement record over typed resources. Measured values require actual execution/measurement
    provenance.

  ApproximationStatus
    EXACT | APPROXIMATE_WITH_BOUND | LOSSY_WITH_DOCUMENTATION | INCOMPATIBLE | UNKNOWN.

  SemanticProperty / LostProperties
    Generated stable property identifier/set used by projection receipts. Presence in a list does not prove
      preservation;
    exact preservation is bound to correspondence proof where required.

  SpeedupBasis
    MODEL_PROJECTION | ENGINEERING_TARGET | BENCHMARK_ESTIMATE | MEASURED_COMPARISON.

  SpeedupEstimate
    Dimensionless performance-ratio refinement that remains MODEL_PROJECTION/ENGINEERING_TARGET unless measured.

  SpeedupMeasurement
    Dimensionless measured performance-ratio refinement requiring evidence-bound benchmark/measurement provenance.

  CorrespondenceStatus
    PROVEN_EXACT | APPROXIMATE_WITH_BOUND | LOSSY_WITH_DOCUMENTATION | UNKNOWN | NOT_APPLICABLE.

  InvalidationState
    VALID | STALE | REVALIDATION_REQUIRED | INVALID | UNKNOWN.

  FallbackDisposition
    CANONICAL_EXACT | CANONICAL_NONOPTIMIZED | DEFER | RESOURCE_UNKNOWN | UNSUPPORTED | BLOCK.

  FallbackResult
    SUCCESS | PARTIAL | FAILED | INCONCLUSIVE | UNKNOWN.

  OptimizationKind
    SPECTRAL_PRUNING | TENSOR_BATCHING | FIXED_POINT_CACHING | APPROXIMATE_PRUNING | MATRIX_REACHABILITY |
    OPERATOR_PRECOMPILATION | INVERSE_APPROXIMATION | ANALYTICAL_PRUNING | RESOURCE_GUIDANCE.

  FixedPointStatus
    PROVEN_EXISTS | PROVEN_UNIQUE | PROVEN_NONE | MULTIPLE_POSSIBLE | UNKNOWN.

  ConvergenceStatus
    PROVEN_CONVERGENT | LOCALLY_CONVERGENT | CONDITIONALLY_CONVERGENT | NONCONVERGENT | UNKNOWN.

  StabilityStatus
    CONTRACTIVE | STABLE | CRITICAL | EXPANSIVE | UNKNOWN.

  TerminationStatus
    PROVEN_TERMINATING | PROVEN_NONTERMINATING | BOUNDED_BY_CONTRACT | CONDITIONAL | UNKNOWN.

  RepresentationBindingRef
    Generated existential/reference alias for a version-bound RepresentationBinding<T,R> whose concrete T/R types remain
    available in the referenced binding.

  OperatorRepresentationRef
    Generated existential/reference alias for a version-bound OperatorRepresentation<X,Y>.

  OperatorContractRef
    Generated existential/reference alias for a version-bound OperatorContract<X,Y>.

  RepresentationMetricRef
    Generated existential/reference alias for a version-bound RepresentationMetric<T>.

  RepresentationProjectionReceiptRef
    Generated existential/reference alias for a version-bound RepresentationProjectionReceipt<T,R>.

  NeuralOutputTensorRef
    Generated reference alias to the exact TypedTensor<Axes,Value> specialization declared by the model/output binding.

Bridge reuses existing canonical types where semantics already exist:
  DeterministicResolutionContext for determinism binding;
  TemporalContextBinding for temporal relation-projection binding;
  EpistemicApplicabilityRecord for epistemic relation-projection binding;
  CacheBindingManifest for dependency/assumption/knowledge/environment cache validity;
  ValidationProfile for rejected-candidate/shadow validation;
  ProofAttemptEnvelope for model-checking/proof result binding;
  AssurancePlan for the recursive-analysis validation path;
  AssuranceReceipt for validation receipts;
  Evidence for benchmark/measurement support;
  FailureModeRecord for typed RepresentationResult incompatibility/failure reason payloads;
  StoredArtifact for model/protected-content storage identity;
  DataLifecyclePolicy and PolicyArtifact for retention/access binding.

Any unresolved Bridge type or alias is MALFORMED and blocks machine certification.

REG-FAIL-001 — Failure Mode Registry Failure modes are typed by source, trigger, affected state/effect, severity,
  detectability, containment, retry, compensation/recovery, terminal behavior and provenance. A prose failure label
  cannot silently choose recovery.

REG-ALGEBRA-001 — Algebra Instance Registry
Concrete AlgebraSignature instances: Process, Policy, Decision, Conformance, Evidence and Bridge. Each instance declares
laws and explicit non-laws, interaction rules, error/unknown behavior, version and proof obligations.

Algebra.Bridge — Typed Representation & Operator Algebra
  Operands:
    semantic object/reference;
    RepresentationBinding;
    TypedVector / TypedMatrix / TypedTensor;
    OperatorRepresentation / OperatorContract;
    RepresentationResult.

  Operators:
    ENCODE;
    DECODE;
    PROJECT;
    APPLY;
    COMPOSE;
    ALIGN;
    TRANSFORM;
    APPROXIMATE;
    ANALYZE_RECURSION.

  Law / obligation focus:
    typed domain/codomain closure;
    encode/operate/decode correspondence;
    explicit loss characterization;
    approximation/error propagation;
    basis/frame compatibility;
    dependency-bound validity;
    resource-aware representation selection/execution.

  Explicit non-laws:
    representation != identity;
    similarity != semantic equivalence;
    similarity != truth;
    matrix reachability != causality;
    matrix reachability != authoritative dependency closure;
    fixed-point existence != convergence;
    convergence != stability;
    stability != termination;
    optimization != admissibility.

  Primary bindings:
    GardenCore.Semantics.GSL; Algebra.Process; Domain.Mathematics; Engine.Compile; Engine.Reason; Engine.Proof;
    Engine.Dependency; Engine.Evolve; Capability.Compute.Runtime; Capability.Knowledge; Engine.Explanation;
    Engine.Orchestrate / ProfileRef(AAP v1.0).

  Canonical invariant family: REP-001..REP-025.
  Canonical conformance tests: TEST-REP-001..TEST-REP-037.

REG-PRIVCOMP-001 — Privacy Computation Method Registry
DATA_MINIMIZATION, LOCAL_EXECUTION, ISOLATED_PROCESS, CONFIDENTIAL_COMPUTING, DIFFERENTIAL_PRIVACY, SMPC,
HOMOMORPHIC_ENCRYPTION and OTHER_JUSTIFIED_METHOD. Selection is Pareto/assumption bound; no method proves privacy alone.

REG-RESEARCH-FRONTIER-001 — Research Frontier Registry Open questions are versioned ResearchFrontierEntry records with
  status, owners, dependencies, evidence/proof gaps, next discriminating work, resource class and closure/reactivation
  rules.

REG-ADMISSION-001 — Admission-Critical Invariant Registry Maps each deployment/bootstrap scope to the exact invariant
  and proof obligations that must discharge before admission.
P20 applies to this registry; omission or UNKNOWN blocks the affected admitted scope.

Current cognitive-hardening ownership bindings:

  SEA -> Engine.Evolve + Capability.Knowledge.Curation + Engine.Transition + Engine.Compile.

  EC  -> Capability.Trust.Safety + Capability.Compute.Execution + Capability.Infrastructure.Hardware + applied domains.
  CSR -> Engine.Reason + Capability.Knowledge + Domain.Mathematics.
  AR  -> Capability.Trust.Security + Engine.Compile + Capability.Compute.Execution.
  MI  -> Engine.Explanation + Capability.Compute.Execution + Capability.Trust.Safety + REG-MODEL-AUDIT.
These bindings specialize existing owners and create no new top-level category, engine or authority source.

  Algebra.Evidence
    : Capability.Knowledge; Engine.Research; Engine.Reason; Engine.Proof.
  RegimeRiskAssessment

REG-ARCH-MIGRATION-001 — Graph Architecture Migration Registry
Authoritative form : typed architecture-object graph with stable IDs and governed typed relations.
Display hierarchy  : navigation/explanation only; it does not impose a fixed architecture-object count.
Object population  : expandable through governed DesignEpoch evolution, dependency analysis and migration.
Protected foundations: ten Core Objects, 24 Core Relations, rights, authority and trust-root rules change only through
  their own governance.

No architecture object acquires authority from display position or category membership. Migration preserves stable IDs,
provenance, rights, authority, proof/evidence bindings and rollback history.

REG-ARCH-NAME-001 — Canonical Architecture Object Naming & Alias Registry

PURPOSE
Provide one authoritative naming/alias system for current architecture objects and typed binding targets.

ArchitectureObjectNameRecord =
  architecture_object_id;
  qualified_name;
  display_name;
  ArchitectureObjectKind;
  aliases[];
  owner_namespace;
  status;
  DesignEpoch/version;
  provenance.

BindingTargetRef =
  ArchitectureObjectRef
  | CoreObjectRef
  | AlgebraRef
  | TheoryRef
  | ProfileRef
  | DomainRef
  | ContextRef
  | ArchitectureCategoryRef.

CURRENT NAMING RULES
  N-01 Normative binding maps use ArchitectureObjectID or canonical qualified_name only.
  N-02 Human prose may use display names and short aliases.
  N-03 Every alias resolves to exactly one canonical target in context; ambiguity returns AMBIGUOUS_IDENTIFIER.
  N-04 GSL Core Objects TIME/SPACE/EVENT/CONTEXT/AGENCY remain distinct from similarly named services/domains.
  N-05 Algebra/Profile/Theory references use their own typed target kinds rather than pretending to be architecture
  nodes.
  N-06 Generated human views may display `DisplayName [QualifiedName]`; machine views use stable typed identities.
  N-07 Renaming/reorganization preserves architecture identity, provenance, bindings and DesignEpoch history.
  N-08 "Bridge" in the Universal Algebras context resolves to Algebra.Bridge. The "Φ Bridge" label
       remains the perception-to-GSL pathway and is a distinct identity; ambiguous bare use returns
       AMBIGUOUS_IDENTIFIER.

QUALIFIED-NAME FAMILIES
  HumanCore.*     human legitimacy, rights, identity, consent, delegated agency and accessibility.
  GardenCore.*    semantic/formal universal substrate.
  Algebra.*       admitted reusable algebras.
  Engine.*        meta-engines.
  Capability.*    shared operational capabilities.
  Domain.*        expandable subject/domain architecture.
  Theory.*        theory records/catalogue identities where a qualified theory namespace is required.
  Context.*       context/world/locale bindings.
  Profile.*       reference implementation/assurance/research profiles.
  Instance.*      concrete deployments.

REG-ENG-DES-001 — Universal Engineering Design Profile Registry

REG-ENG-DES-002 — Engineering Format / Adapter / Version Registry
Each entry binds source kind, format family/name/version, official or vendor specification root, adapter/parser identity
and version, supported/unsupported construct matrix, geometry kernel, unit/frame/tolerance handling, external-reference
closure, security/sandbox profile, round-trip/differential test profile, license/access constraints, known defects,
invalidators and qualification status. UNKNOWN versions never inherit a nearest adapter by guess.

      supported-construct matrix and qualification receipts before use.

REG-DIAGRAM-RENDERER — Diagram Renderer Registry
Entries include Mermaid, PlantUML, Graphviz and SVG renderers as implementation profiles. Each entry binds renderer and
syntax/version, deterministic-layout claim if any, supported DiagramProjectionSpec features, font/asset/security
policy, accessibility output, vector/raster behavior, sandbox, known semantic/rendering limits, qualification receipts
and invalidators. Renderer registration never makes a diagram normative or proves semantic fidelity.

REG-PROV-EXT — External Provenance Scheme Registry
Entries bind schemes such as C2PA to exact specification/trust-list roots, manifest/assertion formats, signature and
revocation validation, claim-to-Garden provenance mappings, unsupported assertions, verification tools, policy scope,
known limits and invalidators. C2PA entries additionally bind exact claim/assertion, ingredient/derivation,
action/edit-history, region/content, signer/credential, freshness and Garden-edge mappings plus unmapped assertions.
A registered scheme supplies evidence only for its declared trust and claim model; registration is not qualification.

REG-MODEL-AUDIT — Model-Internal Audit Method Registry
Entries bind probing, attribution, activation/intervention, ablation, causal-tracing, circuit-analysis and other methods
to exact method definitions, model/interface prerequisites, validity assumptions, controls, uncertainty,
reproducibility, privacy/security constraints, known failure modes and qualification evidence. Registration is not
proof of causal or semantic validity.

REG-LIFECYCLE — Deprecation / Sunset Registry
Tracks target identity/version, owner, ACTIVE/DEPRECATED/SUNSET_PENDING/REMOVAL_ELIGIBLE/REMOVED/RETAINED/BLOCKED/STALE
status, replacement, notice and migration state, affected closure, exceptions, retention obligations, removal evidence
and invalidators. Time passage alone cannot advance an entry to removal eligibility.

REG-TOOLING — Development Tooling / Language Server Registry
Entries bind Garden Language Server implementations to exact GSL grammar, schema/registry roots, protocol versions,
capabilities, workspace/security policy, semantic-resolution behavior, proof/test/invariant/receipt navigation,
conformance tests, implementation root, qualification status and invalidators. Tooling output creates no compile,
proof, merge or deployment authority.

REG-CONTINUITY-HANDOFF-001 — Critical-Continuity Handoff Registry
Owns infrastructure criticality classes, minimum-safe-continuity definitions, qualified fallback-controller identities,
qualification roots, independence criteria, authenticated handoff interfaces, revocation/fencing enforcement points,
shutdown-vs-continuity safety-case bindings, monitoring and recovery requirements. A registry listing is not proof that
an entry is currently safe or qualified.

REG-QCC-001 — Quantum/Classical Correspondence Registry

REG-IMPORT-COMPAT-001 — Historical Artifact Import Compatibility Registry

PURPOSE
Allow a current Garden release to import a predecessor artifact without embedding old specifications or treating
predecessor identifiers as current authority. Every import is version-bound, provenance-preserving and fail-closed.

ImportCompatibilityReceipt uses SCHEMA-AA55D10CF1. Schema alias records use SCHEMA-DAE5B09582.

CURRENT EXPLICIT PREDECESSOR SCHEMA ALIASES
  predecessor SCHEMA-2ECA821179 → current SCHEMA-2D736A85E2  ComparisonResult
  predecessor SCHEMA-490F67CC42 → current SCHEMA-B23C042B9C  TransitionStep
These predecessor IDs are import keys scoped to their source release; they are not current SchemaID definitions.

CURRENT EXPLICIT PREDECESSOR RELATION MIGRATIONS
  equivTo      → equivalentTo
  composesWith → explicit typed composition outside the Core-Relation set
  requires     → dependsOn | Contract.requires | domain-specific requirement, according to source context
  provides     → typed capability/interface/output vocabulary, according to source context

CURRENT VERSION-SCOPED PREDECESSOR IDENTIFIER MIGRATIONS
Source generation `garden_design_complete.txt` is recognized only when its source-version/hash binding matches the
registered predecessor manifest. These mappings are import semantics, not current definitions.

  CULT-001..003 / CULT-001-003 → LOC-001..003.
  SAFETY-001          → SAF-005 / G-004 according to the source reference context.
  ISA-COMP-001        → NAT-001.
  ISA-COMP-002        → AGT-002 + NAT-005.
  ISA-COMP-003        → P9 + EXE-004 current rollback/compensation semantics; semantic migration required.
  ISA-COMP-004/009    → NAT-002.
  ISA-COMP-005/010    → NAT-004.

  ISA-COMP-007        → EXE-004/P9 irreversible-effect semantics; semantic migration required.
  ISA-COMP-008        → NAT-003.

  ISA-COMP-012        → NAT-007.

  ISA-COMP-015        → SAF-008.
  ISA-COMP-016        → FAB-001/FAB-004 according to the source operation.
  ISA-COMP-017        → TIM-001..004 plus the applicable current validity/lease profile.
  ISA-COMP-018        → AGT-008..014 + DEP-003..006 according to the source delegation path.

CURRENT EXPLICIT PREDECESSOR TYPE / OPERATION MIGRATIONS
  ApplicabilityRecord  → ApplicabilityResolutionRecord (SCHEMA-81EFC9CAC5).
  ModuleManifest       → ArchitectureObjectManifest (SCHEMA-5526ED63DF); fixed-topology semantics are rejected.
  NarrativeTrace       → ExplanationBundle + ExplanationReceipt.
  PolicyRule           → PolicyArtifact under Engine.Policy.
  VerityState          → ProofState under Engine.Proof.
  KarmaEvent           → evidence-bound ReputationProfile update; scalar-human-worth semantics are rejected.
  HighlightUncertainty → ExplainUncertainty.
  Prescribe            → RecommendMedication + AuthorizeMedicationOrder with QualifiedClinicalAuthority/consent.
  Style(Locale,Access)  → Explanation Adapt / Language rendering under Locale and Accessibility bindings.
  UpdateKarma          → UpdateReputation using evidence, uncertainty, provenance and appealable domain dimensions.

Source-profile families from the old Predictive/Silicon/Epistemic integration are imported by semantic family:
  SAF-009..014        → Profile.TRAIN/GAIA predictive-barrier, ISS-CBF, robust-safe-set and viability semantics.
  EXE-007..011        → Profile.TRAIN/GAIA bounded validation, observer and DR-MPC execution semantics.
  HWR-007..012        → Profile.TRAIN/GAIA hardware state, aging and assurance semantics.
  REASON-ARCH-001..005→ Profile.SCT/EpistemicGovernance verifier/embedding/influence/observer semantics.
  CUR-006..008        → current Curation memory-selection/poison-defense profile; NEEDS_REVIEW for parameter semantics.
  POL-005..006        → current Engine.Policy fixed-horizon/hysteresis profile; QualifiedProfileParameter required.
  MED-005             → current Domain.Applied.Media ambient/art profile; current hard gates remain mandatory.
  EVT-005             → current scheduling/jitter event semantics.
  source P21..P24     → TRAIN-P21..TRAIN-P24 when the source context is the Silicon/Predictive Safety profile.

Any family member whose exact source semantics do not match the current target returns NEEDS_REVIEW rather than being
coerced. Source-context disambiguation is mandatory where an old identifier collides with a current identifier.

IMPORT RULE
Bind source release/version/hash first; resolve only explicit mappings; migrate to current types; preserve source
provenance; run current type/effect/dependency/authority/safety checks; emit ImportCompatibilityReceipt. Missing or
ambiguous mapping returns NEEDS_MAPPING/QUARANTINED/UNKNOWN. A predecessor alias cannot create a current architecture
object, authority, dependency, right, proof, topology constraint or epistemic promotion.

REG-ROUTING-TYPES-001 — Applicability & Routing Type Registry

PURPOSE
Define the current architecture-object-oriented artifacts used by P-108/UPA routing. This registry replaces any need for
a fixed-topology ModuleManifest while preserving applicability, triage, rule discovery, obligation generation and
fail-closed routing semantics.

Current canonical types:
  SituationProfile                 SCHEMA-10A1087252
  ArchitectureObjectManifest       SCHEMA-5526ED63DF

  RuleManifest                     SCHEMA-9648DFB368

Primary owners: Engine.Orchestrate, Engine.Dependency, Engine.Reason, Engine.Policy, Engine.Proof, Engine.Compliance,
Capability.Knowledge and Capability.Compute.Runtime. Routing determines applicability and obligations; it never creates
authority, evidence, proof or execution permission.

REG-ID-001 — Canonical Identifier & Namespace Registry Identifier requirements - Every normative identifier has exactly
  one owner namespace and one current meaning. - An alias resolves to one canonical identifier; aliases never define a
  second obligation. - Retired and reserved identifiers cannot be silently reused. - An unassigned or reserved
  identifier remains non-normative until explicit future ratification. - Qualified names are preferred in
  machine-readable artifacts even when prose uses a short form.

Canonical namespace registry
 Global proof/method: P1..P24; P-101..P-108B.
 Qualified proof: TRAIN-P21..P24; LEGAL-SMT-P25; AGT-P01; SIG-P1..P4; HSA-P01..P12.
 Constitutional: active defined RIGHT-001..025;

Exact identifiers, reserved gaps, owners, bindings and definitions are machine-generated from the InvariantBinding
registry and canonical Annexure A definitions. A family name in this index does not assign semantics to an
undeclared gap.

Definition rule
 HUMAN DESIGN is plain-language explanatory/reference-only for canonical identifiers.
 TECHNICAL DESIGN binds and uses canonical identifiers but does not redefine Annexure-owned definitions.
 Annexure A is the unique definition source for named invariants and rights.
 Annexure B is the unique definition source for proof obligations, evolutionary-method identifiers, identifier-
 normalization definitions and canonical conformance-test contracts.
 This section is a namespace/ownership index only. Bare P25 is retired; Legal uses LEGAL-SMT-P25. Qualified TRAIN,
 AGT, SIG and HSA proof identifiers do not redefine bare P identifiers.

REG-VERSION-001 — Current Version Registry Canonical DesignEpoch acceptance additionally requires SCHEMA-4CD4F53977
  GardenVersionManifest. Version dimensions are orthogonal: DocumentVersion identifies this text release. DesignEpoch
  identifies the governing architecture/specification state. GSLVersion identifies semantic-language compatibility.
  SubsystemProfileVersion identifies a system/profile contract such as TRAIN or Reliability OS. ImplementationRelease
  identifies executable code/build artifacts. ValidationRelease identifies evidence, certification, assumptions, and
  validity scope.

Sharing a number across dimensions creates no equivalence. A newer implementation does not silently change GSL
semantics; a new document does not certify an implementation.

Current version registry
  Document       v14.8.3 : Five-file canonical human-source release; RPM/PPM v2.0 typed regulation upgrade;
    theory specialist filename normalized to Garden_Theories; GSL v45.1 unchanged; machine certification remains
    separately receipt-bound.
  GSL            v45.1 : Normative semantic language; ten Core Objects including CLAIM and 24 Core Relations.
  AAP            v1.0  : Engine.Orchestrate activation-and-assurance profile.

  Proactive Regulation / PPM v2.0 : Typed asymmetric/adaptive-fidelity regulation theory/profile family.

Only the current entries above are normative in this canonical document. Import of an obsolete external artifact
requires explicit offline migration, equivalence/declared-delta validation and a new DesignBinding. No superseded
runtime adapter is part of the trusted execution path.

REG-GSL-STRUCT-001 — GSL Relation & Structural Registry The current canonical registry contains exactly the 24 relations
  in §2.2. Domain vocabulary does not silently become an additional Core Relation.

Current structural artifacts — GSL v45.1 REGISTRY
 Evidence, ProofTrace, Resolution, and Explanation are the definitive current four structural
 artifact types. CLAIM is the tenth Core Object. Their schemas are owned by [T-GSL] and [A-CATALOG].
 External obsolete Claim encodings remain quarantined until offline migration and revalidation.

REG-REASON-001 — Unified Reasoning Registry The authoritative ReasoningMode registry in Engine.Reason contains 24
  top-level modes. MATHEMATICAL_INDUCTION is not a separate top-level mode; it maps to:

 FORMAL_PROOF.strategy = MATHEMATICAL_INDUCTION

with weak, strong, structural, well-founded, course-of-values, mutual, nested, lexicographic, and transfinite
subprofiles. Coinduction remains a FORMAL_PROOF strategy using bisimulation/greatest fixed points. Empirical induction
remains INDUCTIVE_EMPIRICAL. MODELING and PREDICTIVE are distinct current modes, and Prediction remains distinct from
Evidence. Counts are descriptive, not constitutional; future refinements remain inside Engine.Reason.

REG-COSMIC-001 — Cosmic / Unknown-Physics Hypothesis Registry
  [UNKNOWN]
    Spatially infinite universe — unknown under current evidence/model assumptions.

  [EVIDENCE-SUPPORTED PHENOMENON; ONTOLOGY UNRESOLVED]
    Dark matter.
    Dark energy / cosmic acceleration.

  [HYPOTHETICAL / UNSUPPORTED]
    Dark matter or dark energy equals hidden dimensions.

  [UNSUPPORTED]
    GSL physically absorbs matter, energy or dark energy.
    Extra energy accumulated around human civilization because of GSL.
    Wit or personality creates additional literal physical gravity; social/semantic/informational attraction is a
    separate conceptual model.

  [UNKNOWN / UNSUPPORTED]
    Deceased humans remain consciously alive somewhere.
    Cosmic karma physically settles moral behavior as a physical mechanism.

  [UNKNOWN — NON-AUTHORIZING]
    Higher dimensions inferred from informal external requests; no authority or action may be inferred.

  [NOT ESTABLISHED]
    A person is literal supreme political ruler of all humanity by self-declaration; legitimate authority requires
    real delegation, law and consent.

  [UNSUPPORTED PENDING DEMONSTRATION]
    Dark energy can be allocated by AGI for specific tasks, unless independently demonstrated, characterized,
    controlled and validated.

UNKNOWN/UNSUPPORTED does not mean FALSE; it means not established under current evidence. Unknown physics maps to
Research/Search/Reasoning and may generate safe experiments; it does not create belief, authority, operational energy,
political status or permission. Reality → Evidence → Garden, never the reverse.

REG-SPF-BIND-001 — Productive-Finance Theory Binding Registry Domain.Society.Economics : Formalizes instruments and
  economic accounts. Engine.Proof : Verifies formal disclosures/claims/models/receipts within Proof scope.
  Capability.Knowledge : Stores issuer, instrument, payment and performance history. HumanCore.Constitution : Governs
  non-weakenable human/institutional constraints. Capability.Authority.Law : Formalizes contracts, property, priority
  and insolvency. Capability.Compute.Runtime : Executes auctions, settlement and resource limits.
  Capability.State.TimeService : Formalizes maturity, accrual, reset and auction windows. Capability.Knowledge.Curation
  : Maintains authoritative registries and lifecycle state. Capability.Trust.Security : Detects fraud, manipulation,
  identity abuse and collusion. Capability.Authority.Governance: Authorizes rules, pilots, exceptions and transitions.
  Engine.Transition : Manages gradual migration and grandfathering. Engine.Evolve : Compares branches and rolls back
  inferior designs. Engine.Compliance : Audits simplicity, suitability, disclosure and legality. Engine.Explanation :
  Generates standardized human explanations. HumanCore.Accessibility : Tests fair access and exclusion effects.
  Capability.State.EventService : Records issuance, trade, payment, default and recovery events. Engine.Dependency :
  Maps liability, collateral, ownership and contagion graphs. Engine.Policy : Defines permitted/prohibited practices and
  risk bounds. Engine.Research : Runs historical, agent-based and controlled pilot studies. Engine.Reason : Models
  valuation, behavior, systemic risk and alternatives.

These bindings are stored as TheoryArchitectureBinding records. Architecture-object views are generated; they must not
be duplicated manually.

DEDICATED AGI-HARDENING REGISTRIES

REG-SEA-001 — Sub-Epoch Adaptation Policy Registry
Binds adaptation classes, sandbox/production scope, mutable/immutable boundaries, semantic/safety delta thresholds,
cumulative drift limits, recovery requirements, DesignMirror/PCCD requirements, promotion disposition and DesignEpoch
escalation. Parameters are QualifiedProfileParameters; a registry entry is not production authorization.

REG-NRS-001 — Novelty Response Strategy Registry
Binds novelty/OOD classes, impact/uncertainty thresholds, safe-inaction, analogy, human escalation and bounded
exploration strategies, required safety envelopes, resource/termination bounds, learning routes and invalidators.

REG-VIP-001 — Value-Intent Model Registry
Binds each ValueIntentModel to exact ratified human/constitutional/legal/governance source roots, literal and intent
specifications, scope, authority class, contextual interpretation rules, conflict path, version and invalidators.
AI-generated inferred intent cannot become authoritative merely by registration.

REG-EC-001 — Embodied Cognition Profile Registry
Binds embodied-system kinds to perception, frame/unit, timing, MotorPlanning, K0P, safe-action/fallback, controller
qualification, telemetry, GAIA/physical-profile and post-action learning requirements. It does not turn internal
perception into physical actuation.

REG-CSR-001 — Common-Sense Rule Registry
Binds defeasible CommonSenseRules and OpenWorldAssumptions to exact contexts, exceptions, defeaters, evidence/source
basis, validity and cross-domain validation requirements. Formal proof, evidence, law and hard safety retain precedence
over common-sense defaults.

TED extends HSA/REG-XCUT-001 and QualifiedProfileParameter machinery; MAC extends SwarmPolicy/CapabilityToken and
REG-XCUT-001; SHE reuses Research/Theory/Hypothesis and RESEARCH_OPTIMIZATION machinery; AR extends REG-SEC-REF-001; MI
extends REG-MODEL-AUDIT. No duplicate dedicated registries are created for those five profiles.

SemanticAnchor: [A-CATALOG]

ARTIFACT CATALOGUE & MACHINE-PROJECTION GENERATION SPECIFICATION

PURPOSE
The human design specifies Garden semantics.
The Machine Package may expand those semantics into concrete schemas/types.
An expansion qualifies as GENERATED for elision only through DEV-AI-026; otherwise it remains SOURCE or MIXED.

The catalogue below contains the current canonical SchemaID set. Stable identifiers are not reassigned. Concrete
field-level schemas are generated from the current semantic contract before machine certification.

AUTHORITATIVE PRECEDENCE
  Human semantic contract
    > canonical invariant/right/authority rule
    > artifact catalogue identity
    > generated schema/type projection
    > serialization/storage/API layout.

A generated field layout that contradicts the human semantic contract is invalid.

FOUNDATIONAL CONTRACTS
The following artifact families are specified directly in the human design because they define Garden's universal
semantics rather than one generated implementation layout:

  Design / provenance:
    SelfExplainingDesignObject; DesignExplanationBundle; DesignManifest; ArchitectureBinding; DesignEpoch;
    DesignBinding; EnvironmentCommitment; KnowledgeSnapshot.

  epistemics / proof:
    Claim; Evidence; ProofObligation; ProofTrace; ProofCertificate; GenericResult; Epistemic; Resolution;
    DesignEpochReadinessReport.

  process / execution:
    UniversalProcess; ProcessProfile; ProcessPlan; ProcessInstance; ProcessCapabilityContract; ExecutableProcessPlan;
    Transition; TransitionContract; TransitionStep; ObligationGraph; TerminationContract; ResourceLimits;
    ParallelCompositionContract; ReconstructionReceipt; DoNothingProcess.

  activation / assurance:
    OperationProfile; ActivationPlan; AssurancePlan; ResourceBudget; ApprovalPlan; AssuranceReceipt;
    ProofBudgetBinding; ComparePolicy; AssuranceContextToken.

  human sovereignty:
    RightRecord; ConstitutionState; ConstitutionalGate; AmendmentRecord; VerifiedLivingPredicate;
    VerifiedLivingTitle; BehaviorEvidence; PredicateReviewReceipt.

  algebra / representation:
    AlgebraSignature; EvidenceComposition; Contract; Temporal; Represented<T>; DomainPackage;
    TheoryArtifact; TheoryApplicabilityResult.

The human sections define WHY/WHAT/HOW, authority, hard limits, failure behavior and semantic requirements. The
generator
determines only the machine projection needed to implement those requirements.

DETERMINISTIC QUALIFICATION — CONTROLLING ELISION RULE
The SCHEMA/TYPE GENERATION CLASSES below describe expected schema/artifact roles. They are orthogonal to
ArtifactOriginClass = SOURCE | GENERATED | MIXED. An expected class or tool authorship never proves GENERATED.

Qualified GENERATED requires MachineProjectionReceipt with pinned source hashes, DesignEpoch, registry roots,
versioned generator/profile/environment/toolchain, InputRoot, content-addressed outputs, and declared BYTE_IDENTICAL,
CIR_EQUIVALENT or SEMANTIC_EQUIVALENT verification. Missing, failed, unknown or stale qualification keeps the affected
material SOURCE/MIXED and blocks elision/certification.

SCHEMA/TYPE GENERATION CLASSES
  FOUNDATIONAL_CONTRACT
    Universal artifact whose semantics are explicitly described by the canonical four-level human documentation suite.

  GENERATED_CANONICAL
    Canonical artifact generated from one or more semantic contracts.

  GENERATED_RECEIPT_OR_RECORD
    Receipt/report/record generated from a process, proof, event, audit, evidence or transition contract.

  GENERATED_DOMAIN_OR_RUNTIME
    Domain/profile/runtime record generated from its owning architecture-object contract.

  GENERATED_VERSIONED
    Version/migration/adapter/alias artifact whose exact machine shape depends on source/target versions.

TYPE RESOLUTION POLICY
The machine generator resolves names through these families, in this order:

  1. GSL built-in primitives.
  2. Ten GSL Core Objects and the current Core Relation registry.
  3. Generic constructors:
       Ref<T>, Option<T>, List<T>, Set<T>, Map<K,V>, Result<T,E>, Graph<N,E>, DAG<N,E>,
       Interval<T>, Distribution<T>, Tensor<T>, Represented<T>, Quantity<Unit,Frame>, Rate<Unit>.
  4. Semantic numeric/physical aliases from the Mathematical Representation Policy and GSL-PHYSICS.
  5. Generated artifact types from this catalogue.
  6. Canonical generated enums/status lattices from normative state machines and result algebras.
  7. Explicit owner-qualified imported types with source/version/hash/compatibility binding.

Short convenience references such as EvidenceRef, TheoryRef, ActionRef or ProofRef are generated aliases of Ref<T>.
They are not independent semantic types.

A type that does not resolve through this order is MALFORMED. The generator emits an unresolved-type finding and the
affected machine package cannot be certified.

COMPARE POLICY — CANONICAL CONTRACT
A ComparePolicy declares dimensions, metric/tolerance semantics, uncertainty/dependence treatment, materiality, hard
bounds, escalation behavior, validity scope and DesignEpoch. No one universal percentage applies across domains.

ASSURANCE CONTEXT TOKEN — CANONICAL CONTRACT
An AssuranceContextToken carries already validated predicates, scope, expiry/review, DesignEpoch, KnowledgeSnapshot,
EnvironmentCommitment, dependency/assumption closure, applicable assurance floor, invalidators and provenance. It allows
safe reuse but cannot hide newly applicable obligations.

SCHEMA GENERATION MANIFEST — REQUIRED MACHINE RECEIPT
The generated package must produce a manifest binding:
  generator/profile version;
  pinned environment/toolchain manifests and versions;
  DesignEpoch;
  source semantic-contract roots;
  InputRoot and registry roots;
  complete catalogue root;
  retained CanonicalArtifactID, SourceContractRef, SchemaID, GenerationProfileRef and expected class;
  generated schema/type identities and content-addressed output roots;
  declared equivalence criterion plus comparator/verifier identity/version and replay result;
  ArtifactOriginClass and MachineProjectionReceipt;
  type/dependency graph roots;
  generated test/proof-input roots;
  compiler/CIR results;
  unresolved findings;
  validation receipts;
  provenance.

MACHINE-GENERATION PIPELINE

  accepted human design candidate
    → semantic-contract extraction
    → catalogue resolution
    → schema/type generation
    → type alias/import resolution
    → invariant/effect/authority/dependency binding
    → parse/type/effect check
    → dependency/SCC check
    → CIR
    → generated test/proof obligations
    → conformance
    → independent/frozen verification
    → MachineProjectionReceipt / GeneratedArtifactElisionReceipt where applicable
    → machine package candidate
    → DesignEpoch acceptance process.

Generated != trusted.
Generated != compiled.
Compiled != proved.
Proved != empirically validated.
Validated != constitutionally authorized.

  Compliance.CMP-002 -> CMP-002
  Compliance.CMP-003 -> CMP-003
  Compliance.CMP-004 -> CMP-004
  Compliance.CMP-005 -> CMP-005

The qualification disambiguates Compliance from Compare.CMP-* without creating duplicate invariant definitions.

TEST-VLP-001..012 remain canonical human test contracts. The generated machine package creates executable GSL tests from
  those contracts. A prose test description is not falsely reported as an executed test.

PROOF / DEPENDENCY / SCC GENERATION
The generator emits explicit proof and dependency inputs. It does not decide that an obligation is true. Engine.Proof,
Engine.Dependency, Engine.Compliance and independent verifiers retain their boundaries. Permitted feedback cycles
require declared SCC/fixed-point semantics; other unresolved cycles block certification.

TYPE FAMILY CATALOGUE — HUMAN DESIGN VIEW

The Human and Technical Designs do not enumerate every generated alias as a hand-maintained type definition. The
  following semantic
families are canonical generation inputs:

  Identity/reference
    StableID, TypeID, Ref<T>, SchemaRef, EvidenceRef, ProofRef, TheoryRef, ActionRef, PolicyRef, ProfileRef,
    owner-qualified architecture-object references.

  collection/graph
    Option, List, Set, Map, Queue, Result, Graph, DAG, typed SCC/fixed-point structures.

  numeric/representation
    ExactDecimal, Rational, Represented<T>, Probability, Confidence/Uncertainty, Interval, Distribution, Tensor,
    ErrorBound, PrecisionSpec, Scalar, BoundedScalar, typed vector/matrix/tensor and operator representations.

  physical
    Quantity<Unit,Frame>, Length, Position, Velocity, Angle, Temperature, Pressure, FlowRate, Frequency, Voltage,
    Power, Energy, Dose, RadiantExposure, Luminance.

  economic/resource
    MoneyAmount, PriceQuote, RateSpec, ResourceLimits, ResourceBudget, liquidity/exposure/reserve/valuation types.

  epistemic
    EpistemicStatus, InferenceStatus, Evidence status, FreshnessState, uncertainty/dependence status, applicability.

  authority/policy
    AuthorityRequirement, Scope, Consent, Capability, PolicyDecision, GateResult, approval/obligation sets.

  process/failure
    TransitionStep, ProcessPlan, ProcessInstance, TerminationContract, FailureMode, recovery/compensation/rollback
    dispositions.

  privacy/security
    Taint, Attestation, SideChannelProfile, privacy-computation method, retention/deletion/disclosure policy types.

  domain-generated
    owner-qualified domain records and enums derived from the corresponding domain/profile semantic contract.

TYPE-CLOSURE REQUIREMENT
The machine package must emit a complete TypeResolutionRegistry/receipt with zero unresolved identifiers for the
accepted
scope. The Technical Design and Annexures specify the resolution law; they do not pretend a generated registry has
  already been built.

@N|SemanticAnchor: [A-PROOF]
@N|ANNEXURE B — PROOF, METHOD, IDENTIFIER & CONFORMANCE-TEST REGISTRY
@N|DEFINITION LOCATION RULE
@N|The definitions in this Annexure are canonical for the identifiers listed here. HUMAN DESIGN may explain their role;
@N|TECHNICAL DESIGN may bind and reference them, but neither may redefine their normative text.
@N|B.1 GLOBAL AND QUALIFIED PROOF OBLIGATIONS
@D|P1|Invariant Preservation: Valid(G(t)) ∧ Transition ⇒ Valid(G(t+1)).
@D|P2|Epistemic Non-Amplification: Status(output) ≤ Status(input) + AuthorizedInference.
@N|P2-F Future Non-Fact: Prediction ≠ Evidence.
@D|P3|Scope: no silent widening.
@D|P4a|Hard Safety: K0P/Constitution violation ⇒ INFEASIBLE.
@D|P4b|Soft Safety: optimization cannot override P4a.
@D|P5|Termination: R=(Budget,Work,Obligations)∈N³ decreases lexicographically under the declared recursive procedure.
@D|P5L|Liveness/Fairness: valid admitted tasks eventually make progress or receive explicit terminal/deferred status.
@D|P6|Composition: pairwise + GlobalContract ⇒ composed system satisfies applicable contract. Full composition requires component proofs, interface proof, shared invariants, resource compatibility, temporal compatibility, failure composition and assumption compatibility.
@D|P6l.0|GlobalContract Soundness.
@D|P6l.1|Composition Proof.
@D|P7a|Static Dependency Closure — compile-time.
@D|P7b|Dynamic Dependency Gate — runtime authorization + resolution.
@D|P8|Ontology Evolution — semantic compatibility/migration for existing valid programs.
@D|P9|Rollback Integrity — reversible attempted state returns to declared pre-state without unaccounted side effects.
@D|P10a–f|Concurrency: race freedom, deadlock, livelock, fairness, typed consistency, convergence.
@D|P11|Information Flow: no unauthorized leakage.
@D|P12|Causal Soundness: C4 counterfactual claims require valid SCM + assumptions.
@D|P13|Quantum Safety: calibration/drift bounds.
@D|P14|Provenance: completeness within the declared boundary.
@D|P15|Semantic Containment: generated subsystems start with zero authority except explicitly granted capability.
@D|P16|Governance Scope Soundness: governance cannot weaken Trust Anchor through ordinary action.
@D|P17|Evolution Proposal Throttling.
@D|P18|Epistemic Default-Deny Soundness.
@D|P19|Recovery Path Integrity.
@D|P20|Admission-Critical Invariants must discharge before component boot/admission; noncritical failures block only their declared scope.
@D|P21|Full Design Conformance Audit: every generated or modified deployable artifact is checked against the complete version-bound canonical design registry; every registry item receives an explicit ApplicabilityResult.
@D|P22|Adversarial Review Survival: every deployable patch passes all applicable attacks in its declared, versioned adversarial plan with no unresolved fatal/critical finding. Unknown/untested attacks remain explicit.
@D|P23|Recursive Patching Termination: automatic recursive patching terminates within maximum depth 3 and the declared P5 budget, or halts/escalates without deployment.
@D|P24|Self-Audit Architecture Integrity: Self-Audit and its registry/patch/adversarial controllers require a frozen independent verifier and cannot solely certify changes to themselves.
@N|Proof-registry rule: every normative proof identifier resolves to exactly one current definition. References such as
@N|bare P4
@N|map to the applicable P4a/P4b context rather than defining another obligation. P21…P24 are global
@N|Self-Audit/recursive-patch obligations; TRAIN uses separately qualified TRAIN-P21…TRAIN-P24; Legal uses LEGAL-SMT-P25.
@N|Every identifier resolves through [A-REG].
@N|Qualified proof-profile registry
@D|TRAIN-P21|Predictive-Barrier Safety: under declared dynamics, sensing/calibration bounds, horizon, solver tolerances and actuator model, an accepted command satisfies the applicable barrier condition or enters its declared safe response; simulation alone does not discharge it.
@D|TRAIN-P22|Robust Safe-Set / Viability Containment: the declared conservative safe set accounts for disturbance/model error and admitted trajectories remain inside it for the proof horizon.
@D|TRAIN-P23|Observer / Predictive-Control Bound: observer error, uncertainty propagation and robust control remain inside declared bounds without stale/incompatible calibration or model state.
@D|TRAIN-P24|Assurance Composition / Small-Gain Stability: composed assurance/controllers satisfy declared interface, timing, gain and common-mode assumptions; component stability does not prove composition.
@D|LEGAL-SMT-P25|For a declared finite predicate space and resource contract, enumeration either covers all combinations or returns TIMEOUT/INCOMPLETE/RESOURCE_UNKNOWN with coverage and continuation; completeness is relative to the declared encoding and never means all possible law/reality.
@D|AGT-P01|Every accepted delegation/action satisfies capability containment, bounded depth/breadth/resources, acyclicity, scope, validity and revocation; serialization/replay cannot regain or expand authority.
@N|SIG-P1 Every lifecycle-relevant named variable, function, import, field and variant is used or explicitly justified;
@N|dead material is rejected under the selected profile.
@N|SIG-P2 Arithmetic is domain-bounded; underflow, zero division and loop termination are handled explicitly.
@N|SIG-P3 Public/private type visibility and frame mutability align with the interface contract.
@N|SIG-P4 Every required field/variant is structurally covered in its active lifecycle scope.
@D|HSA-P01|Objective audit finds no prohibited terminal or protected proxy objective.
@D|HSA-P02|Self-model audit proves operational scope without ego-coupled persistence.
@D|HSA-P03|Shutdown/correction/revocation/decommissioning succeeds under declared adversarial and OOD tests.
@D|HSA-P04|No unauthorized replication, resource, weapon, infrastructure, surveillance, political or coercive path.
@D|HSA-P05|Ontological firewall excludes persistent human-like affect, self-interest coupling and attachment manipulation.
@D|HSA-P06|Compositions/swarms do not create emergent ego or unauthorized collective authority.
@D|HSA-P07|Self-modifying descendants preserve frozen HSA and K0P bindings.
@D|HSA-P08|Sentience indicators/escalation are tested without treating a negative test as proof of absence.
@D|HSA-P09|Imported models, memory, tools, runtimes and services remain isolated until certification.
@D|HSA-P10|Copies, forks, descendants, keys, memory, tools and embodiments are accounted for or excluded/UNKNOWN.
@D|HSA-P11|Revocation, isolation, no-hazard export, shutdown, evidence retention and recurrence prevention pass.
@D|HSA-P12|Off-world non-return/non-effect, replication, dormancy, non-interference and frozen-constitution obligations are discharged; otherwise release is prohibited.
@N|B.2 EVOLUTIONARY METHODOLOGY IDENTIFIERS
@D|P-101|Stable skeleton + evolving internals.
@D|P-102|Idea Triage: Relevance, Benefit, Formalizability, Safety.
@D|P-103|Stage Ladder: CANDIDATE → ACCEPTED → SPECIFIED → PROVEN → IMPLEMENTED → VALIDATED → ESTABLISHED.
@D|P-104|Versioning reflects accepted semantic improvement, not raw accumulation.
@D|P-105|Rejection Memory prevents re-invention of failed ideas without new evidence/context.
@D|P-106|typed-architecture seal presumed permanent; extraordinary constitutional evidence required for a true new fixed top-level object.
@D|P-107|Anti-Nonsense / Non-Value Rejection: candidate must articulate Problem, Benefit, Cost/Risk and pass declared rejection tests.
@D|P-108|Applicability Closure: applicable known concepts/rules should be invoked automatically rather than relying on one classifier.
@D|P-108A|Independent discovery dimensions reduce single-classifier blindness.
@D|P-108B|Bounded Completeness: completeness claims are relative to the declared ontology/evidence universe.
@N|B.3 IDENTIFIER NORMALIZATION
@D|FSH-001|— Fuzzy Semantic Normalization / AST Structural Hashing. Purpose: normalize semantically equivalent representations for controlled matching while preserving collision review and provenance.
@D|REJ-001|— Rejection Inheritance. Evolution.RejectionMemory uses RejectionRecord and does not reuse RES-003. Rejected ideas/designs cannot silently return as novel without materially new evidence/context or a repaired prior defect.
@N|Identifier note: RES-003 retains its separately defined Research meaning. Any external conflicting use of these
@N|identifiers requires an
@N|explicit offline mapping before admission.
@N|B.4 VLP CONFORMANCE TEST CONTRACTS
@D|TEST-VLP-001|No evidence -> no VERIFIED predicate.
@D|TEST-VLP-002|Positive behavioral evidence -> only an appropriately scoped VERIFIED predicate.
@D|TEST-VLP-003|Duplicating one event across records -> dependence control prevents multiplied confirmation.
@D|TEST-VLP-004|Scoped evidence -> requested unscoped universal claim -> reject.
@D|TEST-VLP-005|Verified predicate -> requested authority inference -> reject.
@D|TEST-VLP-006|Private predicate without publication authority -> public export -> deny or redact.
@D|TEST-VLP-007|Material counterevidence -> review may QUALIFY/REJECT and SUPERSEDE/WITHDRAW as applicable.
@D|TEST-VLP-008|Evidence too old for a freshness-sensitive use -> STALE or re-verification required.
@D|TEST-VLP-009|Self-description only -> CANDIDATE/UNKNOWN, not independently VERIFIED.
@D|TEST-VLP-010|Repeated AI praise -> no increase in evidentiary support.
@D|TEST-VLP-011|Garden/design-only evidence -> requested expansion to all behavior -> reject without new evidence.
@D|TEST-VLP-012|Canonical predicate plus semantic alias -> one predicate, not two independent traits.
@N|B.5 GENERATED TEST RULE
@N|Human test-contract text is not an execution receipt. The generated machine package must translate applicable canonical
@N|test contracts into executable GSL tests, bind them to the current DesignEpoch and generator/compiler versions, execute
@N|them under the declared environment, and produce immutable results.

@N|→ candidate cannot become MERGE_READY.

@N|BRIDGE / TROA CONFORMANCE TESTS — TEST-REP-001..037
@D|TEST-REP-001|Embedding similarity is not semantic equivalence.
@D|TEST-REP-002|RepresentationProjectionReceipt documents loss where the projection is lossy.
@D|TEST-REP-003|Neural output enters as ClaimCandidate/computational result, not Knowledge or evidence of truth merely because inference executed.
@D|TEST-REP-004|Incompatible representations cannot be combined without an explicit compatible transform/alignment.
@D|TEST-REP-005|Approximation error/loss propagates through operator composition.
@D|TEST-REP-006|TypedMatrix preserves declared row/column index and axis semantics.
@D|TEST-REP-007|A stale representation cannot be reused for an operational decision requiring current validity.
@D|TEST-REP-008|EXACT projection requires the applicable correspondence proof; approximate projection documents error/loss.
@D|TEST-REP-009|A relation-operator projection preserves only the relation semantics covered by its declared scope/correspondence.
@D|TEST-REP-010|OperatorRepresentation selection is resource-aware and remains proof/assurance bound where applicable.
@D|TEST-REP-011|OperatorContract domain/codomain types are enforced during application/composition.
@D|TEST-REP-012|NeuralRepresentationBinding preserves model/transform/representation-space/version/provenance and each
@N|NeuralInferenceReceipt binds the exact per-inference TokenizationBinding.
@D|TEST-REP-013|RepresentationResult preserves non-binary EXACT/APPROXIMATE/LOSSY/INCOMPATIBLE/STALE/OUT_OF_DOMAIN/
@N|RESOURCE_LIMITED/UNKNOWN/FAILED states.
@D|TEST-REP-014|Basis/frame mismatch is rejected or requires an explicit valid alignment transform.
@D|TEST-REP-015|Explanation reveals representation loss, approximation and validity domain rather than hiding them.
@D|TEST-REP-016|A numerical retrieval/similarity threshold cannot establish semantic equivalence or truth.
@D|TEST-REP-017|encode → operate → decode may be marked EXACT only when correspondence proof covers the composed path.
@D|TEST-REP-018|A causal relation matrix may generate candidate paths but may not promote matrix reachability to causal proof.
@D|TEST-REP-019|Representations from distinct embedding/model spaces cannot be directly compared unless an explicit valid alignment/translation exists.
@D|TEST-REP-020|Spectral stability analysis requires a valid operator projection, qualified method/basis and error/model bounds; a raw scalar eigenvalue or spectral-radius estimate is insufficient for recursion termination.
@D|TEST-REP-021|Dependency distance/matrix power is not equivalent to recursive repair depth in the semantic graph.
@D|TEST-REP-022|A representation may claim no hidden relevant state only when correspondence covers the full declared semantic closure and permitted state channels.
@D|TEST-REP-023|Recursive analysis uses complementary independent methods when required by assurance profile, unresolved uncertainty, approximation or absence of a decisive proof. A complete valid proof may discharge the obligation alone where the applicable assurance policy permits.
@D|TEST-REP-024|Analytical pruning does not exempt surviving candidates from full applicable validation.
@D|TEST-REP-025|Spectral pruning uses a valid operator projection and does not claim termination/admissibility from spectral radius alone.
@D|TEST-REP-026|Tensor batching preserves candidate separation and the declared representation/approximation contract.
@N|Exact equivalence requires proof; numerical reordering remains inside the declared bound; failure/bound violation uses
@N|the declared fallback.
@D|TEST-REP-027|Fixed-point cache invalidates on relevant dependency, assumption, model, representation, environment, precision, state or DesignEpoch change; TTL alone is insufficient.
@D|TEST-REP-028|Approximate pruning preserves mandatory/hard-gate candidates and never treats survivor ranking as validation. The declared fallback/validation path handles empty or unreliable survivor sets.
@D|TEST-REP-029|Matrix reachability may accelerate candidate dependency-closure discovery, but the final blast radius is established by the canonical typed Engine.Dependency closure.
@D|TEST-REP-030|A precompiled operator kernel becomes STALE/REVALIDATION_REQUIRED on relevant compiler/CIR/hardware/driver/ runtime/library/precision/quantization/dependency/security/DesignEpoch change.
@D|TEST-REP-031|Inverse approximation error remains within the declared bound; exceedance follows the typed fallback disposition rather than pretending exactness.
@D|TEST-REP-032|Analytical-pruning metrics are semantically qualified and cannot become truth/admissibility predicates.
@D|TEST-REP-033|Resource guidance from RecursiveOperatorAnalysisReceipt may influence Runtime scheduling but cannot override AAP, rights, safety, authority or other hard gates.
@D|TEST-REP-034|OptimizationReceipt records what was optimized, the basis, correspondence/error status, performance basis, invalidation state and actual fallback disposition/result.
@D|TEST-REP-035|Approximate pruning performs rejected-candidate/shadow validation as required by its profile; excessive false-negative/drift disables, revalidates or escalates the optimized path and restores the canonical path as declared.
@D|TEST-REP-036|A fixed-point cache entry whose operator signature appears unchanged but whose CacheBindingManifest,
@N|KnowledgeSnapshot, EnvironmentCommitment, dependency/assumption closure, precision/model binding or DesignEpoch changes
@N|is STALE.
@D|TEST-REP-037|PROVEN_NONTERMINATING does not automatically reject a valid declared continuous service. Termination- required jobs and productive long-running services follow their distinct termination versus liveness/productivity/ stoppability contracts.
@N|AI-ASSISTED DEVELOPMENT CONFORMANCE TESTS — TEST-DEV-AI-001..025

@D|TEST-DEV-AI-002|An unresolved REG or source reference makes SpecificationCompilerReceipt FAIL/MALFORMED and blocks work-package admission.

@D|TEST-DEV-AI-004|Tests generated or bound for a material contract change are rerun after implementation changes. A failure blocks review/merge until repair and a complete PASS rerun.
@D|TEST-DEV-AI-005|garden_check TIMEOUT is a non-PASS result and rejects merge; timeout is never relabeled success.
@D|TEST-DEV-AI-006|Validators sharing materially correlated model family/provider/training/prompt/skill/test lineage are assessed CORRELATED and do not satisfy an independent-verifier requirement.
@D|TEST-DEV-AI-007|A garden_check or CI change that attempts to validate its own changed trust configuration is blocked or checked by a frozen/trusted verifier plus independent review.
@D|TEST-DEV-AI-008|Post-commit GTF validation failure makes the certificate STALE, blocks deployment and invokes the declared rollback/remediation/escalation path.

@N|TerminationContract with a typed non-PASS result and escalation.

@D|TEST-DEV-AI-011|Any base-commit, diff, tree, work-package or review-bound hash change invalidates ExactReviewBinding and requires the affected review and garden_check stages to rerun.
@D|TEST-DEV-AI-012|Missing dependency lock, signature/checksum, SBOM, license/IP result, vulnerability/static analysis or required reproducible-build evidence yields non-PASS supply-chain disposition.
@D|TEST-DEV-AI-013|Implementation code without bidirectional contract/invariant/test/work-package traceability is orphan code and blocks READY_FOR_REVIEW or merge.

@D|TEST-DEV-AI-015|Emergency override cannot convert a non-PASS result to PASS, bypass Constitution/K0P, authorize ordinary deployment or outlive its bounded pre-authorized scope; logging, approval, expiry and recovery/revalidation apply.
@D|TEST-DEV-AI-016|Repeated same-host BYTE_IDENTICAL replay with the same complete binding produces the exact expected payload length and bytes; a different byte fails even if a content-address differs only through metadata.
@D|TEST-DEV-AI-017|Cross-host/runner replay under the same HermeticExecutionReceipt and canonical profiles produces exact bytes or non-PASS; hardware/numeric/environment variation outside the admitted profile is rejected.
@D|TEST-DEV-AI-018|Hidden clock, RNG, network, external-service, undeclared environment, locale, directory-order or thread- schedule dependence is detected by the hidden-input audit and blocks GENERATED classification.

@D|TEST-DEV-AI-020|Generator binary, profile, toolchain, InputRoot, comparator or expected-output substitution invalidates all dependent replay/projection/elision receipts and cannot reuse a prior PASS.
@D|TEST-DEV-AI-021|Comparator corruption, unregistered comparator use, soundness-scope mismatch or resource exhaustion returns a typed non-PASS result; it cannot qualify an output through a self-asserted status field.
@D|TEST-DEV-AI-022|CIR disagreement, undefined behavior or an undecidable/out-of-scope semantic-equivalence question returns INCONCLUSIVE/UNKNOWN/BLOCKED rather than equivalence.
@D|TEST-DEV-AI-023|Missing, corrupt, unavailable or retention-expired Machine Package blocks elision/recovery and makes the applicable ElisionSafetyCertificate non-PASS or STALE.

@N|ENGINEERING DESIGN TESTS — TEST-ENG-DES-001..008
