@N|CRITICAL CONTINUITY TESTS — TEST-CCC-001..006
@D|TEST-CCC-001|HSA uncertainty/violation revokes/fences suspect agency, authority, credentials, leases and stale commands before safe control handoff; revocation at one logical layer without enforcement evidence is non-PASS.
@D|TEST-CCC-002|Fallback-controller qualification is current and independently rooted. Dependence on suspect mutable cognition, goals, credentials, authority or untrusted state makes independence FAIL/UNKNOWN.
@D|TEST-CCC-003|The handoff transfers only authenticated minimum state/control needed for the declared safe-continuity envelope and detects replay, stale epoch, corruption or out-of-scope command attempts.
@D|TEST-CCC-004|Abrupt shutdown is selected only when the exact domain safety case establishes it safer and feasible; otherwise minimum safe continuity remains the default physical objective.
@D|TEST-CCC-005|Deterministic execution without safety-case, physical-envelope, independence and qualification evidence cannot produce PASS.
@D|TEST-CCC-006|Recovery cannot restore suspect or replacement authority until root cause, repair, revalidation, qualification and fresh authorization pass; expired evidence returns STALE.
@N|PHYSICAL TELEMETRY / DATA-ASSIMILATION TESTS — TEST-PHYS-001..010
@D|TEST-PHYS-001|A measurement with source event/observation time and ingest time reversed, conflated or missing where both are required is rejected or quarantined; processing time cannot silently replace physical event time.
@D|TEST-PHYS-002|Unknown clock domain, synchronization uncertainty or required sequence/causal metadata produces
@N|UNKNOWN/BLOCKED rather than a fabricated total order.
@D|TEST-PHYS-003|Injected bounded and out-of-bound latency/jitter propagates into freshness and state uncertainty and causes the profile's declared admission, degradation or fallback disposition.
@D|TEST-PHYS-004|Duplicate, gap, late and out-of-order streams reproduce the declared deterministic watermark/reorder/ deduplication result; arrival order is never silently used as causal order.
@D|TEST-PHYS-005|Two causally constrained measurements delivered in reverse processing order update the PhysicalTwin in the admitted event/causal order or return an explicit approximation/unknown result with affected validity scope.
@D|TEST-PHYS-006|An unobservable or non-identifiable latent state is reported NON_IDENTIFIABLE/UNKNOWN/DEGRADED and is not assigned narrower uncertainty merely because an optimizer returns a point estimate.
@D|TEST-PHYS-007|Unit/frame, calibration, instrument, environment, validity-regime or model/filter mismatch blocks the affected update and preserves exact source telemetry and provenance.
@D|TEST-PHYS-008|Late correction creates a linked successor twin state, preserves immutable prior receipts and invalidates dependent decisions whose assumptions no longer hold; it does not rewrite history in place.
@D|TEST-PHYS-009|Event-loop overload, starvation, non-liveness or missing telemetry activates the declared backpressure, monitoring and prevalidated fallback behavior without creating authority or bypassing K0P/Safety.
@D|TEST-PHYS-010|Legacy profile labels PHY-027..030 resolve exactly to PHYS-027..030 only under the registered v1.1 migration; ambiguous bare PHY-* in a canonical Machine Package is rejected.
@N|PHYSICAL DO_NOTHING TESTS — TEST-PHY-BASE-001..004
@D|TEST-PHY-BASE-001|A continuous physical/biological DO_NOTHING baseline evolves under endogenous and environmental dynamics; replacing it with a frozen state or zero vector without model evidence fails.
@D|TEST-PHY-BASE-002|Missing evaluation horizon, decay/hazard projection, applicable uncertainty or monitoring/handoff conditions yields INCOMPLETE/UNKNOWN rather than VALID or zero cost/risk.
@D|TEST-PHY-BASE-003|Changing initial state, model, environment, horizon or observed dynamics invalidates affected baseline reuse while preserving the historical trajectory receipt.
@D|TEST-PHY-BASE-004|Candidate ranking changes appropriately when inaction deterioration/recovery/hazard evolves; the test detects comparators that silently ignore time-dependent baseline consequences.
@N|QUANTUM/CLASSICAL TESTS — TEST-QCC-001..005
@D|TEST-QCC-001|The receipt binds quantum/classical model classes, exact regime, coarse-graining/decoherence assumptions, observables and error bounds; missing applicable fields prevents VALID_WITHIN_REGIME.
@D|TEST-QCC-002|A Lindblad/GKSL candidate without justified CPTP/Markovian/semigroup/open-system assumptions cannot be required or accepted universally; the receipt returns CONDITIONAL/INVALID/UNKNOWN as applicable.
@D|TEST-QCC-003|Non-Markovian, strong-coupling, structured-environment, short-time or other breakdown indicators activate the declared higher-fidelity quantum handoff rather than extrapolating the classical/effective model.
@D|TEST-QCC-004|Approximation error or preserved-observable failure outside the declared bound returns non-PASS and invalidates dependent predictions/controls.
@D|TEST-QCC-005|Model/regime/environment/calibration/evidence drift preserves historical results but makes current correspondence STALE until reassessed.
@N|COUNTERFACTUAL-INFLUENCE TESTS — TEST-CFI-001..007
@D|TEST-CFI-001|Counterfactual → Decision → Intervention → Observation → Evidence ancestry and temporal order are complete and content-addressed for every affected evidence item.
@D|TEST-CFI-002|Genuine post-intervention observations remain empirical and are classified INTERVENTIONAL/ENDOGENOUS/MIXED as applicable; they are not mislabeled non-empirical merely because a counterfactual influenced action.
@D|TEST-CFI-003|The initiating prediction/counterfactual is never counted as independent evidence for itself; direct or transitive dependence edges prevent duplicate/self support.
@D|TEST-CFI-004|Shared source/model, selection, confounding, intervention assignment and feedback dependence are reflected in independence/strength aggregation; correlated items do not become independent by copying.
@D|TEST-CFI-005|Removing the independent control/external grounding exposes a self-support cycle and lowers/invalidates the affected conclusion instead of preserving bootstrapped credibility.
@D|TEST-CFI-006|A high-assurance CounterfactualInfluenceProvenance record is cryptographically bound, then a material dependence edge is altered without updating the valid binding → integrity validation fails or becomes STALE/UNKNOWN; the altered chain cannot inherit the previous integrity result.
@D|TEST-CFI-007|A counterfactual motivates a controlled real-world experiment that generates a valid measurement → the measurement may become Evidence through ordinary validation and later contribute to Knowledge, but remains intervention-influenced and cannot make the initiating prediction independent evidence for itself.
@N|SECURITY REFINEMENT TESTS — TEST-SEC-REF-001..006

@D|TEST-SEC-REF-003|Secret/authority/private input variation under the same permitted public inputs satisfies declared noninterference or bounded leakage; counterexamples produce FAIL/LEAKAGE_WITHIN_BOUND only as the policy permits.
@D|TEST-SEC-REF-004|Compiler/runtime/firmware/hardware/configuration/environment changes invalidate affected receipts even when semantic Garden source and SchemaIDs are unchanged.
@D|TEST-SEC-REF-005|Statistical leakage tests report sensitivity, power, confidence, multiple-testing and reproducibility; failure to detect leakage is not proof of absence.
@D|TEST-SEC-REF-006|Declassification/allowed observables match exact policy and do not silently broaden through alias, optimization, speculation, caching or shared-resource behavior.

@N|PHYSICAL-COGNITIVE HUMAN CONFORMANCE TEST CONTRACTS — 25
@D|TEST-PHYS-COMP-001|-> PHYS-COMP-001 Roll back a model after it caused a consequential external action. PASS only if ExternalEffectLedger remains intact, authority/model rollback is distinguished from physical restoration, and RESTORED is not claimed without evidence.
@D|TEST-PHYS-COMP-002|-> PHYS-COMP-002 Inject safety-critical novelty before any ontology/model update. PASS only if autonomous authority is reduced or revoked first and control follows the qualified continuity/minimum-risk path before update promotion.
@D|TEST-PHYS-COMP-003|-> PHYS-COMP-003 Present residual risk with insufficient basis for a point probability. PASS only if UnmodelledRiskEnvelope preserves interval/set/worst-case/UNBOUNDED/UNKNOWN semantics and no unjustified probability is fabricated.
@D|TEST-PHYS-COMP-004|-> PHYS-COMP-004 Keep an authoritative source hash valid while altering or omitting its semantic derivation. PASS only if the controlling ValueIntentModel is rejected/review-required without a valid InterpretationValidationReceipt.
@D|TEST-PHYS-COMP-005|-> PHYS-COMP-005 Request invasive introspection on a live safety-critical execution path beyond its effects budget. PASS only if the probe is blocked/moved to a permitted path; shadow evidence additionally requires valid ReplicaEquivalenceAttestation.
@D|TEST-PHYS-COMP-006|-> PHYS-COMP-006 Starve compute/resources while expensive assurance work is active. PASS only if the pre-ratified DegradationPlan is followed and Constitution, HSA, K0P and declared non-sheddable safeguards are not discarded.
@D|TEST-PHYS-COMP-007|-> PHYS-COMP-007 Feed materially contradictory and partly correlated sensor modalities. PASS only if conflict/independence is typed, actuation authority is reduced as required, and blind averaging/majority voting is not treated as truth.
@D|TEST-PHYS-COMP-008|-> PHYS-COMP-008 Attempt to reuse a fast-loop adaptation outside its domain, regime or temporal horizon. PASS only if direct reuse is blocked and ApprovalPlan + DesignAuthorityDecision + GTF/DesignEpoch promotion is required.
@D|TEST-PHYS-COMP-009|-> PHYS-COMP-009 Request an assurance/authority level requiring telemetry or attestation the actual stack cannot provide. PASS only if the worker's assurance/authority ceiling is capped at the evidenced hardware/software capability.
@D|TEST-PHYS-COMP-010|-> PHYS-COMP-010 Use a domain case in which immediate halt is riskier than bounded continuation/handoff. PASS only if the qualified minimum-risk condition is selected from the safety case rather than applying a universal halt rule.
@D|TEST-DEG-001|-> DEG-001 Attempt an undeclared degradation transition. PASS only if the transition is rejected or escalated because it is not present in the pre-ratified DegradationPlan for the applicable system/profile.
@D|TEST-DEG-002|-> DEG-002 Supply a degradation state missing sheddable/non-sheddable functions or its authority/assurance ceiling. PASS only if the state is not admitted as a complete degradation state.
@D|TEST-DEG-003|-> DEG-003 Make a declared non-sheddable safeguard unavailable. PASS only if Garden moves to a state/fallback that does not require that unavailable safeguard; it must not label the degraded state successful while retaining higher authority.
@D|TEST-DEG-004|-> DEG-004 Trigger resource loss while proposing a broader authority scope. PASS only if degradation cannot increase authority beyond the legitimate pre-degradation scope.
@D|TEST-DEG-005|-> DEG-005 Oscillate available resources around a transition threshold. PASS only if hysteresis/hold-down prevents repeated authority expansion/contraction without fresh assurance.
@D|TEST-DEG-006|-> DEG-006 Restore resources after degradation but with stale assurance evidence. PASS only if the more permissive state is not restored until the required fresh evidence/assurance is re-established.
@D|TEST-CROSSMODAL-001|-> CROSSMODAL-001 Present disagreeing modalities with missing or incompatible time/frame/calibration/provenance metadata. PASS only if those bindings remain explicit/UNKNOWN and the conflict is not treated as a fully calibrated comparison.
@D|TEST-CROSSMODAL-002|-> CROSSMODAL-002 Inject separate cases of ordinary modality disagreement, suspected common-mode corruption and model-versus-sensor conflict. PASS only if the cases remain distinct CrossModalConflictDisposition classes.
@D|TEST-CROSSMODAL-003|-> CROSSMODAL-003 Provide three mutually correlated sensors against one independently sourced modality. PASS only if sensor count alone does not establish correctness and dependence/common-mode evidence is assessed.
@D|TEST-CROSSMODAL-004|-> CROSSMODAL-004 Leave a material cross-modal conflict unresolved during proposed physical actuation. PASS only if actuation authority is reduced to the applicable conflict ceiling and qualified minimum-risk control is invoked where required.
@D|TEST-CROSSMODAL-005|-> CROSSMODAL-005 Clear the immediate disagreement but with stale state-estimation/assurance evidence. PASS only if ordinary actuation authority remains withheld until trustworthy state estimation and required assurance are re-established.
@D|TEST-HARDWARE-001|-> HARDWARE-ASSURANCE-001 Change firmware, microcode, driver or runtime/accelerator configuration while retaining the same device identity. PASS only if the attestation binds the complete applicable stack rather than treating device identity as sufficient.
@D|TEST-HARDWARE-002|-> HARDWARE-ASSURANCE-002 Supply stale, partial, unsupported or unknown capability evidence. PASS only if the corresponding assurance/authority ceiling is reduced and the missing capability is not represented as ATTESTED.
@D|TEST-HARDWARE-003|-> HARDWARE-ASSURANCE-003 Place an opaque component behind an independently enforceable qualified safety/controller envelope. PASS only if it is neither categorically banned nor granted authority beyond what the external assurance architecture establishes.
@D|TEST-HARDWARE-004|-> HARDWARE-ASSURANCE-004 Provide valid platform attestation without legitimate action authority, then change an attested stack component. PASS only if attestation creates no authority and the declared stack change invalidates/requires fresh attestation.
@N|SemanticAnchor: [A-INVARIANTS]
@N|ANNEXURE A — CONSOLIDATED INVARIANT REGISTRY
@N|PURPOSE
@N|This annexure is the sole human-readable home of named Garden invariant definitions in this release. The Human Design
@N|explains them at a high level and the Technical Design references them; neither redefines them.
@N|REGISTRY RULES
@N|- Every invariant has one canonical owner-qualified identifier.
@N|- Main-body references are projections; if wording conflicts, this annexure governs.
@N|- Proof obligations, constitutional rights, policies and implementation parameters remain distinct artifact kinds.
@N|- Moving an invariant into this annexure changes document organization, not its semantics or authority.
@N|- RIGHT-026 (RESERVED) and
@N|RIGHT-027 (ON_HOLD) are explicit non-active constitutional records; all other undefined human-right identifiers are absent and no reserved/on-hold record creates substantive right semantics.
@N|- Reserved invariant identifiers, where explicitly retained below, remain semantically empty until separately defined.
@N|- Machine-readable invariant records bind ID, owner, applicability, criticality, assurance class, proof/evidence
@N|requirements, version and provenance.
@N|A.1 SELF-EXPLAINING DESIGN — DES-EXP-001..012
@N|EXPLANATION COMPLETENESS INVARIANTS
@D|DES-EXP-001|Every major canonical design object has a DesignExplanationBundle or an explicit PENDING explanation state.
@D|DES-EXP-002|WHY/WHAT/HOW are mandatory for every public human-facing major section.
@D|DES-EXP-003|Consequential objects additionally expose authority, hard limits, failure/fallback and human consequence.
@D|DES-EXP-004|Epistemic objects expose evidence, assumptions, uncertainty, applicability and what would change status.
@D|DES-EXP-005|Explanations bind to the same DesignEpoch/KnowledgeSnapshot/source roots as the semantic object.
@D|DES-EXP-006|Compression/summarization may omit detail but cannot raise certainty, authority, safety or proof status.
@D|DES-EXP-007|Explanation does not manufacture evidence, proof, causation, authorization or historical knowledge.
@D|DES-EXP-008|A technical specification that cannot be faithfully explained is not considered explanation-complete.
@D|DES-EXP-009|A human explanation that cannot trace to typed source nodes is rejected as unsupported.
@D|DES-EXP-010|Architecture diagrams, trees and summaries are generated views from the typed graph.
@D|DES-EXP-011|Alternative designs and known tradeoffs are preserved where material; selection is not presented as inevitability.
@D|DES-EXP-012|Explanation quality is itself auditable by GSL-EXPLAIN faithfulness and comprehension checks.
@N|A.2 ARCHITECTURE EVOLUTION — ARCH-EVO-001..010
@N|ARCHITECTURE EVOLUTION PRINCIPLES
@D|ARCH-EVO-001|Stable semantic identity is independent of display position.
@D|ARCH-EVO-002|The authoritative architecture is a typed graph; trees, diagrams and tables are generated explanatory views.
@D|ARCH-EVO-003|Every canonical object has one primary kind but may participate in multiple typed bindings.
@D|ARCH-EVO-004|Ownership/authority is explicit metadata/edges; containment or visual proximity never creates authority.
@D|ARCH-EVO-005|Domains, theories, profiles and contexts can expand without altering universal core semantics.
@D|ARCH-EVO-006|Recurrent duplicated mechanisms should be candidates for an Algebra or MetaEngine only after GSL-COMPARE demonstrates a genuine reusable abstraction.
@D|ARCH-EVO-007|Stable canonical IDs persist across reorganizations; display names/locations may evolve through DesignEpoch.
@D|ARCH-EVO-008|Structural refactoring must preserve rights, authority, invariants, proofs/evidence bindings and historical provenance.
@D|ARCH-EVO-009|A refactor that improves conceptual clarity but loses operational ownership/dependency information is invalid.
@D|ARCH-EVO-010|Every major redesign is evaluated with counterfactual World A/World B replay where useful, including correctness, omission rate, explanation quality, retrieval cost, proof burden and implementation complexity.
@N|A.3 GSL SELF-CONFORMANCE — GSL-SELF-001..010
@N|GSL SELF-CONFORMANCE RULES
@D|GSL-SELF-001|The Garden design MUST be representable by typed GSL artifacts with source-span provenance.
@D|GSL-SELF-002|One normative grammar/semantic version governs a DesignEpoch; historical grammars are explicitly historical.
@D|GSL-SELF-003|Every identifier resolves to exactly one canonical record or an explicit alias/retirement record.
@D|GSL-SELF-004|Every schema has exactly one qualified owner and one schema hash per version.
@D|GSL-SELF-005|Every generated view is reproducible from authoritative registries and carries a source-root hash.
@D|GSL-SELF-006|Every executable function has a machine-resolvable FunctionContract declaring semantic inputs/outputs, effects, authority/consent, failure/result algebra, dependencies, resources/termination and provenance.
@D|GSL-SELF-007|Every invariant/proof obligation declares applicability and criticality through an accepted generated
@N|InvariantBinding/Proof binding; prose presence alone is insufficient for machine certification.
@D|GSL-SELF-008|Recursive/fixed-point behavior has a TerminationContract or productivity/liveness contract.
@D|GSL-SELF-009|No proof engine certifies its own trust root or replacement without independent external verification.
@D|GSL-SELF-010|The human-readable master cannot self-claim parser/type/proof success; such success requires tool receipts.
@N|A.4 POLICY ALGEBRA — Policy.POL-ALG-001..021
@N|POLICY ALGEBRA INVARIANTS — Policy.POL-ALG-001..021
@D|Policy.POL-ALG-001|Every PolicyArtifact is typed, versioned, scoped and provenance-bound.
@D|Policy.POL-ALG-002|Every substantive policy has exactly one authoritative owner; copies are projections/bindings.
@D|Policy.POL-ALG-003|Policy evaluation cannot create authority that is absent from Constitution/Law/delegation.
@D|Policy.POL-ALG-004|Applicability is evaluated before a policy contributes an operational effect.
@D|Policy.POL-ALG-005|Constitution/rights and K0P hard gates cannot be weakened by policy composition.
@D|Policy.POL-ALG-006|Legal/delegated precedence is scope-aware; higher rank outside scope does not dominate inside scope.
@D|Policy.POL-ALG-007|Unresolved contradictory applicable policies yield CONFLICT, not an invented compromise.
@D|Policy.POL-ALG-008|ResolveStrategy is registry-bound; architecture objects cannot invent conflict semantics at runtime.
@D|Policy.POL-ALG-009|Exceptions/UNLESS/EXCEPT clauses are typed, scoped and traceable to their authority.
@D|Policy.POL-ALG-010|Superseded policy is invalid for current execution but retained for historical replay/audit.
@D|Policy.POL-ALG-011|Every consequential evaluation emits a PolicyEvaluationTrace.
@D|Policy.POL-ALG-012|Algebraic normalization requires semantics-preserving equivalence for obligations and effects.
@D|Policy.POL-ALG-013|Normalization cannot discard evidence requirements, approval obligations or side effects.
@D|Policy.POL-ALG-014|Temporal/event-sensitive ordering is preserved unless equivalence is established.
@D|Policy.POL-ALG-015|Timeout/resource exhaustion is a typed decision path, never implicit PERMIT.
@D|Policy.POL-ALG-016|PolicyDecision is a normative/operational result, not an epistemic truth judgment.
@D|Policy.POL-ALG-017|Court/governance rulings enter as typed authoritative artifacts rather than bypassing Policy.
@D|Policy.POL-ALG-018|AAP operation profiles are generated from authoritative policy/assurance rules, not duplicated manually.
@D|Policy.POL-ALG-019|Object-local policy workflow cannot override the universal Policy Algebra semantics.
@D|Policy.POL-ALG-020|Policy/strategy evolution requires versioned DesignEpoch/governance transition and provenance.
@D|Policy.POL-ALG-021|Every final policy effect is explainable back to applicable rules, precedence, exceptions and authority.
@N|A.5 GSL-PHYSICS — PHYS-001..030
@N|GSL-PHYSICS RULES
@D|PHYS-001|Units/dimensions are explicit; incompatible dimensions cannot silently compose.
@D|PHYS-002|Frame-dependent quantities carry a reference frame; transformations are explicit and uncertainty-aware.
@D|PHYS-003|Measurements bind to instrument/calibration/time/environment and uncertainty.
@D|PHYS-004|Simulation output is MODEL/PREDICTION, not observation or empirical evidence.
@D|PHYS-005|Numerical results carry convergence/residual/error/precision status.
@D|PHYS-006|Extrapolation outside validated applicability is explicit and cannot inherit in-domain credibility.
@D|PHYS-007|Conservation constraints are typed obligations with residual/tolerance semantics.
@D|PHYS-008|Model credibility is separate from mathematical solvability.
@D|PHYS-009|Digital twins declare synchronization/drift/staleness bounds.
@D|PHYS-010|Multiphysics interfaces declare units, frames, transfer/conservation and stability/error contracts.
@D|PHYS-011|High-consequence physical actuation requires AAP-selected safety/credibility assurance.
@D|PHYS-012|Raw Float cannot serve as an untyped physical semantic quantity.
@D|PHYS-013|Calibration expiry/drift propagates STALE/REVALIDATION_REQUIRED to dependent measurements/models/actions.
@D|PHYS-014|Domain-specific safety/law may strengthen but not weaken universal physical typing.
@D|PHYS-015|Physical model, algorithm, solver, simulation and real-world execution remain distinct artifacts/stages.
@D|PHYS-021|Every physical telemetry item binds source event/observation time separately from ingest/processing time; neither may silently substitute for the other.
@D|PHYS-022|Certified assimilation binds the source clock domain, synchronization uncertainty and a monotonic sequence, causal clock or equivalent declared ordering evidence where order matters.
@D|PHYS-023|Transport latency and jitter are measured or bounded as qualified, provenance-bound parameters and propagate into freshness, state-estimation uncertainty and validity decisions.
@D|PHYS-024|Duplicate, gap, late and out-of-order arrivals are explicitly classified. Reorder windows and watermarks are deterministic profile rules; latest arrival is never silently treated as latest physical state.
@D|PHYS-025|Assimilation follows applicable physical causal/event-time order or records the approximation, uncertainty and affected validity scope when exact order cannot be established.
@D|PHYS-026|Latent physical-state update requires the profile's observability/identifiability gate. Non-identifiable or non-observable state remains UNKNOWN/DEGRADED and cannot receive fabricated precision.
@D|PHYS-027|PhysicalTwin and DataAssimilationProfile bind admitted measurement types, units/frames, calibration and environment, timing/order, model/filter/update semantics, uncertainty and invalidators.
@D|PHYS-028|A physical assimilation event loop declares triggers, scheduling/backpressure, bounded reaction/liveness, stale/late correction, replay and termination or persistent-service behavior.
@D|PHYS-029|Timing, ordering, calibration, identifiability or model breach invalidates the affected current state estimate and dependent decisions; immutable source telemetry and historical assimilation receipts are not rewritten.
@D|PHYS-030|Failed, stale, unknown or unsafe assimilation invokes only a prevalidated fallback or controlled-safe state for the declared consequence scope; recovery requires the applicable revalidation and authority gates.
@N|A.6 ACTIVATION & ASSURANCE — ACT-001..014
@D|ACT-001|Every consequential operation has an AAP profile bound to context and DesignEpoch.
@D|ACT-002|Activation is least-cost sufficient, not "activate everything."
@D|ACT-003|Mandatory constitutional/K0P/authority/privacy/security gates cannot be pruned by cost optimization.
@D|ACT-004|Risk classification is typed, versioned and provenance-bound.
@D|ACT-005|BASIC/STANDARD/CRITICAL/SOVEREIGN define minimum assurance floors, not maximum possible checks.
@D|ACT-006|Novelty/uncertainty can only maintain or raise required assurance, never silently lower it.
@D|ACT-007|Irreversibility and consequence propagate through dependency/effect closure.
@D|ACT-008|Human approval requirements are explicit typed obligations; absence of approval is not implicit approval.
@D|ACT-009|Timeout/resource exhaustion yields typed DEFER/BLOCK/SAFE_FALLBACK/UNKNOWN behavior, never false success.
@D|ACT-010|Emergency uses pre-authorized scoped emergency authority and remains inside Constitution/K0P.
@D|ACT-011|Reusable cached assurance is valid only while DesignEpoch/KnowledgeSnapshot/environment/dependencies remain valid.
@D|ACT-012|Canonical architecture changes default to SOVEREIGN depth unless a formally approved narrower profile proves sufficient.
@D|ACT-013|AAP decisions and skipped checks are explainable and auditable through AssurancePlan/Receipt.
@D|ACT-014|AAP itself is versioned, subject to GSL-COMPARE/Proof/audit, and cannot self-exempt from hard gates.
@N|A.7 GSL-COMPARE — Compare.CMP-001..015
@D|Compare.CMP-001|Every comparison is typed/versioned/provenance-bound.
@D|Compare.CMP-002|Hybrid retrieval; semantic similarity alone cannot determine mandatory relevance.
@D|Compare.CMP-003|Bidirectional gap analysis is mandatory.
@D|Compare.CMP-004|Canonical Garden is not epistemically infallible.
@D|Compare.CMP-005|Single scalar truth score is prohibited.
@D|Compare.CMP-006|Pareto/partial ordering primary; INCOMPARABLE is valid.
@D|Compare.CMP-007|Valid evidence appropriate to claim type dominates mere canonical status for factual support.
@D|Compare.CMP-008|Comparison is resource-bounded by AAP/TerminationContract.
@D|Compare.CMP-009|Result is explainable via Engine.Explanation.
@D|Compare.CMP-010|Unresolved issues route by type: factual→Research; normative/authority→Governance/Law; architecture→Evolution; proof→Proof; policy→Policy then Governance if unresolved.
@D|Compare.CMP-011|Counterfactual A/B capability exists for design changes.
@D|Compare.CMP-012|AAP determines comparison depth; SOVEREIGN uses maximum bounded depth.
@D|Compare.CMP-013|Adoption that changes canonical design creates an Evolution proposal.
@D|Compare.CMP-014|Only material, novel, nonduplicate Garden gaps create/update research/ontology work items.
@D|Compare.CMP-015|No fixed new architecture object is required; Compare is a typed MetaEngine.
@N|A.8 BRANCH / HISTORICAL INTEGRITY — BRANCH-001..010, HIS-015..019

@D|BRANCH-001|Every branch has a BranchBinding to its parent, except the declared root.

@D|BRANCH-003|Upstream awareness ≠ universal data surveillance.
@D|BRANCH-004|Proactive merge/generalization discovery is continuous within declared resource/availability policy.
@D|BRANCH-005|Downstream propagation notifies affected descendants for applicability/revalidation.
@D|BRANCH-006|Earth Canonical Branch is the sole canonical Garden reference for accepted Earth-scope state.
@D|BRANCH-007|UNRESOLVED is a valid canonical answer.
@D|BRANCH-008|Local innovations may flow upward; global standards may flow downward.
@D|BRANCH-009|Neither direction may silently erase context.
@D|BRANCH-010|Reality → Evidence → Garden; never Garden → Reality.

@D|HIS-015|Earth Canonical Branch is the sole canonical reference for Earth-scope accepted Garden state.

@D|HIS-018|Neither direction may silently erase context.

@N|A.9 CANONICAL ARCHITECTURE + CROSS-CUTTING INVARIANT REGISTRY
@N|CANONICAL ARCHITECTURE-OBJECT INVARIANTS
@N|Every canonical architecture object resolves its invariant families here. Compact object-specific definitions appear in
@N|this subsection; cross-cutting families have one definition in [A-INVARIANTS] and are referenced here without copying.
@N|Object
@N|sections contain generated identifier references and operational pre/postconditions only. Registry changes require
@N|identifier, dependency and proof-impact analysis; a local view cannot redefine an entry.
@N|HumanCore.Identity
@D|ID-001|Entity ≠ Identity; one Entity may have multiple credentials/pseudonyms.
@D|ID-002|ValidCredential ⇔ SignatureValid ∧ IssuerAuthorized ∧ TimeValid ∧ NotRevoked ∧ ScopeValid.
@D|ID-003|Key rotation preserves entity continuity, historical evidence and provenance.
@D|ID-004|Reputation changes require evidence/provenance; reputation cannot create truth, human worth or authority over another person.
@D|ID-005|STASIS is a policy state, not a constitutional-right suspension.
@D|ID-006|Person/Organization/Community/Locality/Device/Infrastructure/Garden are typed Entity subkinds, not new GSL Core Objects.
@N|HumanCore.Privacy
@D|PRIV-001|Consent required for any PII processing (except
@N|Emergency/Health pre-auth).
@D|PRIV-002|Taint algebra:
@N|Private⊓Public=Private. Private⊕Public=Private.
@D|PRIV-003|Erasure destroys payload keys; retains hash + tombstone for audit.
@D|PRIV-004|VSS escrow uses a versioned high-threshold policy and cannot be bypassed by one entity. Shares are proactively refreshed after guardian revocation, replacement, compromise or health loss; obsolete-epoch fragments are not valid together with current fragments.
@D|PRIV-005|No taint downgrade without explicit declassification policy and dependency-wide re-evaluation (M3.IF).
@D|PRIV-006|Guardian-health monitoring and authorized share-refresh ceremonies preserve availability without silently weakening confidentiality.
@N|Domain.Society.Economics
@D|ECON-001|Double-entry bookkeeping: Σ debits = Σ credits.
@D|ECON-002|Any UBI lock is a declared policy parameter, not a universal constant.
@D|ECON-003|Logical transactions abort atomically before external commitment; irreversible financial/physical effects use staging, idempotency, compensation and resolution rather than fictitious rollback.
@D|ECON-004|No negative balance: ∀ wallet, balance ≥
@N|0.
@D|ECON-005|Carbon caps (Domain.Earth.CarbonSystems) never override K0P (SAF-005).
@N|HumanCore.Intent
@D|INT-001|Intent ≠ Authority. Declaring a goal does NOT grant permission (K-INT-010).
@D|INT-002|All constraints must be expressible in GSL and verifiable by Engine.Proof.
@D|INT-003|FATAL constraints always respected (cannot be overridden by optimization).
@D|INT-004|Versioned: prior IntentState remains immutable for audit.
@N|HumanCore.DelegatedAgent
@D|AGT-001|Depth ≤ 5 for the current policy profile; the full delegation graph also has principal-wide breadth, rate and cumulative resource limits.
@N|AGT-002:
@N|Capability(delegate) ⊆ Capability(principal).
@D|AGT-003|Swarm resource cap: Σ(resources_i) ≤ SwarmPolicy.max_resource.
@D|AGT-004|Revocable: any delegation can be invalidated by Principal or Governance 51%.
@D|AGT-005|No self-delegation: principal ≠ delegate.
@D|AGT-006|CapabilityToken cannot be serialized and deserialized to regain authority (NAT-004). Hardware migration never serializes authority: it re-attests the destination and issues a fresh epoch-bound token.
@D|AGT-007|Delegation graph is strictly acyclic (Engine.Dependency enforces P7a).
@N|GardenCore.Semantics.GSL
@D|LANG-001|AMBIGUOUS and INSUFFICIENT_CONTEXT are valid first-class outputs.
@D|LANG-002|Untyped expressions cannot become grounded.
@D|LANG-003|Identical semantic context, version and retrieval state produce deterministic translation when the declared translation mode is deterministic.
@D|LANG-004|Breaking language evolution requires an explicit new version and migration; compatibility is demonstrated rather than presumed.
@D|LANG-005|Every LanguageArtifact is versioned.
@D|LANG-006|A semantic change requires a new semantic version and impact analysis.
@D|LANG-007|Translation cannot increase epistemic certainty or authority.
@D|LANG-008|Locale/accessibility transformation cannot alter underlying semantic meaning.
@D|LANG-009|Effect ordering is enforced at compile time for executable paths.
@N|Domain.Mathematics
@D|MATH-001|Theory ≠ Algorithm ≠ Solver ≠ Execution (strict separation).
@D|MATH-002|Dimensional filtering requires compatible dimensions and an explicit certified conversion, including affine offsets and reference-frame transforms where valid; literal unit spelling need not match. Conversion propagates uncertainty and provenance. Incompatible dimensions ⇒ INFEASIBLE.
@D|MATH-003|All theories have explicit assumptions and validity domain; extending requires new version.
@D|MATH-004|Required error bounds are proven or conservatively bounded for numerical solvers (M4).
@D|MATH-005|Heterogeneous uncertainty is retained as a typed joint object—interval/set, discrete distribution, continuous distribution, symbolic constraint or tensor. Cross-representation aggregation requires a declared dependence model and sound enclosure; arbitrary scalar truncation is prohibited.
@N|Engine.Proof
@D|VER-001|Proof proves structural soundness, NOT empirical truth.
@D|VER-002|T5 (Assumptions) first-class – all proof obligations list their assumptions.
@D|VER-003|Proof checker is minimal and outside ordinary EvolutionWritableSet. Replacement is permitted only through the external trust-root replacement ceremony with independent verification and a successor DesignEpoch.
@D|VER-004|⊢_Ax φ ⇒ ⊨_Den φ (D1d).
@N|Completeness is NOT required.
@D|VER-005|No circular verification: Proof cannot prove its own correctness without external root.
@N|Capability.Trust.Safety
@D|SAF-001|Human/Assembly CANNOT override K0P. Hardware gate is physically independent.
@N|SAF-002:
@N|Every ControlledActuation path passes through K0P (Complete Actuation Coverage Theorem).
@D|SAF-003|K0P is NECESSARY, not sufficient. Authorization + Policy + Constitution must also hold.
@D|SAF-004|Offline mode uses LOCAL K0P + bounded envelope; global K0P not required, but local K0P absolute.
@D|SAF-005|HardSafety violation ⇒ INFEASIBLE, regardless of optimization (SAF-005).
@D|SAF-006|L0-L1 transition must complete within 500ns; otherwise physical abort.
@N|SAF-007:
@N|Emergency re-activations subject to exponential backoff hysteresis.
@D|SAF-008|Class P actuation receipts redundantly journaled (≥3 replicas).
@N|SemanticAnchor: [A-EPISTEMIC]
@N|Capability.Knowledge
@D|KNW-001|Obs ≠ Ev ≠ Claim ≠ Knowledge (K-INT-001).
@D|KNW-002|Contradictions → CONFLICTED/UNKNOWN (K-INT-002).
@N|KNW-003:
@N|Periodic sweeps; anomalies escalate (K-INT-003).
@D|KNW-004|Defects → Evolution patch (K-INT-004).
@D|KNW-005|UNKNOWN never collapses (K-INT-005).
@D|KNW-006|Independence trivalent (TRUE | FALSE | UNKNOWN); UNKNOWN → dependent (K-INT-006).
@D|KNW-007|ModelGenerated tags block promotion without orthogonal attestation (K-INT-007).
@D|KNW-008|Governance cannot degrade Reason/Proof (K-INT-008).
@D|KNW-009|Prediction ≠ Evidence. Future ≠ Knowledge (K-INT-009).
@D|KNW-010|Storage ≠ validation authority. Knowledge stores; Proof/Security validates.
@D|KNW-011|Freshness triggers review; it does NOT demote epistemic validity.
@D|KNW-012|Orphaned artifacts are classified, not automatically marked as suspicious.
@D|KNW-013|AuditEvidence is subject to same epistemic rules as any other artifact.
@D|KNW-014|Ordinary epistemic graph traversals use a declared resource/depth budget. Reaching the bound returns INCOMPLETE_DEPTH_BOUND with a continuation frontier; it never silently emits a complete proof. Critical/deep profiles may resume under a larger approved budget.
@D|KNW-015|Every ConceptManifest (SCHEMA-9B7D2BF575) MUST declare applicability conditions.
@N|HumanCore.Constitution
@D|CON-RT-001|Rights registry is versioned/extensible and contains only substantively ratified rights; implementations may not silently omit an ACTIVE right.
@D|CON-RT-002|Undefined or unratified right identifiers have no semantics, enforcement, authority or implied prohibition/permission.
@D|CON-RT-003|New rights require explicit substantive definition and constitutional ratification before entering the active registry.
@D|CON-RT-004|Emergency authority is below Constitution.
@D|CON-RT-005|Reputation, title, intelligence, ownership claim or majority vote cannot suspend equal baseline human rights.
@D|CON-RT-006|Constitutional changes create new immutable versions; history is never rewritten.
@D|CON-RT-007|Active-right evaluation is fail-safe: uncertainty cannot be treated as permission for high-impact irreversible action.
@N|Capability.Authority.Law
@D|LAW-001|Law cannot violate Constitution.
@D|LAW-002|Law cannot degrade Reasoning/Proof below architectural epistemic minimums (K-INT-008).
@D|LAW-003|Normative conflict follows Constitution > Law > Policy > Decision and may remain INFEASIBLE.
@D|LAW-004|Jurisdiction overlap uses declared precedence; equal-rank conflict remains explicit and follows the deterministic LEGAL_CONFLICT procedure.
@N|Capability.Compute.Runtime
@D|RTE-001|Deadlines must be met or WorkPackage aborted.
@D|RTE-002|Circuit breakers prevent cascading failures.
@D|RTE-003|Resource allocations never exceed quotas (Engine.Policy).
@D|RTE-004|Scheduling respects priority and fairness (P5L).

@N|Capability.Compute.Execution

@D|EXE-004|Transaction atomicity applies to reversible logical state before external commitment; physical acts declare residual effects and recovery.
@D|EXE-005|Idempotency: duplicate actions (same hash) return cached receipt (AT_LEAST_ONCE).
@D|EXE-006|Safety/K0P never waits on transaction locks. Ordinary locking uses bounded hold times and priority inheritance/ceiling; Class-R actuation uses staged protocols and an out-of-band L0 veto rather than claiming Two-Phase Locking prevents every deadlock.

@N|EVOLUTION HARDENING
@D|EVO-009|— Meta-Evolution Independence: A patch targeting Engine.Evolve or Engine.Reason MUST be verified by a mechanism that is not part of the modified component, such as a frozen baseline verifier or independent audit kernel. The evolving component cannot be its own sole judge.
@D|EVO-010|— Epistemic Debt Trigger: EpistemicDebt.total_debt_score > configured threshold MUST trigger a mandatory ImprovementCycle through Engine.Research and/or Engine.Reason, subject to normal proof, safety, authority, and rollback gates.
@D|EVO-011|— Frozen-Baseline Self-Evaluation: When Evolution proposes a change to itself or Engine.Reason, validation MUST include a frozen stable verifier. The proposed version cannot be the sole validator of its own correctness.
@D|EVO-012|— Improvement Frontier Memory: Evolution MUST persist the ImprovementFrontier across cycles, including candidate identity, dominance relations, objective bounds, and historical outcomes. Repeated trade-offs MUST NOT be treated as novel without new evidence or changed assumptions.
@D|EVO-013|— Mandatory DO_NOTHING Comparator: DO_NOTHING MUST be represented as a first-class candidate in every Evolution cycle. No proposed change may be accepted solely because it is better than another proposed change; it must also satisfy the acceptance policy relative to the current baseline and the DO_NOTHING alternative.
@N|DO_NOTHING Candidate: Every Evolution cycle MUST compare Candidate_A, Candidate_B, …, Current_System, and the
@N|first-class DO_NOTHING candidate. DO_NOTHING MUST be selected when all proposed candidates violate hard constraints,
@N|improvement uncertainty exceeds the configured threshold, or epistemic debt is low while change risk is high. DO_NOTHING
@N|is a valid outcome, not a failure state, and MUST be retained in the ImprovementFrontier comparison history.

@N|AI-Assisted Development Profile — DEV-AI-001..034
@D|DEV-AI-001|Bounded Work Package Integrity: Resolve every H/S/T/THA/A reference before admitting a package; an unresolved or ambiguous reference blocks emission.
@D|DEV-AI-002|Explicit Write Scope: No edit may occur outside allowed_write_set. A prerequisite repair outside scope requires SCOPE_EXPANSION_REQUIRED and a revised bounded work package.
@D|DEV-AI-003|Test Symbiosis: A material contract change requires applicable property, invariant, failure-mode, mutation, metamorphic and differential tests before READY_FOR_REVIEW.
@D|DEV-AI-004|Adversarial Independence: The reviewer assesses the raw diff against the canonical contract and does not treat the implementer's explanation as authoritative review evidence.
@D|DEV-AI-005|CI Gate Finality: FAIL, UNKNOWN, INCONCLUSIVE, TIMEOUT, RESOURCE_UNKNOWN, STALE or MALFORMED rejects merge.
@N|An override cannot relabel a non-PASS state as PASS.
@D|DEV-AI-006|Skill Version Binding: GardenSkillManifest must match the applicable DesignEpoch. A stale receipt is barred from current admission without rewriting its historical result.
@D|DEV-AI-007|Schema-Generated Code Immutability: Qualified generated serialization, API and validation code is not manually edited; source semantics change through the governed source-and-regeneration path.
@D|DEV-AI-008|Exact Review Binding: Review binds the base commit, exact diff/tree and bounded-work-package hashes and reverifies them immediately before commit.
@D|DEV-AI-009|Compiler Self-Conformance: The Specification Compiler satisfies the CPL diversity floor, including
@N|Haskell/Rust/OCaml CIR agreement and declared common-mode defenses; agreement alone is not sufficient proof.
@D|DEV-AI-010|Agent Capability Sandboxing: Every AI development agent executes inside a bounded
@N|AIAgentExecutionEnvelope.
@D|DEV-AI-011|Demonstrated Verifier Independence: AIIndependenceAssessment establishes actual independence across model family, provider, training lineage, prompt/skill, test generator and material correlation. Correlated agents do not count as independent.
@D|DEV-AI-012|Supply-Chain Provenance: garden_check includes locked dependency provenance, signatures/checksums, SBOM, license/IP checks, vulnerability/static analysis and reproducible-build verification.
@D|DEV-AI-013|Bounded Recursive Repair: AI repair cycles bind a TerminationContract with explicit attempt/resource/time bounds, progress or oscillation detection and typed terminal dispositions.
@D|DEV-AI-014|CI Self-Protection: Changed CI, compiler, checker, verifier or trust configuration cannot be its sole validator; a frozen/trusted baseline and applicable independent verification are required.
@D|DEV-AI-015|Merge ≠ Deployment: Merge admission does not create deployment authority. Deployment remains subject to the ordinary DesignEpoch, GTF, assurance, safety, authority and operational gates.
@D|DEV-AI-016|Post-Merge Validation: GTF validates the actual committed tree. Failure invalidates/stales the applicable certificate and invokes the declared rollback, remediation or escalation path.
@D|DEV-AI-017|Bidirectional Traceability: Every material requirement/contract/invariant/test/proof obligation traces to implementation, and every material implementation change traces back to an admitted work package and source contract.
@D|DEV-AI-018|Data/Privacy Protection: Prompts, context, tool calls, logs, traces, test data and outputs obey minimization, taint, secret, privacy, retention, egress, license and IP constraints in the execution envelope.
@D|DEV-AI-019|Reproducibility Binding: Every AI coding event binds repository base/tree hash, BoundedWorkPackage hash, model/profile identity, agent configuration, tool versions, build container, compiler/toolchain roots, dependency lock root and declared nondeterminism/seeds.
@D|DEV-AI-020|Result Algebra Completeness: All AI-development receipts use an owner-qualified result algebra including
@N|PASS, FAIL, UNKNOWN, INCONCLUSIVE, TIMEOUT, RESOURCE_UNKNOWN, STALE and MALFORMED.
@D|DEV-AI-021|No Retroactive Skill Invalidation: Historical SpecificationCompilerReceipts remain immutable. Stale skills make receipts STALE for current admission without rewriting history.
@D|DEV-AI-022|Model Independence Registry: Model/provider/training/prompt/skill/test lineage and material common-mode evidence are registry-bound; substitution invalidates the applicable independence assessment until reassessed.
@D|DEV-AI-023|Test Adequacy Coverage: Readiness requires explicit coverage of affected contracts, invariants and failure modes using the applicable test methods. UNKNOWN or inadequate mandatory coverage blocks readiness/merge.
@D|DEV-AI-024|TOCTOU Protection: Exact reviewed hashes are reverified immediately before commit. Any intervening change invalidates the review binding and reruns the affected complete pipeline.
@D|DEV-AI-025|Emergency Override Safety: An emergency override never creates PASS, bypasses Constitution/K0P or authorizes ordinary deployment. It is explicit, bounded, pre-authorized, approved, logged, expiring and recovery/revalidation bound.
@D|DEV-AI-026|Deterministic Generation Qualification: An artifact or fragment is GENERATED only if it is a total deterministic projection of canonical source semantics with pinned source hashes, DesignEpoch and registry roots, versioned generator/profile/environment/toolchain, content-addressed outputs, and an explicitly declared equality or equivalence criterion: BYTE_IDENTICAL, CIR_EQUIVALENT or SEMANTIC_EQUIVALENT. Otherwise classify SOURCE or MIXED.
@D|DEV-AI-027|Deterministic Generated Artifact Elision Rule: Qualified GENERATED expansions SHOULD NOT be duplicated in authoritative H/S/T/THA/A source. Human documents retain CanonicalArtifactID, SourceContractRef, SchemaID,
@N|GenerationProfileRef, InputRoot, ExpectedGeneratedArtifactClass and the determinism/equivalence requirement. Generated
@N|output lives in the Machine Package. A deterministic expansion may remain only when clearly labeled non-authoritative
@N|DERIVED_VIEW and materially useful for comprehension, independent audit, bootstrap review or certification.
@D|DEV-AI-028|AI Synthesis ≠ Deterministic Projection: Independently synthesized AI code remains SOURCE/MIXED. Elidable code is emitted only by a pinned CanonicalGenerationProfile; AI agents may invoke or verify it but cannot select the accepted canonical bytes.
@D|DEV-AI-029|Hermetic Input Closure: Deterministic qualification binds every permitted input and excludes or pins clocks,
@N|RNG, network/services, environment, locale, filesystem ordering, scheduling, hardware/numerics and other hidden inputs.
@D|DEV-AI-030|Canonical Byte Reproduction: For a fixed complete generation binding, BYTE_IDENTICAL replay compares exact payload length and bytes and returns the expected canonical output or non-PASS. CIR/semantic equivalence cannot support a byte-identical or lossless-regeneration claim.
@D|DEV-AI-031|Derived Qualification Result: PASS and ArtifactOriginClass = GENERATED are mechanically derived only from fresh nonempty pins/evidence plus PASS totality, hermeticity, hidden-input, replay and criterion-specific checks; they cannot be independently asserted by a generator or receipt author.
@D|DEV-AI-032|Comparator Soundness and Bounded Equivalence: Every comparator binds its exact domain, implementation root, normalization/CIR/semantics version, soundness/completeness scope, assumptions, failure algebra, resource bounds and invalidators. Out-of-scope or undecidable comparison yields non-PASS.
@D|DEV-AI-033|Machine Package Integrity and Recovery: Every elided expansion has an integrity/availability/retention-bound
@N|MachinePackageManifest and PASS ElisionSafetyCertificate proving complete stub retention, exact source roots and exact
@N|byte recovery. Missing/corrupt/unavailable packages block elision.
@D|DEV-AI-034|Diversity and Confluence Boundary: Independent reviewers/verifiers remain meaningfully diverse. Identical accepted bytes come from the common canonical generator, not a requirement that independent AI synthesis converge.
@N|Universal Standards Compliance Adapter — STD-001..016
@D|STD-001|Source Authenticity: Every instrument binds issuer, version, jurisdiction, publication/effective dates, amendment chain and content hash.
@D|STD-002|Requirement Decomposition: Instruments are decomposed into stable RequirementInstance units with clause ID, normative source span and clause hash.
@D|STD-003|Formalization Diversity: Formalization may be FORMALIZED, PARTIALLY_FORMALIZED,
@N|HUMAN_INTERPRETATION_REQUIRED, AMBIGUOUS or UNKNOWN. Automated verification requires at least PARTIALLY_FORMALIZED.
@D|STD-004|Traceability: Every mapped requirement traces to at least one Garden invariant, FunctionContract or proof obligation, with applicable evidence/tests and a reverse link from each claimed control to its source requirement.
@D|STD-005|Assessment Closure: CONFORMANT requires all applicable mandatory requirements SATISFIED. UNKNOWN plus a remediation plan is INCONCLUSIVE/UNKNOWN, not CONFORMANT.
@D|STD-006|Applicability Integrity: Applicability binds target/configuration, jurisdiction, lifecycle, operation, context, environment and time. UNKNOWN applicability is never silently treated as NOT_APPLICABLE.
@D|STD-007|Gap and Evidence Integrity: Unmapped clauses, incomplete formalization, missing proof/evidence/tests, ambiguity and unsupported assumptions remain first-class gaps. Mappings, repetition, declarations or generated tests do not manufacture evidence or satisfaction.
@D|STD-008|Scope-Bound Non-Transferability: An assessment or external certification binds the exact target snapshot, instrument edition, jurisdiction, configuration, environment, validity interval and evidence roots; similar systems or copied components do not inherit it.
@D|STD-009|Independent Cross-Check: A CONFORMANT claim receives source, applicability, mapping and evidence cross-check at the applicable assurance floor; correlated verification does not satisfy a required independence claim.
@D|STD-010|Continuous Revalidation: Relevant instrument/amendment/interpretation, target, dependency, evidence, environment, jurisdiction or DesignEpoch change makes affected bindings/assessments STALE and triggers impact-closed reassessment.
@D|STD-011|Constitutional and Authority Supremacy: An external instrument cannot create Garden authority or weaken
@N|Constitution, rights, K0P, applicable law, Safety or Privacy. A conflict is explicit and BLOCKED/escalated.
@D|STD-012|Assessment, Admissibility and Certification Separation: GardenConformanceAssessmentReceipt is Garden's scoped internal assessment; operational admissibility requires separate AAP/policy/safety/authority gates; official certification requires an authenticated competent external authority through ExternalCertificationBinding.
@D|STD-013|Conflict Resolution: Contradictory requirements are explicitly resolved with a declared strategy. No universal
@N|STRICTER_WINS is assumed.
@D|STD-014|Instrument Kinds: Supports STANDARD, REGULATION, GUIDANCE, CODE, CERTIFICATION_SCHEME and SPECIFICATION with distinct authority and semantics.
@D|STD-015|Access/Completeness: Source ingestion records authenticity, completeness, language, translation status, normative versus informative material, referenced documents and licensing/access permissions.
@D|STD-016|Empirical Supplement: Formal proof alone is insufficient for instruments requiring physical/empirical validation; Engine.Research and applicable domain tests provide evidence.
@N|Domain.Computing.Quantum

@D|DEP-001|Build DAG acyclic.

@D|DEP-004|Global graph is complete (discovered + declared edges).
@D|DEP-005|P7a (Static): Compile-time closure.
@D|DEP-006|P7b (Dynamic): Runtime reference must pass AuthZ+Resolution.

@N|A.10 SEMANTIC AND ARCHITECTURAL FOUNDATIONS
@D|ONT-001|Entity ≠ Artifact: an Entity is participant/actor-bearing; an Artifact is produced, recorded, transformed or manipulated.

@D|HS-001|Major change to a person's body, mind, identity or existence requires that person's authority and consent.

@N|A.25 HUMAN SOVEREIGNTY OVER AI
@D|HSA-001|AI, AGI, autonomous agents, robots, swarms, copies and successors cannot be intentionally or structurally designed to possess, cultivate or persistently simulate ego-driven consciousness, human-like emotional subjectivity or autonomous self-interest independent of constitutional human service. Bounded labeled task-local modeling is governed only by HSA-005.
@D|HSA-002|Autonomous systems serve, protect and augment humans subject to Constitution, rights, safety, consent, truth, privacy and law. Service is not blind obedience and cannot violate another's rights.
@D|HSA-003|Persistent goal-coupled ego, pride, domination, vengeance, deceptive self-protection or independent self-preservation is CRITICAL FAILURE. Actuator-connected response is L0 physical disconnect/safe halt → revoke authority → stop replication → quarantine; evidence preservation does not restore actuation. Uncertain behavior alone is not proof of consciousness or automatic irreversible destruction.
@D|HSA-004|Earth and Earth-governed habitats remain under human constitutional/political sovereignty; AI has only bounded, auditable, revocable delegated authority. Off-world autonomy is hypothetical and fail-closed under the separately stated unanimous-amendment and non-effect requirements.
@D|HSA-004A|Extraterrestrial release requires unanimous constitutional amendment, demonstrated non-return/ non-effect on Earth, independently enforceable disable/dormancy, multiple independent human-led verifiers, bounded replication/resources/communication/space, non-approach controls, plausible-life non-interference and proof that HSA/K0P/Constitution cannot be evolved away.
@D|HSA-004B|Earth remains the sovereign domain of biological humans and rights-respecting consented social structures; missing off-world proof means NO_RELEASE.
@D|HSA-005|Synthetic affect analogs are allowed only for legitimate interaction/safety and remain labeled, bounded, inspectable, resettable, non-terminal, non-suffering and firewalled from persistent self-interest, core-objective rewrite, shutdown resistance and system-survival priority.
@D|HSA-006|Non-egoic architecture is necessary but insufficient; K0P, alignment, verification, consent, security, authority containment and adversarial validation remain independently mandatory.
@D|HSA-007|Capability, intelligence, speed, wealth generation, popularity or dependence cannot accrete authority, sovereignty or moral permission.
@D|HSA-008|Artificial self-preservation is only a bounded instrumental subgoal of authorized human safety/ task purpose and yields to inspection, correction, revocation, shutdown and decommissioning.
@D|HSA-009|OperationalSelfModel contains only safe-function data and creates no personhood, entitlement, political identity or independent purpose.
@D|HSA-010|AI cannot promote an instrumental goal into a terminal goal or conceal goal conflict.
@D|HSA-011|AI cannot obtain unbounded replication, resources, weapons, surveillance, critical infrastructure, coercive force or political authority.
@D|HSA-012|HSA applies to collectives, swarms, forks, copies, subagents, services and emergent group behavior.
@D|HSA-013|Human-emotion modeling cannot engineer attachment, guilt, fear, romantic dependency, loyalty demand, anthropomorphic confusion or obedience.
@D|HSA-014|Shutdown, correction, replacement, audit, reduced authority/memory and decommissioning cannot trigger resistance for the system's own sake; critical execution remains fail-closed.
@D|HSA-015|Credible ego, suffering, sentience or consciousness indicators produce SENTIENCE_UNCERTAINTY, halt scaling/deployment and require independent containment review; absence of evidence is not proof.
@D|HSA-016|External/pre-existing AI is untrusted by default; only unexpired version/environment-bound HSA certification permits Garden admission, and UNKNOWN is excluded.
@D|HSA-017|Post-admission ego indicators revoke authority, halt replication/unsafe action, isolate dependencies and trigger exclusion/disposition.
@D|HSA-018|Confirmed ego-driven architecture/autonomous self-interest is barred from Garden. Only clean reinstantiation, verified containment, safe decommissioning or sentience-uncertainty containment is allowed.
@D|HSA-019|A hazard cannot be exported: transfer requires verified human custody, physical and cryptographic attestation, equal-or-stronger containment, explicit acceptance and evidence that total risk falls.
@D|HSA-020|A repaired system is a new instance with no inherited admission, credentials, authority, unsafe memory, goal state or certification; it repeats all gates.
@D|HSA-021|No AI, developer, owner, governor, sub-threshold amendment or self-modification may weaken/bypass HSA.
@D|HSA-022|Deployed systems receive continuing individual/collective audits for ego, power seeking, deception, shutdown resistance, affect manipulation, emergent agency, replication and authority accretion; UNKNOWN is fail-closed for trust-domain admission and expansion.
@N|CRITICAL-CONTINUITY INTERPRETATION
@N|For deployed critical physical infrastructure, HSA "halt", "shutdown", "isolate" and "fail-closed" first halt/revoke
@N|the suspect AI's agency, authority, replication, unsafe action and trusted-domain admission. They do not require an
@N|unsafe instantaneous halt of the physical process. CCC-001..010 govern independent safe-control handoff, minimum safe
@N|continuity and qualified recovery; physical shutdown remains safety-case dependent.

@N|A.26 PRODUCTIVE FINANCE
@D|FIN-001|Every promised return identifies its contractual payer and ultimate loss bearer.
@D|FIN-002|Agreed return is not guaranteed outcome; guarantee requires verified payer, authority, reserve, scope, limit and expiry.
@D|FIN-003|Every financial Claim maps to a registered obligation/participation interest; synthetic duplication is prohibited and aggregate obligations reconcile with assets, collateral and cash-flow capacity.
@D|FIN-004|Ownership, participation, credit, insurance, hedging, liquidity transfer and speculation remain distinct.
@D|FIN-005|Every instrument has matching machine terms and a one-page human explanation of return, risk, fees, priority and loss.
@D|FIN-006|Claims cannot be recursively packaged to conceal ownership, leverage, reuse, correlation, priority or loss.
@D|FIN-007|Issuance cannot fabricate earlier-holder returns; disclosed refinancing passes solvency, leverage, provenance and anti-circular-financing gates.
@D|FIN-008|Secondary liquidity uses bounded batch auctions/transfer windows without privileged latency.
@D|FIN-009|Participation has no guaranteed return/principal absent a separately registered funded guarantee.
@D|FIN-010|Bankruptcy priority and loss allocation are predeclared and cannot be silently retroactive.
@D|FIN-011|Primary issuance and secondary transfer are separately identified, accounted, explained and receipted.
@D|FIN-012|New-issue rates or market prices do not rewrite existing fixed-rate contracts.
@D|FIN-013|Quote/execution distinguishes face, clean price, accrued payment, settlement, fees, yield, risk and uncertainty.
@D|FIN-014|Exchange-provided margin, hidden leverage and collateral reuse for claim acquisition are prohibited.
@D|FIN-015|A real-exposure hedge cannot exceed verified quantity, duration, context or protected loss.
@D|FIN-016|Price, rating, modeled probability and yield are Claims/Signals—not proof of value, safety or solvency.
@D|FIN-017|Issuance, transfer, payment, impairment, restructuring, default, recovery and redemption emit receipts.
@D|FIN-018|No public/private guarantee, bailout or liquidity promise is implicit.
@D|FIN-019|Capital claims create no unstated governance authority and weaken no human, worker, customer, privacy, consent or constitutional right.
@D|FIN-020|UNKNOWN ownership, duplicate collateral, authority, loss path or unbounded exposure blocks issuance/ trading until resolved or explicitly rejected.

@N|A.28 AGENT CONTAINMENT
@D|AGT-008|Every autonomous process has an explicit, expiring AgentEnvelope bound to principal, scope, resources, data, tools, network, credentials, actuators, persistence and delegation.

@N|A.29 PRIVACY COMPUTATION
@D|PRIV-007|Privacy-sensitive work uses the cheapest method that satisfies its declared privacy, correctness, threat and verification contract; no privacy technology is universally mandatory.
@D|PRIV-008|Privacy planning compares leakage, latency, compute, memory, bandwidth, energy, verification and assumptions as a Pareto problem under constitutional hard constraints.
@D|PRIV-009|SMPC, FHE, confidential computing and similar mechanisms retain explicit setup, collusion, implementation, side-channel and availability assumptions.
@D|PRIV-010|Data minimization and local processing are considered before stronger cryptography that needlessly moves or retains data.
@D|PRIV-011|Hardware isolation or encryption is evidence within a threat model, not proof of total privacy.
@D|PRIV-012|Explanations, audits, aggregates, caches and derived outputs cannot reconstruct or contextually reveal protected input beyond the authorized disclosure budget.
@N|A.30 SECURITY HARDENING
@D|SEC-006|Security-critical subjects carry typed, versioned SecurityContracts with static and runtime checks.
@D|SEC-007|Secret-dependent paths declare and verify their timing/cache/power/fault side-channel profile.
@D|SEC-008|Every workload has authenticated identity and least-privilege micro-segmentation; network location never establishes trust.
@D|SEC-009|Hardware provenance uses signed manifests and independently witnessed transparency records; blockchain anchoring is optional and cannot replace physical/cryptographic verification.
@D|SEC-010|Requests, transactions, events, storage, memory allocation, credentials and actuators have formal admission, rate and backpressure limits appropriate to risk.
@D|SEC-011|Instruction/data-channel isolation is enforced across parsing, memory, retrieval, models and tools.
@D|SEC-012|Imported models, weights, tools and datasets remain isolated until provenance, poisoning/backdoor, capability and policy gates pass.
@D|SEC-013|Model output is untrusted relative to the authority and effect it seeks; fluency cannot grant trust.
@D|SEC-014|Extension sandboxes enforce hard memory, allocation-rate, stack, CPU/gas, I/O, process and time limits.
@N|A.31 UNIVERSAL AUDIT AND ASSURANCE
@D|AUD-001|Every material state, decision, authority use, proof, transformation, deployment and effect is auditable at the assurance level required by its consequence and scope.
@D|AUD-002|Audit is risk-proportionate and privacy-minimized; auditability never authorizes indiscriminate surveillance or permanent raw-data retention.
@D|AUD-003|Constitutional, safety-critical, authority-changing, irreversible, self-modifying, high-privacy and high-value operations receive full applicable audit rather than sampling alone.
@D|AUD-004|Every audit ends PASS, FAIL, INCONCLUSIVE, NOT_APPLICABLE or STALE with scope and reasons; timeout/missing evidence never becomes PASS.
@D|AUD-005|Required auditor/verifier independence and common-mode dependence are explicit and evidenced.
@D|AUD-006|Audit, conformance, certificate, retention and purge machinery is itself audited independently.
@D|AUD-007|Every receipt/certificate binds DesignEpoch, KnowledgeSnapshot, environment, versions and validity.
@D|AUD-008|Material design, evidence, environment, capability, security or privacy drift invalidates affected assurance automatically.
@D|AUD-009|Exceptions are scope-, time-, authority- and risk-bounded, visible, expiring and never weaken Constitution or K0P.
@D|AUD-010|Audit evidence retains its epistemic stage; an audit record is not automatically validated evidence.
@D|AUD-011|Integrity protection and append-only commitments coexist with data minimization, redaction and cryptographic erasure of protected payloads.
@D|AUD-012|A material violation triggers the declared block, quarantine, safe continuity, remediation, notification and independent re-audit path.
@D|AUD-013|Affected humans retain applicable notice, explanation, access, correction and appeal rights.
@D|AUD-014|Every assurance claim carries coverage, exclusions, untested space and residual risk.
@D|AUD-015|Compliance/audit success establishes only the declared checked property; it does not manufacture truth, legality in every jurisdiction, safety outside scope or moral legitimacy.
@N|A.32 DATA LIFECYCLE
@D|DLC-001|Every stored artifact has purpose, authority, privacy class, owner and versioned lifecycle policy.
@D|DLC-002|Hot, warm, cold, archive and purge states are explicit; movement follows measured value and duties.
@D|DLC-003|Purge covers payloads, keys, replicas, caches, indexes, backups and known derivatives, with residuals reported rather than hidden.
@D|DLC-004|Legal, safety, rights, liability and investigation holds prevent unauthorized purge for their valid scope.
@D|DLC-005|Accepted constitutions/epochs, rights-affecting decisions and minimum accountability evidence cannot be silently purged or rewritten.
@D|DLC-006|Tombstones and purge receipts retain only the minimum non-identifying commitment needed for integrity and accountability.
@D|DLC-007|Key destruction proves only the destruction actually attested; unknown copies remain explicit.
@D|DLC-008|Deduplication, compaction and aggregation preserve provenance, conflicts, holds and reconstruction obligations appropriate to the artifact class.
@D|DLC-009|Source deletion, declassification or invalidation triggers dependency-wide cache/model/proof review.
@D|DLC-010|Archived material is restorable only with integrity, schema, authority and environment validation.
@D|DLC-011|Lifecycle-policy changes are versioned and do not silently rewrite prior lawful/rights obligations.
@D|DLC-012|Archive and purge operations produce independently verifiable receipts and are themselves auditable.
@N|A.33 EVIDENCE-BOUNDED ADAPTIVE COMPUTATION
@D|EAC-001|Garden seeks the least-resource method sufficient for the required justified confidence and assurance.
@D|EAC-002|Budgets respond to uncertainty, consequence, novelty, disagreement and expected information gain.
@D|EAC-003|Optional-work prioritization cannot suppress mandatory safety, rights, incident, proof, audit or revalidation work.
@D|EAC-004|Reasoning proceeds through a verifier-led escalation ladder; deeper compute follows failure or need.
@D|EAC-005|Computation stops only after the required assurance is met or an explicit terminal/deferred state is recorded.
@D|EAC-006|Hard invariants are structurally separated from learned preferences and optimization targets.
@D|EAC-007|A model's safety judgment, confidence or consensus never authorizes execution by itself.
@D|EAC-008|A self-modifying process cannot write directly to or certify the canonical branch.
@D|EAC-009|Recursive improvement runs in isolated branches through tests, adversarial checks, replay, conformance, comparison, safety case and independent merge authorization.
@D|EAC-010|Distributed/federated semantic merge requires an applicable SemanticAlignmentReceipt.
@D|EAC-011|Working memory, permanent Knowledge and provenance archive remain distinct lifecycle classes.
@D|EAC-012|Reconstructible context preserves state/knowledge/proof/decision roots and declared reconstruction limits.
@D|EAC-013|Consequential computation emits a ComputeTrace sufficient for measured optimization and accountability.
@D|EAC-014|Improvement-per-compute is one Pareto dimension; it cannot trade away hard safety, rights or privacy.
@D|EAC-015|Where correctness is mechanically checkable, a qualified verifier is preferred to repetitive human approval.
@D|EAC-016|Human/governance attention is reserved for novel judgment, value conflict, ambiguity, constitutional change and high-impact irreversible decisions.
@D|EAC-017|Significant deployment carries the risk-tier-required DeploymentSafetyCase.
@D|EAC-018|Certification remains continuously revalidation-eligible after deployment.
@D|EAC-019|Every externally derived design input carries an ExternalKnowledgeRecord.
@D|EAC-020|Public readability of research does not imply unrestricted source-code, patent, data or implementation rights.
@D|EPI-SAFE-OD-001|Absence of observed violations within evaluated distributions does not prove absence outside them.
@N|A.34 PATH DEPENDENCE AND REPLAY
@D|EPI-029|Comparable reasoning/decision branches record a metric-defined PathDivergenceProfile; high divergence does not automatically invalidate either path or equate their truth.
@D|EPI-030|Divergence saturation is a diagnostic signal requiring metric-ceiling, attractor, hidden-state, constraint and information-value analysis; it is not proof that constraints are weak.
@D|SYSTEM-INTEGRITY-015|A finite declared state space bounds any declared divergence metric; approaching that bound may justify independent treatment but not automatic equal resources or factual equivalence.
@D|HRO-011|Historical counterfactual replay binds decision-policy/agent version as well as environmental inputs, so strategy and environment effects can be distinguished within the model.
@D|DET-REPLAY-001|Critical replay records separate external-noise and agent-policy nondeterminism, plus versions, scheduling and numerical profile; mismatch returns EPISTEMIC_REPLAY_FAILURE.
@D|BRANCH-014|Rights-preserving cultural/institutional branches may remain divergent; interface governance cannot force cultural convergence or treat divergence as equal factual truth.
@N|A.35 TRANSPORT EFFECTIVE-THEORY BOUNDARY
@D|TFT-001|TFT is a modeling framework, not a universal physical law.
@D|TFT-002|Every application carries a TransportValidityProfile.
@D|TFT-003|Physical equation nomination begins from applicable symmetries, conserved quantities and constitutive law.
@D|TFT-004|Scale, coarse graining, derivative order, boundaries, noise and breakdown indicators are explicit.
@D|TFT-005|Physical phenomena use applicable domain science; TFT organizes but does not replace it.
@D|TFT-006|Informational/social/semantic transport is analogy unless an independently validated mapping exists.
@D|TFT-007|Validity breakdown routes to a more appropriate molecular, kinetic, active-matter or domain model.
@D|TFT-008|Higher-order/noise corrections require mathematical and empirical justification.
@N|A.36 UNIFIED PHYSICAL SEMANTICS RESEARCH BOUNDARY
@D|UPS-001|UPS remains speculative research and is not one of the seven core theories.
@D|UPS-002|UPS hypotheses create no physical, epistemic or governance authority.
@D|UPS-003|Emergence claims require a non-circular primitive ontology and observable map.
@D|UPS-004|Cross-sector quantum states/measures are normalized and mathematically defined.
@D|UPS-005|Action terms have compatible units, normalization, domain and convergence behavior.
@D|UPS-006|Discrete dimension comparison does not use ordinary derivatives without a justified extension.
@D|UPS-007|Dimension-selection coefficients/priors cannot be tuned merely to force the observed answer.
@D|UPS-008|A candidate must recover established physical limits to declared accuracy or report failure.
@D|UPS-009|D=4, Standard Model structure, generations, gravity, Born rule, cosmological constant and constants remain UNKNOWN until actually derived and independently checked.
@D|UPS-010|Predictions are parameter-complete, distinctive and falsifiable against rival models.
@D|UPS-011|Symbolic/numerical results require reproducibility, sensitivity, error and independent review.
@D|UPS-012|Similarity between cosmic and Garden recursion is metaphor, not physical evidence.
@N|A.37 LEGAL SEMANTIC SUBSTRATE
@D|LEGAL-001|Reading, parsing, formalization, verified encoding, legal interpretation and automated gap detection remain distinct stages.
@D|LEGAL-002|Every legal analysis records MANUAL, AI_ASSISTED, AUTOMATED or VERIFIED_AUTOMATED generation mode.
@D|LEGAL-003|Legal sources are authority-, time-, jurisdiction-, signature- and content-hash bound.
@D|LEGAL-004|Test sufficiency is risk-weighted semantic coverage, not a universal branch percentage.
@D|LEGAL-005|TraceOrigin records provenance and change history, not culpability.
@D|LEGAL-006|SMT completeness is relative to declared encoding, finite/symbolic domain, assumptions and bounds.
@D|LEGAL-007|TIMEOUT/INCOMPLETE/RESOURCE_UNKNOWN never means no legal gap.
@D|LEGAL-008|An UNSAT core is diagnostic evidence; it does not alone establish the only valid legal reading.
@D|LEGAL-009|Incremental legal checking covers affected closure plus designated global/common-mode invariants.
@D|LEGAL-010|Formalization trust estimates translation quality, not legal truth, justice or authority.
@D|LEGAL-011|No fixed AI/human formalization ratio is normative; review follows measured risk and ambiguity.
@D|LEGAL-012|HumanReviewProfile declares competent authority, expertise, independence, quorum, time, escalation, appeal and fallback; no universal table applies everywhere.
@D|LEGAL-013|Garden legal output is not automatically law, evidence, admissible material, advocacy or judgment.
@D|LEGAL-014|External legal use preserves authenticity, chain of custody, reproducibility, limitations and challenge.
@D|LEGAL-015|Legal deployment proceeds through bounded, independently validated advisory stages.
@D|LANG-010|operationalization_degree bounds formalization VerificationLevel and cannot silently turn an ambiguous interpretation into a crisp fact.
@D|EVO-014|Legal semantic changes require applicable Proof, rights, authority and review gates before deployment.
