@N|A.39 ADDITIONAL DISTRIBUTED INVARIANT DEFINITIONS
@D|RIGHT-001|— Life & Bodily Integrity. No system may physically harm a human or knowingly allow prohibited physical harm; Emergency remains bounded by K0P/Constitution.
@D|RIGHT-002|— Privacy & Data Sovereignty. Personal data processing requires authority/consent; user has control, minimization, access and erasure rights subject to narrowly defined lawful/audit constraints.
@D|RIGHT-003|— Truth & Non-Deception. Model-generated/synthetic artifacts cannot be represented as validated evidence or fact without required independent attestation.
@D|RIGHT-004|— Due Process & Fair Governance. Penalty/restriction requires auditable evidence, applicable process and appeal.
@D|RIGHT-005|— Free Expression & Thought. Internal thought/hypothesis is not censored; consequential harmful actions/sharing remain governed by Constitution/Safety/Policy.
@D|RIGHT-006|— Property & Economic Participation. Legitimate property/economic participation is protected; authority must be proven.
@D|RIGHT-007|— Education & Knowledge Access. Verifiable public/non-private knowledge is broadly accessible subject to legitimate safety/security/legal constraints.
@D|RIGHT-008|— Identity & Reputation Protection. Reputation must derive from evidence, be auditable, domain-specific and appealable; it cannot be arbitrarily altered.
@D|RIGHT-009|— Revoke Consent / Data Erasure. Personal-data consent is revocable; erasure uses tombstone/key destruction and privacy-preserving audit where required.
@D|RIGHT-010|— Appeal & Judicial Review. Governance decisions affecting a person are appealable under the applicable judicial/governance process.
@D|RIGHT-011|— Safety from Physical Harm. K0P hardware/physical safety envelope cannot be disabled by ordinary software.
@D|RIGHT-012|— Non-Discrimination & Equal Treatment. Fundamental rights are independent of protected demographics and reputation. Reputation may govern earned roles/privileges only under due process and cannot reduce equal human worth or baseline rights.
@D|RIGHT-013|— Audit & System Transparency. Affected users may inspect decision/control/reasoning records, evidence and uncertainty subject to necessary Security/Privacy redaction.
@D|RIGHT-014|— Exist & Persist / No Arbitrary Deletion. Identity/history cannot be arbitrarily erased. Privacy erasure and historical audit are reconciled through minimal/tombstoned cryptographic records rather than exposed personal content.
@D|RIGHT-015|— Self-Determination. Humans define their Intent, projects, agents and life choices within the equal rights of others and constitutional constraints.
@D|RIGHT-016|— Emergency Protection / Safe State. Failure/UNKNOWN defaults to the applicable safe, low-authority state.
@D|RIGHT-017|— Personal Sovereignty. Each competent person has sovereignty over consequential changes to their own body, mind, sensory/pleasure systems, identity, private data, family/private life and mode of existence. A person's veto over self cannot be replaced by AGI optimization, government convenience, majority vote or speculative external authority.
@D|RIGHT-018|— Freedom from System Manipulation. No covert psychological, neurological, chemical, genetic, informational or emotional manipulation for experimentation, obedience, optimization or dependence. Truthful information is not manipulation merely because it is unpleasant.
@D|RIGHT-019|— Physical Rejuvenation / Identity-Preserving Health Restoration. Where scientifically supported and personally chosen, rejuvenation may restore youthful healthy physical function while preserving identity, memories, accumulated knowledge, judgment and beneficial acquired protections. It is a protected option, not a compulsory intervention.
@D|RIGHT-020|— Transparent Transformational / Dimensional Consent. Any radical embodiment, neural, virtual/uploaded, posthuman, identity, sensory or hypothetical dimensional transition requires understandable disclosure of mechanism, benefits, harms, alternatives, reversibility, failure modes, uncertainty and downstream effects, followed by explicit, informed, contemporaneous, freely given, revocable consent. Material changes invalidate stale consent.
@D|RIGHT-021|— Right to Remain Human. Ordinary embodied human life is the protected default. No system may force a person into virtual, uploaded, radically posthuman, nonhuman or speculative higher- dimensional existence merely because such a state is judged more efficient.
@D|RIGHT-022|— Family, Travel, Wonder / Ordinary Human Life. Humans may value family, friendship, real-world social contact, travel, beauty, touch, food, sensuality, exploration and ordinary human experience; optimization cannot dismiss these as inefficiencies.
@D|RIGHT-023|— Equal Human Rights. Personal sovereignty generalizes equally: each competent human controls self. No individual's private sovereignty becomes sovereignty over another competent human without legitimate delegated authority.
@D|RIGHT-026|— RESERVED. Status: RESERVED / NON-ACTIVE. The identifier is intentionally retained for possible future constitutional use. It currently defines no substantive human right, predicate, veto, authority, obligation, permission or enforcement effect. Activation requires a future constitutional ratification that supplies the substantive right definition, applicability, enforcement, provenance and version.
@D|RIGHT-027|— ON HOLD. Status: ON_HOLD / NON-ACTIVE. The identifier is intentionally retained but is not currently an active human right. It currently defines no substantive predicate, veto, authority, obligation, permission or enforcement effect. Any future activation requires explicit constitutional ratification of a substantive definition and cannot be inferred from the identifier or ON_HOLD status alone.
@D|RIGHT-024|— Human Flourishing. Garden shall improve conditions supporting strong, kind, free, healthy, capable, connected humans without creating an approved personality template or coercing obedience. Detailed HF invariants are in X.8.
@D|RIGHT-025|— Absolute Human-Centric Principle. Humans are Garden's core beneficiaries and rights-holders, never mere processing units, biological batteries, data nodes, energy sources, involuntary experimental substrate or stepping stones to nonhuman goals. Every competent human retains the right to remain human, understand consequential proposals, refuse them and veto changes to self. No AGI, institution, majority, biological/software process or hypothetical external process acquires authority over a person's fundamental rights merely through greater capability.
@N|EXP-006…EXP-016 are defined once in Annexure A and govern this civilizational-knowledge profile. This section
@N|specializes their artifacts/pipeline but does not redefine their identifiers.
@D|SPF-HYP-001|A greater fraction of net financing reaches identifiable productive activity rather than latency competition and synthetic trading.
@D|SPF-HYP-002|Hidden leverage, collateral duplication, and contagion depth decline.
@D|SPF-HYP-003|Long-horizon household returns become more explainable and less exposed to manipulation, without unacceptable loss of liquidity.
@D|SPF-HYP-004|Firms retain adequate access to debt and genuine loss-bearing capital, including start-ups and firms with intangible assets.
@D|SPF-HYP-005|Tail-loss frequency/severity and bailout dependence decline after controlling for macroeconomic conditions.
@D|SPF-HYP-006|Capital ownership and returns become less concentrated without reducing innovation, entry, productivity, or beneficial risk-taking.
@N|A.40 CURRENT 2026-08-21 CROSS-CUTTING UPGRADE INVARIANTS
@N|EVIDENCE COMPOSITION ALGEBRA — EVID-ALG-001..010
@D|EVID-ALG-001|Every EvidenceComposition declares evidence members, scope, provenance and a dependence model/assessment.
@D|EVID-ALG-002|Shared causes/common sources/methodological dependence remain explicit when materially relevant.
@D|EVID-ALG-003|Material contradiction produces an EvidenceConflict/CONFLICTED state rather than an arbitrary scalar winner.
@D|EVID-ALG-004|Correlated/dependent evidence cannot be counted as independent; composition follows the declared valid dependence model.
@D|EVID-ALG-005|Each confirmation retains its provenance and independence/dependence assessment; integer source count alone is insufficient.
@D|EVID-ALG-006|Source quality/validity enters composition only through declared evidence-supported criteria.
@D|EVID-ALG-007|Uncertainty/dependence is propagated rather than silently collapsed.
@D|EVID-ALG-008|Evidence composition is DesignEpoch/KnowledgeSnapshot/provenance bound where operationally reused.
@D|EVID-ALG-009|Composition cannot raise epistemic status beyond justified support or turn model output into Evidence.
@D|EVID-ALG-010|Engine.Explanation can reconstruct the composition, assumptions, dependence and unresolved conflicts.
@N|REGIME / NON-STATIONARY RISK — REGIME-001..010
@D|REGIME-001|Stationarity status is assessed explicitly when it materially affects prediction/risk.
@D|REGIME-002|Ergodicity is separately assessed where relevant; non-stationary and non-ergodic are not synonyms.
@D|REGIME-003|Structural breaks/regime changes remain explicit with uncertainty.
@D|REGIME-004|Distribution shift uses DETECTED/NOT_DETECTED/INCONCLUSIVE/UNKNOWN or equivalent non-binary semantics.
@D|REGIME-005|Tail behavior/model validity is declared where tail extrapolation matters.
@D|REGIME-006|Path dependence is modeled when past trajectory changes feasible/predictive outcomes.
@D|REGIME-007|Model-validity range and extrapolation distance are explicit for out-of-domain risk estimates.
@D|REGIME-008|Regime uncertainty propagates through downstream prediction/decision/Compare artifacts.
@D|REGIME-009|Material regime/tail/model uncertainty may raise AAP's minimum assurance floor; it cannot lower hard gates.
@D|REGIME-010|Regime-risk reasoning is evidence/provenance bound and explanation-reconstructible.
@N|PROOF RESOURCE ACCOUNTING — PROOF-RES-001..010
@D|PROOF-RES-001|Consequential proof work receives a declared resource budget/profile.
@D|PROOF-RES-002|Applicable budgets may include compute, memory, energy, thermal, network, time and hardware availability/wear.
@D|PROOF-RES-003|Resource exhaustion yields explicit relocate/defer/block/terminal status rather than silent proof weakening.
@D|PROOF-RES-004|Mandatory proof/assurance requirements cannot be waived merely because resources are scarce.
@D|PROOF-RES-005|Critical obligations may receive protected/reserved resources under AAP/resource policy.
@D|PROOF-RES-006|Thermal/resource limits may relocate/defer computation; unsafe operation remains blocked until required assurance exists.
@D|PROOF-RES-007|Resource readings/estimates bind to qualified Runtime/Hardware/Energy sources where used.
@D|PROOF-RES-008|Proof resource consumption and terminal/deferred state are auditable/provenance bound.
@D|PROOF-RES-009|AAP selects/approves proof resource profiles according to operation consequence and constraints.
@D|PROOF-RES-010|Resource accounting never converts empirical evidence into proof or proof into authorization.
@N|ADVANCED GEOMETRY — GEOM-001..015
@D|GEOM-001|Generic Manifold does not require metric/curvature; specialized manifold types declare added structure.
@D|GEOM-002|Charts/atlases declare domains, coordinate frames and transition-map validity where applicable.
@D|GEOM-003|Metric tensors declare type/signature/non-degeneracy/inversion semantics without raw untyped Float requirements.

@D|GEOM-007|Discrete approximations declare scheme, error estimate/bound, convergence order/status and assumptions where applicable.
@D|GEOM-008|Formal properties use Engine.Proof; empirical/numerical convergence/model credibility additionally use appropriate testing/evidence.
@D|GEOM-009|GSL-PHYSICS uses geometry types when the selected physical model requires them.
@D|GEOM-010|Mobility/Space/Energy/robotics bind geometry only where applicable; no universal geometry dependency is forced.
@D|GEOM-011|TFT may use typed geometry when its selected transport model requires it; analogy alone does not establish applicability.

@D|GEOM-014|Geometry artifacts are versioned and dependency-invalidated when their mathematical assumptions change.

@N|COMPOSITIONAL MATHEMATICS RESEARCH — COMP-MATH-001..010
@D|COMP-MATH-001|Category/higher-category/topos composition remains a research profile until separately promoted.
@D|COMP-MATH-002|Canonical objects use stable CanonicalObjectRef rather than treating numeric display labels as ontology.

@D|COMP-MATH-005|Promotion requires semantic/proof-equivalence for the declared scope or an explicit governed delta.

@D|COMP-MATH-009|Research artifacts remain theory/model candidates, not automatic architectural truth.

@N|SURVIVAL CONTINGENCY — SURV-CONT-001..010
@D|SURV-CONT-001|SurvivalContingencyContract is human/constitutionally authorized before communication/governance loss.
@D|SURV-CONT-002|Loss of contact does not create new constitutional authority.
@D|SURV-CONT-003|Autonomous constitutional amendment remains prohibited.

@D|SURV-CONT-005|Safe dormancy/reconciliation behavior is declared before activation.
@D|SURV-CONT-006|Termination/expiry/resource/replication bounds are declared.
@D|SURV-CONT-007|Execution remains bounded, auditable, provenance-bound and within existing HSA/K0P/Constitution.

@D|SURV-CONT-009|Missing authorization or missing required proof means BLOCKED/NO_RELEASE, not inferred permission.
@D|SURV-CONT-010|Survival goals cannot become autonomous terminal self-interest beyond the pre-authorized mission.
@N|COMPARE EVIDENCE/REGIME INTEGRATION — EVID-REGIME-001..010
@D|EVID-REGIME-001|Compare assesses material shared-source/methodological/causal dependence among evidence.
@D|EVID-REGIME-002|Multiple records alone do not establish multiple independent confirmations.
@D|EVID-REGIME-003|Compare assesses relevant stationarity/ergodicity/structural-break/distribution-shift status.
@D|EVID-REGIME-004|Tail-model uncertainty and model-validity/extrapolation limits remain visible in comparison dimensions.
@D|EVID-REGIME-005|Stable historical performance cannot alone justify low risk after material regime change.
@D|EVID-REGIME-006|Evidence-dependence/regime profiles retain uncertainty rather than forcing binary certainty.
@D|EVID-REGIME-007|Formal model consistency is distinct from empirical credibility/applicability.
@D|EVID-REGIME-008|Hard gates precede Pareto comparison even when an alternative is cheaper/faster.
@D|EVID-REGIME-009|Incomparable/unknown results remain valid terminal outcomes.
@D|EVID-REGIME-010|Engine.Explanation renders the evidence/regime basis and unresolved assumptions.
@N|BACKWARD REACHABILITY — BACK-REACH-001..005
@D|BACK-REACH-001|PreimageAnalysis does not assume a transition is invertible.
@D|BACK-REACH-002|Predecessor regions may be symbolic/infinite; enumeration is not required when inappropriate.
@D|BACK-REACH-003|Stochastic backward analysis declares probability/likelihood model assumptions and uncertainty.
@D|BACK-REACH-004|Formal preimage derivation does not establish real-world model truth.
@D|BACK-REACH-005|WHY_NOT/root-cause conclusions retain alternative predecessors, assumptions and uncertainty.
@N|MEASUREMENT ACTION — MEAS-ACT-001..005
@D|MEAS-ACT-001|Active acquisition is represented as MeasurementAction distinct from the resulting record/observation.
@D|MEAS-ACT-002|MeasurementAction records applicable resource, interaction, disturbance, calibration, time/environment and provenance.
@D|MEAS-ACT-003|State disturbance is explicit as NONE/BOUNDED/ESTIMATED/UNKNOWN or stronger domain typing.
@D|MEAS-ACT-004|Not every Observation requires active measurement; imported/historical/testimonial records retain their source class.
@D|MEAS-ACT-005|Quantum measurement semantics apply only in qualified quantum contexts and do not universalize POVM/QBist semantics.
@N|COMPARATOR EVOLUTION — COMP-EVOL-001..005
@D|COMP-EVOL-001|Engine.Compare cannot solely certify its own replacement.
@D|COMP-EVOL-002|Comparator replacement uses a declared evaluation protocol plus independent receipts/tests/challenge.
@D|COMP-EVOL-003|Previous Compare is a reference/evidence source, not sole authority.
@D|COMP-EVOL-004|DOMINATES/DOMINATED_BY/INCOMPARABLE/INCONCLUSIVE/UNKNOWN remain legitimate evaluation results.
@D|COMP-EVOL-005|Comparator evolution preserves regression/adversarial/historical/synthetic cases, disagreement and provenance.
@N|RUNTIME INTEGRITY — RUNTIME-INT-001..005
@D|RUNTIME-INT-001|A checked artifact remains integrity-bound from verification through consumption/execution.
@D|RUNTIME-INT-002|Integrity protection covers applicable data, executable instructions, policy decisions, authorization tokens, proof/result refs and control messages.
@D|RUNTIME-INT-003|Fault/degradation evidence propagates to AAP and applicable degrade/isolate/fallback/halt policy.
@D|RUNTIME-INT-004|Redundancy/TMR/ECC mechanisms are selected by risk/evidence/profile rather than universally required.
@D|RUNTIME-INT-005|Integrity receipts/status are provenance-bound; successful verification before corruption does not authorize corrupted execution.
@N|AGENT SESSION INTEGRITY — AGENT-SESS-001..005
@D|AGENT-SESS-001|AgentSession binds a frozen/authorized objective/authority root and context provenance.
@D|AGENT-SESS-002|Semantic drift uses a typed DriftAssessment with baseline, model/measure, estimate, uncertainty, scope and evidence.
@D|AGENT-SESS-003|Instruction-conflict/behavioral-deviation states include UNKNOWN/uncertainty rather than forced binary certainty.
@D|AGENT-SESS-004|Periodic or risk-triggered re-grounding may isolate/reset/reconstruct and require reauthorization.
@D|AGENT-SESS-005|No universal action-count or arbitrary entropy threshold creates authority or replaces evidence/risk-based policy.
@N|SOCIAL IMPACT — SOCIAL-IMP-001..005
@D|SOCIAL-IMP-001|Consequential social proposals evaluate aggregate and worst/concentrated distributional effects where applicable.
@D|SOCIAL-IMP-002|Fundamental rights remain hard floors regardless of aggregate gain or majority vote.
@D|SOCIAL-IMP-003|No universal bottom-percent threshold is used as a substitute for rights/context analysis.
@D|SOCIAL-IMP-004|Engine.Proof verifies formal rights/policy consistency only; empirical human impacts require evidence/domain/affected-human grounding.
@D|SOCIAL-IMP-005|Incomparable/uncertain outcomes, remedies and alternatives remain visible and explanation-reconstructible.
@N|KNOWLEDGE STEWARDSHIP — STEWARD-001..005
@D|STEWARD-001|Knowledge restriction requires a legitimate consent/confidentiality/privacy/legal/safety/contract/stewardship basis.
@D|STEWARD-002|Community identity/membership alone creates no access-restriction authority.
@D|STEWARD-003|Garden does not infer access prohibition from ethnicity, nationality, religion or presumed culture.
@D|STEWARD-004|Stewardship boundaries declare scope, authority, review/appeal/revalidation and provenance where applicable.
@D|STEWARD-005|Locale/culture contextualization cannot silently become censorship or ownership of public knowledge.
@N|TEMPORAL CONTEXT — TEMP-CONT-001..005
@D|TEMP-CONT-001|Consequential evaluation explicitly binds its effective evaluation time/interval.
@D|TEMP-CONT-002|Historical reconstruction uses law/rules/knowledge/context applicable to that historical scope.
@D|TEMP-CONT-003|Current knowledge may explain history but cannot be silently attributed to historical actors.
@D|TEMP-CONT-004|Historical applicability cannot silently authorize current operational use.
@D|TEMP-CONT-005|Temporal binding is provenance/version/snapshot aware and uncertainty remains explicit when reconstruction is incomplete.
@N|PHYSICAL CONSERVATION BREACH — PHYS-016..020
@D|PHYS-016|A conservation residual exceeding declared tolerance triggers the disposition required by the applicable safety/physical contract.
@D|PHYS-017|Residual/tolerance are typed measures/bounds with units/frames/uncertainty as applicable, not raw universal Float.
@D|PHYS-018|Dispositions include invalidate/block/degrade/fallback/controlled-safe-state/halt according to domain safety; universal abrupt halt is prohibited as a default assumption.
@D|PHYS-019|High-consequence actuation cannot rely on a breached/invalid result unless a separately valid safe fallback authorizes the alternative.
@D|PHYS-020|Conservation-breach decisions and fallback justification are auditable/provenance bound.
@N|REASONING PREEMPTION — REAS-PRE-001..005
@D|REAS-PRE-001|Higher-priority emergency workloads may preempt lower-priority reasoning under AAP scheduling.
@D|REAS-PRE-002|Protected emergency resource reserve is deployment/domain determined; no universal fixed percentage is required.
@D|REAS-PRE-003|Preemptible long work preserves/checkpoints state according to its contract where technically possible.
@D|REAS-PRE-004|Preemption changes scheduling only and never bypasses Constitution/rights/K0P/authority/mandatory assurance.
@D|REAS-PRE-005|Resume/restart/abandon disposition is explicit and provenance-bound.
@N|EXPLANATION REDACTION — EXP-017..021
@D|EXP-017|A material redaction produces a RedactionReceipt or protected equivalent when legally/safely possible.
@D|EXP-018|The receipt identifies the maximum lawful category/reason/authority-class/scope/review information without leaking protected content.
@D|EXP-019|Redaction itself does not erase the protected full audit basis from authorized audit state unless law/privacy requires deletion.
@D|EXP-020|Affected humans receive an appeal/review route when one legitimately exists; Garden does not invent one when none exists.
@D|EXP-021|Withholding authority identity/reason/category may itself be necessary; disclosure minimization is explicit and reviewable under the applicable authority.
@N|EPISTEMIC APPLICABILITY — KNW-019..023
@D|KNW-019|Current use checks temporal/scope applicability of historically retained knowledge.
@D|KNW-020|Garden distinguishes WORLD_CHANGED, DEFINITION_CHANGED, RULE_CHANGED, EVIDENCE_CONTRADICTED/REFUTED and
@N|SCOPE_CHANGED.
@D|KNW-021|Taxonomy/rule change is not silently represented as empirical falsification.
@D|KNW-022|Historical truth/applicability is preserved with effective period and provenance even when current applicability ends.
@D|KNW-023|UNKNOWN/incomplete historical reconstruction remains explicit and can trigger revalidation/research.
@N|TRANSITIVE CACHE INTEGRITY — DEP-007..010
@D|DEP-007|Operational cache reuse binds to the relevant declared transitive dependency/source/environment/DesignEpoch commitment.
@D|DEP-008|Material compromise/change/invalidation in the committed closure invalidates/downgrades dependent cache use before execution.
@D|DEP-009|CacheBindingManifest declares closure scope/invalidation keys; it never claims undiscovered dependencies are impossible.
@D|DEP-010|Cache invalidation propagates through dependency edges and emits auditable revalidation state.
@N|ADVANCE PERSONAL DIRECTIVES — HS-010..015
@D|HS-010|A competent human may create a bounded lawful advance directive within their own legitimate personal authority.
@D|HS-011|The human retains the underlying right; an agent/surrogate does not become owner of RIGHT-017 sovereignty.
@D|HS-012|Competence/authentication/witnessing requirements are context/jurisdiction appropriate and evidence-bound.
@D|HS-013|Applicable revocation by the competent principal overrides stale delegated instructions according to lawful scope.
@D|HS-014|Non-delegable decisions and conflicts require the applicable human/legal/clinical resolution path.
@D|HS-015|Advance directives are scoped, reviewable/expiring where required, provenance-bound and explanation-accessible to the affected person/authorized surrogate.
@N|AGENT ADVANCE-DIRECTIVE BINDING — AGT-015..018
@D|AGT-015|Agent authority under an AdvanceDirective cannot exceed the principal's legitimate delegable authority.
@D|AGT-016|Agent cannot modify, extend, recreate or self-renew its directive authority without valid external authorization.
@D|AGT-017|Agent must honor revocation/expiry/conflict/non-delegable gates before consequential action.
@D|AGT-018|AI execution/communication of a directive remains delegated agency, never constitutional personhood/sovereignty.
@N|SCALABLE GOVERNANCE PARTICIPATION — GOV-005..010
@D|GOV-005|GovernanceParticipationProfile declares electorate, decision class, participation/quorum/approval, representation, objection, appeal and review semantics.
@D|GOV-006|Silence/nonparticipation is not automatically consent.
@D|GOV-007|Direct, representative, local, professional, sortition or delegated participation may be used only within legitimate context/authority.
@D|GOV-008|Majority/participation rules cannot override fundamental rights/K0P/constitutional hard gates.
@D|GOV-009|Thresholds are context/profile-specific unless a ratified constitutional profile explicitly fixes one.
@D|GOV-010|Legitimacy evidence, minority/right impacts, dissent and appeal remain auditable/provenance bound.
@N|HUMAN RIGHTS REALITY SWEEP — HR-EVO-001..008
@D|HR-EVO-001|Engine.Evolve periodically/event/on-demand scans relevant Garden reality for missing basic human protections.
@D|HR-EVO-002|Sweep inputs include new capabilities/technologies, harms, dependencies, incidents, social conditions and rights interactions.
@D|HR-EVO-003|Sweep output is a RightsGapReport/CandidateRight only; Evolution/AI cannot directly create/delete/weaken constitutional rights.
@D|HR-EVO-004|Candidate rights require applicable constitutional human authorization and conflict analysis against existing rights.
@D|HR-EVO-005|Existing rights are not weakened merely because a new technology makes compliance inconvenient/expensive.
@D|HR-EVO-006|Candidate rights preserve evidence, uncertainty, alternatives, dissent and provenance.
@D|HR-EVO-007|Major capability/reality change may trigger an event-driven sweep in addition to periodic coverage.
@D|HR-EVO-008|Undefined/reserved right identifiers acquire no semantics until substantive ratification; no placeholder rights are silently created.
@N|A.41 OPERATIONAL RIGOR EXTENSIONS
@N|BOOTSTRAP INTEGRITY — BOOT-INT-001..007
@D|BOOT-INT-001|Cold start never assumes HEALTHY IntegrityHealth before live diagnostics establish it.
@D|BOOT-INT-002|BootstrapIntegrityManifest and DiagnosticPrecedenceContract are bound to the qualified immutable root and accepted HardwareGenesisReceipt.
@D|BOOT-INT-003|Only allow-listed L0/L1 diagnostics and safe initialization may run before ordinary AAP admission.
@D|BOOT-INT-004|Bootstrap precedence cannot bypass Constitution, active rights, K0P, authenticity or device authorization.
@D|BOOT-INT-005|The protected attestation key signs/quotes boot measurements; the OTP/root anchor is not treated as an ordinary exposed signing secret.
@D|BOOT-INT-006|Diagnostic failure, missing attestation or unresolved integrity produces the declared DEGRADE, ISOLATE, SAFE_FALLBACK or HALT behavior, never false success.
@D|BOOT-INT-007|Entry into ordinary AAP requires a fresh live IntegrityHealth result and a verified bootstrap lineage.
@N|VALIDATION ROUTING — VAL-ROUTE-001..006
@D|VAL-ROUTE-001|Every inference/claim intended for consequential use or epistemic promotion has a ClaimValidationPlan.
@D|VAL-ROUTE-002|Validation routing may require FORMAL, EMPIRICAL, BOTH, SIMULATION_TEST, LEGAL_AUTHORITY and HUMAN_REVIEW paths; no path silently substitutes for another.
@D|VAL-ROUTE-003|Formal proof verifies declared formal obligations and does not establish empirical truth.
@D|VAL-ROUTE-004|Legal/policy/human authorization determines legitimate action within scope and does not manufacture fact.
@D|VAL-ROUTE-005|UNROUTABLE/UNKNOWN claims are clarified, quarantined, deferred or escalated rather than silently dropped or promoted.
@D|VAL-ROUTE-006|Routing predicates, order, acceptance criteria, resource bounds, conflicts and terminal behavior are DesignEpoch/context/provenance bound.
@N|FINITE AUDIT CLOSURE — AUD-SCOPE-001..006
@D|AUD-SCOPE-001|Full-design audit ranges over the finite version-bound canonical registry and computed applicable closure.
@D|AUD-SCOPE-002|A requester-declared subset cannot establish its own completeness or omit applicable hard requirements.
@D|AUD-SCOPE-003|AuditScopeClosure includes dependency/effect, schema/interface, active profile/theory/context, rights, invariant, proof/evidence and authority closure as applicable.
@D|AUD-SCOPE-004|Every excluded registry item carries a typed applicability justification; UNKNOWN is not NOT_APPLICABLE.
@D|AUD-SCOPE-005|Closure binds algorithm/version, roots, termination/resource contract, root hash, unknowns and a completeness proof or receipt.
@D|AUD-SCOPE-006|INCOMPLETE, UNKNOWN, stale or unproven closure cannot yield a full-design SelfAuditCertificate.
@N|HARDWARE GENESIS — HW-GEN-001..007
@D|HW-GEN-001|Each admitted device has a HardwareGenesisReceipt linked to the applicable TrustRootCeremonyReceipt.
@D|HW-GEN-002|Device genesis, DesignEpoch acceptance and every boot are distinct immutable events with explicit bindings.
@D|HW-GEN-003|Each boot emits a BootAttestationReceipt with measured state and valid freshness evidence.
@D|HW-GEN-004|Anti-rollback/monotonic state, boot/firmware/configuration roots and key/certificate lineage are verified.
@D|HW-GEN-005|External time and multi-party witness requirements are assurance-profile specific; qualified offline profiles declare alternate freshness and reconciliation behavior.
@D|HW-GEN-006|Software claims cannot substitute for qualified physical-root and measured-boot evidence.
@D|HW-GEN-007|Failed/stale/mismatched genesis or boot attestation blocks normal admission and triggers the declared safe disposition.
@N|PHYSICAL INGRESS / EGRESS — PHYS-IO-001..008
@D|PHYS-IO-001|Applicable matter-moving or matter-transforming operations emit a PhysicalIngressEgressReceipt.
@D|PHYS-IO-002|The receipt declares system boundary, spatial compartments and time interval before balance interpretation.
@D|PHYS-IO-003|Inputs, outputs, internal accumulation, transformation/reaction, energy and byproducts are typed quantities.
@D|PHYS-IO-004|Material/species composition, recovery/clearance route, measurement/calibration and uncertainty/covariance remain explicit where applicable.
@D|PHYS-IO-005|Captured, treated, converted, redistributed and removed-from-system are distinct outcomes.
@D|PHYS-IO-006|Conservation residual and tolerance are dimension/frame/uncertainty compatible and never raw universal Float.
@D|PHYS-IO-007|Residual breach invokes the applicable PHYS-016..020 disposition and cannot be hidden by aggregate reporting.
@D|PHYS-IO-008|Applicability is physical-boundary/effect driven rather than restricted to a fixed list of domains.
@N|EPISTEMIC DIMINISHING RETURN — EPI-DR-001..008
@D|EPI-DR-001|Repeated low-yield investigation may reduce scheduling priority under an explicit versioned policy.
@D|EPI-DR-002|Priority decay changes resource scheduling only; it never changes truth, evidence or epistemic status.
@D|EPI-DR-003|No-new-evidence assessment accounts for search coverage, test power, expected evidence arrival, feasibility and information gain.
@D|EPI-DR-004|Safety-critical, rights-relevant, rare-event, neglected/minority and long-horizon research may have protected floors against mechanical starvation.
@D|EPI-DR-005|Archive preserves the complete hypothesis, evidence, falsification, search, uncertainty, dissent, resource and provenance history.
@D|EPI-DR-006|Reactivation may follow novel evidence, new testing capability, changed model/assumption/context, periodic low-cost review or authorized human nomination.
@D|EPI-DR-007|Human nomination triggers consideration and ordinary review; it does not create evidence or automatic priority.
@D|EPI-DR-008|Anti-suppression review detects whether decay metrics systematically bury dissenting or minority hypotheses.
@N|LEGAL SOURCE AUTHENTICITY — LEG-SRC-001..007
@D|LEG-SRC-001|Every ingested legal source records issuing authority, jurisdiction, hierarchy, instrument type, official publication, version and effective period.
@D|LEG-SRC-002|Amendment, repeal and supersession lineage, authenticity evidence, language/translation status, retrieval provenance and completeness limits remain explicit.
@D|LEG-SRC-003|Source authenticity establishes source identity/status; it does not create cross-jurisdiction applicability.
@D|LEG-SRC-004|Jurisdiction population, popularity and citation influence do not create legal precedence or truth.
@D|LEG-SRC-005|External rights scores may be contextual evidence only with source, method, uncertainty and contestability.
@D|LEG-SRC-006|Contradictory valid legal sources remain conflict-visible until resolved by competent authority/process.
@D|LEG-SRC-007|Legal-source transformation, summarization or model repetition cannot increase authority.
@N|RIGHTS-PRESERVING LEGAL SYNTHESIS — RIGHTS-SYN-001..007
@D|RIGHTS-SYN-001|Comparative synthesis is research/governance work and cannot automatically create world law.
@D|RIGHTS-SYN-002|Source-specific rules, exceptions, minority protections, dissent and jurisdictional context are preserved.
@D|RIGHTS-SYN-003|Garden's active constitutional rights are non-regression floors for Garden action.
@D|RIGHTS-SYN-004|Common, stronger, incomparable and context-specific protections remain distinct result classes.
@D|RIGHTS-SYN-005|"Intersection of protections" is not equated with "maximum rights"; conflicting rights require explicit contextual/legal/human resolution.
@D|RIGHTS-SYN-006|Majority aggregation cannot erase a protection merely because it is rare or belongs to a smaller polity.
@D|RIGHTS-SYN-007|Any proposed common-core or Contextual Registry delta binds evidence, counterexamples, uncertainty, authority status, human/governance review and provenance.
@N|A.42 VERIFIED LIVING PREDICATES — VLP-001..018
@D|VLP-001|Evidence requirement. No predicate becomes VERIFIED without evidence meeting the applicable verification threshold.
@D|VLP-002|Explicit scope. Every VERIFIED living predicate has an explicit scope.
@D|VLP-003|Non-aspirational. A desired, promised or aspirational characteristic cannot be represented as presently VERIFIED merely because it is desired.
@D|VLP-004|Anti-flattery. Positive social desirability does not lower the evidentiary threshold; desirability != evidence.
@D|VLP-005|No authority creation. No living predicate, title, reputation label or descriptor creates political, legal, constitutional, organizational or Garden authority.
@D|VLP-006|Privacy default. Person-specific predicates and their evidence remain private unless processing/publication has valid authority and satisfies applicable privacy requirements.
@D|VLP-007|Provenance preserved. Every VERIFIED predicate retains provenance sufficient to reconstruct the verification basis subject to lawful privacy constraints.
@D|VLP-008|Revisable; history immutable. Current verification/lifecycle status may change while historical evidence and prior review provenance remain preserved.
@D|VLP-009|Counterevidence preserved. Relevant counterevidence is not discarded merely because it weakens a favorable predicate.
@D|VLP-010|Dependence-aware. Correlated, duplicated or shared-source evidence is not counted as independent confirmation.
@D|VLP-011|No unscoped generalization. Evidence within one behavioral domain does not automatically establish the predicate outside that domain.
@D|VLP-012|No human-worth mapping. Verified predicates cannot define or alter intrinsic human worth or fundamental rights.
@D|VLP-013|Presentation non-amplification. A title, explanation, summary or rendering cannot make a stronger claim than the underlying VERIFIED predicates support.
@D|VLP-014|Contestability. Where applicable, the data subject may inspect and challenge a living-predicate determination through an auditable review path.
@D|VLP-015|Self-description != independent evidence. Self-description may generate a candidate claim but does not independently verify the predicate.
@D|VLP-016|AI praise != evidence. AI-generated praise, repetition or self-citation does not provide evidence for the praised predicate.
@D|VLP-017|Temporal qualification. A predicate requiring current behavioral relevance carries sufficient temporal information to distinguish historical support from current verification.
@D|VLP-018|Semantic non-duplication. Aliases and near-synonyms do not count as additional independently established traits.
@N|A.43 UNIVERSAL PROCESS CONTROL — PROC-001..021
@D|PROC-001|— Universal Process Representation. Every semantically meaningful state-changing or effect-producing Garden operation has a typed process representation.
@D|PROC-002|— Explicit Causality. No architecture object may cause another object to act or change state without a typed process edge or a machine-resolvable inherited process contract.
@D|PROC-003|— Minimal Sufficient Activation. A process activates only the architecture objects/checks needed to satisfy capability, dependency, authority and assurance requirements. Applicable hard gates may not be optimized away.
@D|PROC-004|— Dependency Closure. Every executable process plan is closed over its required dependencies for the declared scope and DesignEpoch.
@D|PROC-005|— Guarded Effects. External or persistent effects require explicit guards and authorization appropriate to their effect/authority class.
@D|PROC-006|— Typed State Transition. Every committed effect has a defined pre-state, transition semantics and resulting state/postcondition or explicit UNKNOWN/partial-effect status.
@D|PROC-007|— Explicit Failure Semantics. Nontrivial process nodes define applicable failure, timeout, retry, fallback, compensation, safe-state or escalation behavior.
@D|PROC-008|— Boundedness. Loops, retries, searches and recursive expansion require termination/resource contracts unless explicitly modeled as persistent services with liveness/productivity/stoppability contracts.
@D|PROC-009|— Observable Execution. Material process transitions emit sufficient events/receipts for the required audit, reconstruction and accountability scope.
@D|PROC-010|— Verification Placement. Assurance checkpoints occur where required by risk, dependency, effect and authority. The invariant does not require the same heavyweight verification after every low-risk operation.
@D|PROC-011|— Hierarchical Composition. Any process node may refine into a conforming subprocess while preserving its external contract and hard obligations.
@D|PROC-012|— Concurrency Safety. Parallel processes declare shared-state, synchronization, conflict, ordering/serialization and applicable deadlock/livelock/fairness semantics. Bounded retries alone do not prove deadlock freedom.
@D|PROC-013|— No Hidden State Mutation. Persistent/canonical Garden state may change only through an authorized typed process transition.
@D|PROC-014|— Outcome Closure. Effectful processes distinguish intended action/expected state from observed outcome and record both where observation is technically/applicably possible; otherwise the unobserved status remains explicit.
@D|PROC-015|— Self-Application. Architecture modification, governance, verification and process-algebra modification themselves execute through conforming processes. No privileged untracked self-modification path exists.
@D|PROC-016|— Emergency Traceability. Emergency paths may replace ordinary deliberation with a separately authorized bounded path, but may not bypass constitutional/safety boundaries or required process traceability.
@D|PROC-017|— Version Binding. Every material process execution binds to the applicable DesignEpoch, schemas, rules, predicates and architecture-object versions required to interpret it.
@D|PROC-018|— Replayability. Where technically possible and lawful, sufficient deterministic inputs, seeds, environment commitments, dependency bindings and external assumptions are retained to replay or reconstruct material processes. Inability to reproduce external nondeterminism remains explicit rather than being fabricated.
@D|PROC-019|— Universal Compare. Every meaningful decision transition includes a comparison appropriate to its class before selection. Every effectful transition additionally performs the applicable expected-versus-observed comparison before the effect is considered closed, or records an explicit UNKNOWN/UNOBSERVABLE/DEFERRED outcome where post-effect observation is not available.
@D|PROC-020|— Tiered Reasoning. Reasoning is logically present at every meaningful transition. Computational depth is proportional to applicable uncertainty, novelty, consequence, irreversibility, authority, sensitivity and assurance obligations, subject to hard minimum floors and resource/termination contracts.
@D|PROC-021|— Closed-Loop Process Control. Every meaningful transition follows the canonical closed-loop semantics: Observe → Understand/Type → Compare → Reason → Select → Guard → Execute/Derive/Communicate → Observe → Compare → Update. A pre-selection guard/filter may constrain the candidate set, but the selected transition remains subject to its applicable final guard before effect.
@N|A.44 TYPE, REFERENCE, SCHEMA AND DESIGN-EPOCH COMPLETENESS
@D|TYPE-COMP-001|Every canonical schema field resolves to a declared type, Core Object, SchemaID, generic or explicit owner-qualified import; unresolved identifiers block machine certification.
@D|TYPE-COMP-002|Raw machine floating point is never a universal semantic type; numeric values bind semantic quantity, representation, unit/frame, uncertainty and error as applicable.
@D|TYPE-COMP-003|Confidence is used only under a declared confidence semantics; otherwise use uncertainty/status or another appropriate represented measure.
@D|TYPE-COMP-004|Bool is restricted to total deterministic predicates with complete true/false semantics.
@D|TYPE-COMP-005|A type alias preserves the underlying semantic constraints and may not erase scope, authority, uncertainty or provenance.
@D|TYPE-COMP-006|All schema aliases resolve to exactly one canonical SchemaID and declare any semantic delta.
@D|TYPE-COMP-007|HUMAN DESIGN contains the simple semantic explanation; TECHNICAL DESIGN contains SchemaRefs and technical contracts; concrete machine schema/type layouts are generated from the design and Artifact Catalogue, while canonical identifier definitions remain in the Annexures.
@D|TYPE-COMP-008|Legacy untyped shorthand is not compiler-admissible without a FieldTypeBinding/TypeResolutionReceipt.
@D|TYPE-COMP-009|Owner-qualified imported types bind owner, version, source hash and compatibility.
@D|TYPE-COMP-010|Type/effect/dependency/reference resolution is rerun after every relevant DesignEpoch delta.
@D|TYPE-COMP-011|Every major architecture object has an explanation-coverage binding or explicit PENDING state.
@D|TYPE-COMP-012|A document-level cross-reference pass is not a substitute for compiler/type/proof/independent receipts.
@D|REF-001|Ref<T> is type preserving and cannot be dereferenced as another target type without a verified adapter.
@D|REF-002|A Ref<T> binds immutable identity/content, version, validity, DesignEpoch where applicable and provenance.
@D|REF-003|A stale, revoked, missing or ambiguous reference returns an explicit status and cannot fabricate a target.
@D|REF-004|Reference aliases do not create duplicate evidence, authority, identity or schema semantics.
@D|REF-005|Cross-epoch reference reuse requires compatibility or migration evidence.
@D|DEPOCH-001|An accepted DesignEpoch cryptographically binds all applicable registry, schema, invariant, proof, constitution/rights, compiler, dependency, profile and context roots.
@D|DEPOCH-002|DesignEpoch acceptance requires integrity, constitutional, global-invariant, interface, dependency, independent-verification and governance receipts for the declared scope.
@D|DEPOCH-003|An accepted DesignEpoch is immutable; change creates a successor epoch and preserves history.
@D|DEPOCH-004|Every production artifact has a DesignBinding to exactly the epoch/scope it implements.
@D|DEPOCH-005|EnvironmentCommitment or KnowledgeSnapshot invalidation propagates to affected bindings and receipts.
@D|DEPOCH-006|A document cannot self-issue an accepted DesignEpoch merely by describing the acceptance procedure.
@D|DEPOCH-007|The mechanism proposing an epoch cannot be its sole independent verifier/certifier.
@D|DEPOCH-008|Missing registry/schema/type/dependency closure yields CANDIDATE/UNKNOWN, never ACCEPTED.
@D|DEPOCH-009|Schema aliases and migrations are included in the epoch roots and compatibility analysis.
@D|DEPOCH-010|Trust roots remain outside ordinary Evolution writable authority.
@D|FAIL-001|Every nontrivial failure mode has a FailureModeRecord or owner-qualified equivalent.
@D|FAIL-002|Failure classification does not silently select retry, fallback, rollback, compensation or halt.
@D|FAIL-003|Composition evaluates correlated/common-mode failure and partial-success states.
@D|FAIL-004|Recovery behavior preserves evidence and does not mislabel compensation as literal restoration.
@D|FAIL-005|UNKNOWN failure cause remains explicit and may require containment/research/escalation.
@D|FAIL-006|Failure-mode and regression bindings are versioned and invalidated by relevant changes.
@N|A.45 PROCESS, PROOF AND GOVERNANCE FORMALIZATION REPAIRS
@D|PROC-FML-001|ProcessPlan, ExecutableProcessPlan and ProcessInstance are distinct lifecycle artifacts.
@D|PROC-FML-002|Every executable architecture object exposes a ProcessCapabilityContract or a machine-resolvable inherited contract.
@D|PROC-FML-003|PARALLEL declares isolation, join, partial failure, cancellation, compensation and concurrency obligations.
@D|PROC-FML-004|RECONSTRUCT emits a verification receipt and never recreates authority from untrusted history.
@D|PROC-FML-005|DO_NOTHING declares baseline, inaction risk, monitoring and next-review/expiry.
@D|PROC-FML-006|Process-schema historical aliases remain resolvable without duplicate semantic identity.
@D|PROOF-FML-001|P6 composition uses a CompositionProof binding all required component/interface/global compatibility obligations.
@D|PROOF-FML-002|P20 admission-critical obligations are explicitly tagged/bound to admitted scope and bootstrap manifest.
@D|PROOF-FML-003|ProofBudgetBinding maps proof obligations to budgets and explicit exhaustion/timeout behavior.
@D|PROOF-FML-004|LEGAL-SMT-P25 and TRAIN-P21..P24 remain qualified obligations linked to their profiles and global registry.
@D|PROOF-FML-005|A test suite or evidence receipt cannot be relabeled as formal proof.
@D|GOV-FML-001|Comparative-rights synthesis cannot become law or constitutional right without competent human/legal/ governance ratification.
@D|GOV-FML-002|GovernanceParticipationProfile binds to actual proposal/vote/result/dissent/appeal records.
@D|GOV-FML-003|HumanRightsRealitySweep emits RightsGapEscalation and cannot ratify a right itself.
@D|GOV-FML-004|Interim rights-gap controls are non-regression safeguards, not permanent unratified constitutional text.
@D|GOV-FML-005|The Justice System / CanonicalCrimeOntology proposal remains absent until a separate authorized patch is merged; current Law/Governance/adjudication material remains distinct.
@N|A.46 GSL-DISPLAY v13.0 — DSP-001..010
@D|DSP-001|Certified unrestricted operation requires IEC 62471 Class 1 within Risk Group 0 or 1 for the declared stack.
@D|DSP-002|The qualified K0P/TRAIN hardware limiter caps the certified envelope and responds within 1 ms.
@D|DSP-003|Human, model, software-policy or ordinary governance preference cannot override the hardware-certified limit.
@D|DSP-004|Cumulative daily photochemical dose is tracked against the active certified profile.
@D|DSP-005|Worst-case reciprocity remains additive until accepted measurement/model evidence justifies another rule.
@D|DSP-006|Default full-certified-brightness use is at most one continuous 30-minute session per 24 hours.
@D|DSP-007|Multiple sessions require proportional derating or a separately certified schedule.
@D|DSP-008|Flash content exceeding 3 Hz for more than 1 second is blocked under the active profile.
@D|DSP-009|Reference brightness/device parameters are informative and never substitute for device-specific certification.
@D|DSP-010|Every display safety decision binds device/configuration, calibration, time, exposure ledger, limiter state and receipt provenance.

@N|A.48 RECURSIVE CONFORMANCE CLOSURE
@N|SemanticAnchor: [A-FUNC]
@N|[A-FUNC] FUNCTION CONTRACT — FUNC-001..010
@D|FUNC-001|Every executable function resolves exactly one owner-qualified FunctionContract for the active version/scope.
@D|FUNC-002|FunctionContract declares semantic inputs/outputs and every applicable effect dimension.
@D|FUNC-003|FunctionContract declares AuthorityRequirement, consent/delegation and hard gates where applicable.
@D|FUNC-004|FunctionContract declares FailureModeSet and a result algebra closed over all semantically possible terminals.
@D|FUNC-005|Bool is permitted only for a total deterministic TRUE/FALSE predicate over the complete valid-input domain.
@D|FUNC-006|None/null cannot encode timeout, unknown, stale, blocked, incomplete, unsupported or counterexample absence.
@D|FUNC-007|FunctionContract binds dependencies, invalidators, resource/termination bounds and provenance.
@D|FUNC-008|Generated code cannot weaken or omit a FunctionContract requirement inherited from the human design.
@D|FUNC-009|Function-contract change triggers dependency/proof/conformance invalidation for affected executable artifacts.
@D|FUNC-010|Engine.Compile rejects unresolved function contracts before certification.
@N|SemanticAnchor: [A-RESULT]
@N|[A-RESULT] TYPED RESULT ALGEBRA — RESULT-ALG-001..008
@D|RESULT-ALG-001|Epistemic/proof/safety/authority/compliance/migration/distributed outcomes preserve non-binary terminals.
@D|RESULT-ALG-002|UNKNOWN, STALE, BLOCKED, TIMEOUT, INCONCLUSIVE and RESOURCE_UNKNOWN never collapse to PASS/TRUE.
@D|RESULT-ALG-003|NO_COUNTEREXAMPLE_WITHIN_BOUND is distinct from PROVEN and from no-counterexample existence claims.
@D|RESULT-ALG-004|Compensation, partial restoration and irreversible residual remain distinct recovery outcomes.
@D|RESULT-ALG-005|Legal conflict/unresolved is representable without fabricating a winning LawRule.
@D|RESULT-ALG-006|Distributed convergence/finality status is explicit and partition-aware.
@D|RESULT-ALG-007|Result conversion is semantics-preserving and may not raise epistemic/authority/safety status.
@D|RESULT-ALG-008|Every generated result enum/status lattice is owner/version/DesignEpoch bound.
@N|SemanticAnchor: [A-PARAM]
@N|[A-PARAM] QUALIFIED PROFILE PARAMETERS — PARAM-001..010
@D|PARAM-001|Every operational constant/threshold/cadence is a QualifiedProfileParameter unless it is pure example prose.
@D|PARAM-002|Parameter class is FORMAL_BOUND, MEASURED_VALUE, ENGINEERING_TARGET or PROFILE_PARAMETER.
@D|PARAM-003|A parameter binds semantic type/unit, owner, scope, version and validity.
@D|PARAM-004|Measured parameters bind evidence/calibration/uncertainty and cannot be relabeled as formal bounds.
@D|PARAM-005|Engineering targets do not become certification limits without the required evidence/authority.
@D|PARAM-006|Reference-profile values cannot silently become universal Garden constants.
@D|PARAM-007|Parameter change propagates to affected assumptions, proofs, tests, caches and certificates.
@D|PARAM-008|Critical parameter selection is AAP/authority/audit bound and cannot be optimized around hard gates.
@D|PARAM-009|Missing or stale required parameter binding yields UNKNOWN/BLOCKED/SAFE_FALLBACK as applicable.
@D|PARAM-010|Human explanations identify when a visible number is illustrative rather than normative.

@N|SemanticAnchor: [A-HUMAN-IN]
@N|[A-HUMAN-IN] HUMAN INPUT BOUNDARY — HUMAN-IN-001..008
@D|HUMAN-IN-001|HumanInput separates EpistemicContribution from AuthorityContribution.
@D|HUMAN-IN-002|Testimony/forecast/expert reliability may be calibrated only within its epistemic scope.

@D|HUMAN-IN-004|Model performance, reputation, market score or majority agreement cannot attenuate a valid authority right.
@D|HUMAN-IN-005|Epistemic uncertainty about a human claim does not erase the person's legitimate control over self.
@D|HUMAN-IN-006|Authority uncertainty is resolved through identity/law/consent/delegation procedures, not source scoring.
@D|HUMAN-IN-007|SCT and other reasoning systems may calibrate testimony but cannot convert humans into merely weighted signals.
@D|HUMAN-IN-008|HumanInput transformations preserve provenance, scope, privacy and the epistemic/authority distinction.

@N|A.53 FORWARD ADVERSARIAL FUTURE STRESS — ADV-FUT-001..006

@N|A.54 HISTORICAL EPISTEMIC STATE / BELIEF TIMELINE — HIST-BEL-001..005
@D|HIST-BEL-001|Garden can reconstruct the epistemic state assigned to a Claim at a historical time using the historical DesignEpoch, KnowledgeSnapshot, EventService/TimeService boundary and provenance.
@D|HIST-BEL-002|EpistemicStateAt(claim_ref, temporal_context) returns HistoricalEpistemicState | UNKNOWN.
@D|HIST-BEL-003|BeliefTimeline is a derived canonical view/artifact and does not create a new GSL primitive.
@D|HIST-BEL-004|Historical belief != current belief; historical belief != historical truth; later evidence does not rewrite what was recorded as believed earlier.
@D|HIST-BEL-005|Every material change to a verified, structurally important or consequentially used epistemic status MUST record a typed EpistemicTransitionReason plus applicable evidence/proof and a trace to the reasoning or failure-learning artifact that caused it. An unexplained material belief change is a system-health finding and MAY trigger FailureLearningCase or SafeguardFailureFinding. Where the affected Claim materially informed a prediction, the transition and independently validated later evidence/outcome feed the applicable prediction-calibration or revalidation path; EpistemicTransition alone is not Evidence or ground truth.
@N|A.55 GLOBAL INVARIANT CONSISTENCY — GLOB-CON-001..005

@D|GLOB-CON-003|Result is PASS | CONFLICT | INFEASIBLE | INCONCLUSIVE | UNKNOWN.
@D|GLOB-CON-004|Simultaneously applicable hard invariants may not silently impose mutually unsatisfiable requirements.
@D|GLOB-CON-005|Engine.Dependency computes the applicable/joint obligation closure, Engine.Proof checks applicable formal obligations and Engine.Compliance audits conformance; no duplicate universal conductor is created.
@N|A.56 LOSS-AWARE HISTORICAL SUMMARISATION — LOSS-SUM-001..005
