@N|A.57 CONTINUOUS FAILURE LEARNING — FAIL-LEARN-001..004
@D|FAIL-LEARN-001|— Persistent Failure-Learning Obligation
@N|Every materially validated failure, including one discovered retrospectively, enters Continuous Failure Learning &
@N|Recurrence Prevention. Closure of the immediate incident does not erase unresolved root-cause, recurrence, regression
@N|or revalidation obligations. Retention/continued processing remain bounded by privacy, law, safety, evidence and
@N|resource
@N|rules.
@D|FAIL-LEARN-002|— Safeguard Blind-Spot Analysis
@N|Every material failure investigation asks:
@N|1. What caused the observed failure?
@N|2. Which applicable prevention, detection, assurance, audit, proof, policy, dependency or monitoring mechanisms were
@N|expected to prevent, detect, intercept or escalate it?
@N|3. Why did each applicable mechanism succeed or fail?
@N|An applicable safeguard failure is recorded as SafeguardFailureFinding and is itself subject to failure learning.
@D|FAIL-LEARN-003|— Structural Generalisation and Recurrence Prevention
@N|A materially supported causal failure pattern is searched across the bounded applicable closure of current state,
@N|relevant history, deployed instances, pending changes and applicable future candidates. Repairing only the originally
@N|observed instance is insufficient where evidence supports broader generalisation. Coverage, exclusions, uncertainty and
@N|resource limits remain explicit.
@D|FAIL-LEARN-004|— Assurance-Invalidation Propagation
@N|When a defect is discovered in an auditor, verifier, proof method, dependency resolver, safety checker or other
@N|assurance
@N|mechanism, Garden identifies materially dependent prior receipts, certificates, decisions and deployments. Affected
@N|artifacts become STALE or REVALIDATION_REQUIRED until rechecked through an acceptable independent path.
@N|A.58 UNIVERSAL PROCESS ACTIVATION — PROC-ACT-001..012
@D|PROC-ACT-001|Every activated Garden process has a typed TriggerEnvelope or an explicitly declared persistent-service activation contract.
@D|PROC-ACT-002|Trigger occurrence, admission, execution, commitment and physical/semantic effect are distinct states and are separately receipted where consequential.
@D|PROC-ACT-003|Subscriptions, trigger bindings and cached/pre-authorized execution envelopes cannot create authority, widen capability or survive outside their current scope, validity, DesignEpoch, authority or dependency closure.
@D|PROC-ACT-004|Duplicate delivery cannot cause duplicate consequential effect unless duplicate execution is explicitly declared safe.
@D|PROC-ACT-005|Recursive trigger chains preserve causal ancestry and are bounded against oscillation, event storms and resource exhaustion.
@D|PROC-ACT-006|Critical triggers cannot be silently dropped because of queue saturation; overload receives an explicit typed disposition.
@D|PROC-ACT-007|Mandatory eligible work receives bounded starvation protection.
@D|PROC-ACT-008|A required continuous monitor that becomes unavailable produces an explicit DEGRADED/UNKNOWN state and its declared fallback; silence is not health.
@D|PROC-ACT-009|Distributed execution ownership is epoch/lease/fencing bound; an executor that has lost ownership cannot commit a consequential stale result.
@D|PROC-ACT-010|Subscription delivery and consequential use remain constrained by current privacy, consent, authority, policy and capability scope.
@D|PROC-ACT-011|Revocation invalidates affected subscriptions and pending unauthorized work and applies applicable retention/deletion rules.
@D|PROC-ACT-012|Missed, failed, expired, dead-lettered, coalesced or shed triggers remain auditable according to consequence and retention requirements.
@N|A.59 SCHEDULING — SCHED-001..005
@D|SCHED-001|Scheduling preserves hard safety, constitutional, authority, deadline and continuity floors before optimization.
@D|SCHED-002|Priority is multi-dimensional; no universal scalar may silently trade away hard constraints.
@D|SCHED-003|Priority inversion that could violate a mandatory deadline or safety obligation MUST be bounded or resolved.
@D|SCHED-004|Preemption/cancellation occurs only at semantically safe boundaries or through declared compensation/recovery.
@D|SCHED-005|Periodic scheduling declares clock-degradation and missed-run semantics.
@N|A.60 PRESENTATION BUILDER / DOCUMENT LEVEL OWNERSHIP — DOC-PRES-001..011
@D|DOC-PRES-001|The five H/S/T/THA/A source files remain the authoritative human-documentation suite; generated reader views do not destructively replace them.
@D|DOC-PRES-002|Presentation may change layout, ordering, typography, navigation, diagrams and progressive disclosure but may not change canonical identity, authority, epistemic status or definition ownership.
@D|DOC-PRES-003|Every generated reader view binds exact source versions/roots, stable anchors and a reproducible PresentationBuildManifest.
@D|DOC-PRES-004|Material qualifiers including scope, conditionality, uncertainty, temporality, exceptions, rights, authority and applicability MUST survive simplification. Semantic strengthening or weakening is projection failure.
@D|DOC-PRES-005|Theory, Equation and Artifact Cards are views of canonical objects, not competing definitions.
@D|DOC-PRES-006|Breadcrumbs, reader paths, compact reference panels and display hierarchy are navigation projections only. Default generated paths include Implementer, Auditor and Scientist tracks; additional audience paths may be added without changing canonical source ownership or authority.
@D|DOC-PRES-007|Generated diagrams bind source semantics and declare abstraction/omitted detail; a simplified diagram cannot silently become the normative system model. Rich projections prefer vector rendering where feasible; Mermaid or equivalent source syntax is an implementation choice, not canonical truth.
@D|DOC-PRES-008|Hand editing a generated reader projection does not modify canonical Garden design.
@D|DOC-PRES-009|Reader-view construction follows the level-ownership rules in DOC-OWN-001..012 and cannot reassign canonical ownership through layout or repetition.
@D|DOC-PRES-010|A reader view may surface Level-3 detail beside a Level-2 flow only as a source-bound drill-down, never as a competing definition.
@D|DOC-PRES-011|When a reader projection compresses or relocates technical detail, it preserves what enters, what happens, where it goes, what comes out and how failure/uncertainty routes.
@N|A.61 BRIDGE / TYPED REPRESENTATION & OPERATOR ALGEBRA — REP-001..025
@D|REP-001|— Representation Is Not Identity
@N|A numerical, vector, matrix, tensor, embedding, operator or hardware representation of a semantic object is never the
@N|semantic object itself. Operations on a representation may inform, suggest, approximate or implement declared semantics
@N|only through the applicable correspondence and validity contracts.
@D|REP-002|— Typed Axis Semantics
@N|Every represented index, dimension and tensor/matrix/vector axis has explicit semantic/index binding. Reorder, reshape,
@N|broadcast, aggregation or alignment may not silently change the meaning of an axis.
@D|REP-003|— Explicit Loss Characterization
@N|Every semantic-to-computational projection declares EXACT, APPROXIMATE, LOSSY or INCOMPATIBLE behavior as applicable,
@N|including preserved properties, lost properties and error/loss bounds. Uncharacterized loss cannot be reported as exact.
@D|REP-004|— Representation Provenance
@N|Every material representation binds its semantic source, encoder/transform, model or algorithm version, DesignEpoch,
@N|validity domain and provenance. Derived representations preserve lineage to those bindings.
@D|REP-005|— Basis/Frame Compatibility
@N|Representations may be combined or compared only when their mathematical basis, feature space, coordinate frame or
@N|physical reference frame is compatible or connected by an explicit valid alignment/transform.
@D|REP-006|— Metric Qualification
@N|A RepresentationMetric declares the semantic/representation space, valid range, method, validity domain and applicable
@N|threshold semantics. Metric values from incompatible spaces are not silently comparable.
@D|REP-007|— Approximation Error Propagation
@N|Approximation, quantization, numerical error and loss propagate through applicable operator composition. A later stage
@N|cannot silently discard or narrow upstream error merely because computation completed.
@D|REP-008|— No Similarity-to-Truth Promotion
@N|Candidate-match and retrieval thresholds are computational convenience values. Crossing a similarity threshold is a
@N|retrieval/pruning signal, not semantic equivalence, truth, evidence or authority.
@D|REP-009|— No Correlation-to-Causation Promotion
@N|A causal/relation matrix may generate candidate paths but matrix reachability, correlation or operator connectivity may
@N|not be promoted to causal proof. Causal claims require the applicable causal model, assumptions, identification
@N|conditions and evidence.
@D|REP-010|— Neural Output Is Epistemically Unpromoted
@N|A NeuralInferenceReceipt establishes the computation and its bindings, not the truth of decoded content. Neural output
@N|enters as ClaimCandidate or another appropriately typed computational result and follows ordinary epistemic validation.
@D|REP-011|— Stale Representation Invalidates Applicability
@N|A representation whose semantic source, model, basis/frame, metric, dependency, environment, DesignEpoch or other
@N|validity binding is stale or invalid cannot remain operationally applicable merely because stored numerical data exist.
@D|REP-012|— Exactness Requires Correspondence Proof
@N|A represented path may be classified EXACT only when the declared preserved semantics are proven to commute through the
@N|encode/operate/decode path inside the declared validity domain. Pure round-trip exactness requires decode(encode(x)) ≡ x
@N|for the declared semantic closure.
@D|REP-013|— Operator Domain/Codomain Closure
@N|Operator application and composition require compatible typed domain/codomain contracts. Incompatible domains require an
@N|explicit validated transform; implicit coercion is prohibited.
@D|REP-014|— Resource-Aware Representation Selection
@N|Bridge may select sparse, dense, low-rank, factorized, implicit, hardware-specific or other qualified representations
@N|using resource/cost information, but resource optimization cannot weaken rights, safety, authority, proof, epistemic or
@N|other applicable hard gates.
@D|REP-015|— Semantic Recoverability or Explicit Loss
@N|Where semantic recovery/decoding is partial or impossible, the unrecoverable semantic properties are explicit. A lossy
@N|or one-way representation cannot be treated as if the full semantic source were recoverable.
@D|REP-016|— Fixed-Point Existence != Convergence != Stability != Termination
@N|Fixed-point existence, uniqueness, convergence, stability/contractivity and process termination are distinct properties.
@N|No one may be inferred from another without a valid theorem/model and representation correspondence covering that
@N|inference. Spectral conclusions require a valid operator projection and qualified analysis.
@D|REP-017|— Dependency Distance != Recursive Repair Depth
@N|Graph/operator distance, matrix power and represented reachability may estimate or prioritize candidate impact, but they
@N|do not define the semantic recursive-repair depth. Engine.Dependency and the actual process/repair contracts control the
@N|authoritative closure and recursion depth.
@D|REP-018|— Representation Completeness Required for No-Hidden-State Claims
@N|A representation may claim that no semantically relevant hidden state is omitted only when the encode/decode
@N|correspondence proof covers the full declared semantic closure and all permitted state channels for that claim.
@D|REP-019|— Recursive Analysis Is Multi-Method and Assurance-Aware
@N|Garden may combine spectral/operator analysis, ranking functions, well-founded measures, resource recurrences, abstract
@N|interpretation, model checking, simulation, symbolic methods and other valid techniques. Multiple independent methods
@N|are required only when mandated by the applicable assurance profile, when one method remains inconclusive/approximate,
@N|or when no single decisive proof discharges the obligation. A complete valid proof may satisfy the obligation alone
@N|where the applicable assurance policy permits.
@D|REP-020|— Analytical Pruning Cannot Replace Validation
@N|Spectral, analytical, approximate, matrix, neural or other pruning may reduce/prioritize candidate work only within its
@N|declared soundness/assurance contract. Surviving candidates still pass every applicable canonical validation and hard
@N|gate.
@D|REP-021|— Recursive Operator Analysis Must Preserve Correspondence
@N|A recursive property derived from an operator representation may be transferred to the semantic recursive process only
@N|when the applicable projection/correspondence proof covers the property and validity domain used by the analysis.
@D|REP-022|— Optimization Preserves Correspondence
@N|An optimization may accelerate, approximate, batch, cache, precompile or prune a representation-based operation only
@N|within its declared correspondence and approximation contract. If the optimized path cannot preserve that contract it
@N|enters the declared canonical fallback, blocked, deferred or unknown path and records the disposition.
@D|REP-023|— Optimization Cannot Create Admissibility
@N|An optimization, ranking, spectral result, cache hit, similarity threshold, pruning decision or performance estimate
@N|cannot create truth, evidence, authority, safety, proof, applicability or admissibility. Mandatory candidates, hard
@N|gates
@N|and materially unresolved UNKNOWN states cannot be silently removed unless the applicable assurance obligation proves
@N|that elimination sound.
@D|REP-024|— Optimization Validity Is Dependency-Bound
@N|Cached, precompiled, tensorized, approximate or otherwise optimized results remain valid only while their semantic,
@N|representation, dependency, assumption, model, environment, hardware, compiler, precision and DesignEpoch bindings
@N|remain valid. Relevant invalidation makes the optimized artifact STALE before operational reuse.
@D|REP-025|— Performance Claims Are Evidence-Bound
@N|Estimated performance improvement remains a model projection, benchmark estimate or engineering target. Actual
@N|performance improvement may be reported as measured only when supported by a declared workload/hardware/compiler/
@N|baseline measurement profile with provenance and uncertainty where applicable.
@N|A.62 UNIVERSAL ENGINEERING DESIGN — ENG-DES-001..015
@D|ENG-DES-001|Every imported engineering design binds an immutable source content root, acquisition context, format/version identity, access policy, provenance and DesignEpoch; import never mutates the source.
@D|ENG-DES-002|Import authenticates source and resolves native dependency/reference closure; missing, encrypted, unauthorized, stale or unavailable dependencies remain explicit.
@D|ENG-DES-003|Every parsed chunk has stable native identity/path, content hash, order/dependency semantics and reverse traceability to the source root.
@D|ENG-DES-004|Geometry import preserves coordinate frames, topology, orientation, units, dimensions, tolerances, precision, materials, interfaces and approximation/loss within the declared scope.
@D|ENG-DES-005|Unsupported, approximated, repaired, ambiguous, nonmanifold, lossy or unmapped source constructs are never silently treated as exact/complete.
@D|ENG-DES-006|EngineeringDesignSemanticMap provides bidirectional traceability between source spans/chunks/geometry and
@N|Garden components, interfaces, requirements, constraints, quantities, behaviors and analyses.
@D|ENG-DES-007|Source access/licensing/restrictions are checked before import/use/derivation/disclosure; UNKNOWN never creates permission, ownership or authority.
@D|ENG-DES-008|EngineeringDesignImportReceipt.result is derived from the complete access, format, reference, chunk, geometry, semantic, loss, ambiguity, security and resource findings.
@D|ENG-DES-009|Assessment binds exact source/import/semantic roots, scope, objectives, requirements, models, evidence, assumptions, environment, KnowledgeSnapshot, assurance profile and DesignEpoch.
@D|ENG-DES-010|Missing/UNKNOWN mandatory safety, physics, interface, requirement or evidence obligations prevent
@N|ACCEPTABLE; official certification and operational authority remain separate.
@D|ENG-DES-011|EngineeringDesignSafetyCase exposes hazards, counterevidence, assumptions, operating envelope, controls, continuity/handoff, residual risk, unknowns and independent review.
@D|ENG-DES-012|Every improvement candidate carries an exact semantic/source delta, affected closure, benefits, costs, risks, tradeoffs, uncertainty, access constraints, rollback and validation plan.
@D|ENG-DES-013|Current design and an evolving physical DO_NOTHING baseline are mandatory comparators where applicable; predicted performance cannot validate itself.
@D|ENG-DES-014|Acceptance/partial acceptance requires the exact current validation, rights/access, proof, safety, authority, monitoring and DesignEpoch closure; improve_engineering_design cannot deploy or certify.
@D|ENG-DES-015|Source, adapter, dependency, semantic map, requirement, model, evidence, environment or DesignEpoch drift preserves history but makes affected current receipts STALE and reopens closure.
@N|A.63 CRITICAL-CONTINUITY CONTAINMENT — CCC-001..010
@D|CCC-001|Deployed critical-infrastructure HSA uncertainty/violation follows AGENCY/AUTHORITY REVOCATION → INDEPENDENT
@N|SAFE CONTROL HANDOFF → MINIMUM SAFE CONTINUITY → QUALIFIED RECOVERY.
@D|CCC-002|Suspect agency, authority, credentials, leases, commands and actuator paths are revoked/fenced at independently verifiable enforcement points before handoff.
@D|CCC-003|Fallback control is separately qualified for the exact infrastructure, physical envelope, environment and minimum-safe-service scope.
@D|CCC-004|Fallback operation does not depend on suspect mutable cognition, goals, credentials or authority; shared lineage or hidden dependence is explicit and may make independence UNKNOWN/FAIL.
@D|CCC-005|Handoff state/control is authenticated, integrity/epoch bound and minimized to the declared safe-continuity need.
@D|CCC-006|Determinism alone, majority agreement, replication, software isolation or separate labels do not prove safety, qualification or independence.
@D|CCC-007|Abrupt physical shutdown is not the universal fallback; it is selected only when the domain safety case proves it safer and feasible for the current state.
@D|CCC-008|Minimum safe continuity has explicit envelope, resources, monitoring, uncertainty, duration and escalation.
@D|CCC-009|Failure/UNKNOWN at any handoff prerequisite cannot restore suspect authority or silently continue ordinary autonomous control.
@D|CCC-010|Recovery requires root-cause closure, repair, independent revalidation/qualification and fresh human/competent authority; history and evidence remain immutable.
@N|A.64 PHYSICAL / BIOLOGICAL DO_NOTHING BASELINE — PHY-BASE-001..006
@D|PHY-BASE-001|For continuous physical/biological systems, DO_NOTHING means no new evaluated intervention while endogenous/environmental dynamics continue; it never implies a frozen world by default.
@D|PHY-BASE-002|PhysicalBaselineTrajectory binds initial state/time, dynamics, boundary/operating conditions and explicit evaluation horizon/resolution.
@D|PHY-BASE-003|Applicable decay, wear, disease, recovery, hazards, failures, human/agent action and exogenous forcing are included or explicitly out of scope with justification.
@D|PHY-BASE-004|Model, measurement, parameter and scenario uncertainty plus sensitivity/model disagreement propagate into projected state, cost and risk.
@D|PHY-BASE-005|Monitoring, review/expiry, handoff and emergency conditions are explicit wherever baseline drift can alter admissibility or safety.
@D|PHY-BASE-006|Missing required trajectory/horizon/uncertainty/monitoring yields INCOMPLETE/UNKNOWN; a zero vector or zero-cost inaction cannot be assumed.
@N|A.65 QUANTUM-CLASSICAL CORRESPONDENCE — QCC-001..008
@D|QCC-001|Every quantum/classical correspondence claim binds exact model classes, observables, purpose and validity regime.
@D|QCC-002|Coarse-graining, decoherence, state preparation, measurement, environment and boundary assumptions are explicit.
@D|QCC-003|CPTP assumptions are stated and justified where applicable; no universal CPTP assumption is imposed outside its valid model class/use.
@D|QCC-004|Markovian, non-Markovian or mixed treatment is explicit with justification and consequences.
@D|QCC-005|Lindblad/GKSL is required or accepted only where its declared assumptions hold; it is not universal quantum dynamics.
@D|QCC-006|Approximation order, error bounds, uncertainty and preserved observables/symmetries/conservation claims are receipt-bound and evidence/proof supported.
@D|QCC-007|Breakdown indicators/thresholds and the required higher-fidelity quantum handoff are explicit and monitored.
@D|QCC-008|Regime/model/environment/calibration/evidence drift invalidates current reuse without rewriting historical receipts; UNKNOWN never becomes valid correspondence.
@N|A.66 COUNTERFACTUAL-INFLUENCE PROVENANCE — CFI-001..008
@D|CFI-001|Material Counterfactual → Decision → Intervention → Observation → Evidence ancestry is explicit, ordered and content-addressed.
@D|CFI-002|Genuine subsequent observations remain empirical even when a counterfactual influenced intervention.
@D|CFI-003|Evidence production is classified OBSERVATIONAL, INTERVENTIONAL, ENDOGENOUS, MIXED or UNKNOWN for its exact scope.
@D|CFI-004|A prediction/counterfactual cannot become independent evidence for itself directly or through copied/transitive support.
@D|CFI-005|Confounding, selection, assignment, feedback, shared source/model and other dependence affect aggregation and independence status.
@D|CFI-006|Counterfactual influence may explain evidence production but cannot manufacture validation, causation, truth, authority or admissibility.
@D|CFI-007|Where the assurance profile requires tamper-evident counterfactual provenance, the complete material influence chain is content-addressed and bound through an accepted cryptographic/external provenance mechanism. Missing, altered, stale or unverifiable required bindings prevent a stronger integrity result.
@D|CFI-008|Counterfactual influence changes dependence, independence and causal interpretation; it does not by itself make a genuine observation non-empirical or prohibit ordinary Evidence/Claim/Knowledge derivation. The initiating counterfactual remains ineligible as independent evidence for itself.
@N|A.67 MICROARCHITECTURAL REFINEMENT / NONINTERFERENCE — SEC-REF-001..010
@D|SEC-REF-001|Consequential semantic security/privacy/authority policy has an explicit refinement contract to exact compiler/runtime/firmware/hardware/configuration machine traces.
@D|SEC-REF-002|Semantic TransitionStep granularity is preserved; CPU instructions/traces are refinement evidence, not one new semantic TransitionStep per instruction.
@D|SEC-REF-003|Attacker capabilities, observation points, shared resources, protected properties, declassification and allowed observables are explicit.
@D|SEC-REF-004|Timing, cache, memory-access, speculative-execution, shared-resource-contention, power/EM and other relevant covert/side channels receive explicit applicable coverage/status.
@D|SEC-REF-005|Noninterference, quantitative leakage bounds and statistical evidence are distinguished; failure to detect leakage is not proof of absence.
@D|SEC-REF-006|Machine-trace abstraction/simulation relates actual traces to semantic operations and preserved policy properties under declared assumptions.
@D|SEC-REF-007|Applicable unmodeled/unobserved channels remain UNKNOWN/OUT_OF_SCOPE and cannot aggregate to PASS.
@D|SEC-REF-008|Optimization, caching, speculation, scheduling, frequency scaling or shared resources cannot silently widen declassification or authority.
@D|SEC-REF-009|Compiler/runtime/firmware/hardware/configuration/environment drift invalidates affected receipts even when semantic SchemaIDs are unchanged.
@D|SEC-REF-010|PASS/LEAKAGE_WITHIN_BOUND/FAIL/INCONCLUSIVE/UNKNOWN/STALE are derived from complete channel/property coverage and cannot be overridden by an asserted field.
@N|A.68 INVARIANT SEMANTIC DELTA / PROOF INVALIDATION — INV-DELTA-001..010
@D|INV-DELTA-001|Every assurance/proof artifact binds exact InvariantSemanticIdentity refs and semantic/text hashes for its scope, not only invariant names or SchemaIDs.
@D|INV-DELTA-002|Invariant semantic identity includes quantification, domains, scope/applicability, modality, temporality, jurisdiction, authority, precedence, exceptions, assumptions, definitions and result/UNKNOWN semantics.
@D|INV-DELTA-003|Every invariant evolution is classified EQUIVALENT, STRENGTHENING, WEAKENING, REFINEMENT, INCOMPARABLE,
@N|REDEFINITION or UNKNOWN.
@D|INV-DELTA-004|Classification is semantic and evidence-bound; lexical similarity/difference does not determine it.
@D|INV-DELTA-005|Changes propagate across the complete proof, contract, test, schema, registry, generated, cached, certification and deployment dependency closure.
@D|INV-DELTA-006|Proof reuse across DesignEpochs requires verified equivalence/refinement/migration evidence adequate for the exact dependent proposition.
@D|INV-DELTA-007|SchemaID compatibility, unchanged identifier text, parse success or implementation compatibility alone cannot justify proof reuse.
@D|INV-DELTA-008|UNKNOWN/INCOMPARABLE/REDEFINITION and unmitigated weakening invalidate/reopen every dependent assurance artifact whose validity cannot be preserved.
@D|INV-DELTA-009|Strengthening does not automatically preserve proofs of satisfiability, implementability or consistency; affected propositions are analyzed individually.
@D|INV-DELTA-010|Historical proof artifacts remain immutable; invalidation changes current applicability/status rather than rewriting history.
@N|A.69 DERIVED-VIEW DIVERGENCE — DRV-DIV-001..007
@D|DRV-DIV-001|Every edited DERIVED_VIEW records source generated hash, edited hash, editor/authority, timestamp/event/provenance, exact delta, semantic classification, reason and authoritative-source relationship.
@D|DRV-DIV-002|Hand editing changes the affected origin/status to MIXED/STALE unless the view is regenerated or separately admitted as SOURCE through ordinary gates.
@D|DRV-DIV-003|A hand-edited view never silently becomes canonical semantic source or overrides its authoritative source.
@D|DRV-DIV-004|Divergence records are append-only and bind generation profile, source root and exact view identity.
@D|DRV-DIV-005|Exact delta/semantic classification cannot be replaced by a generic "edited" flag for material changes.
@D|DRV-DIV-006|Regeneration/reconciliation preserves or explicitly archives/rejects divergent edits according to retention and authority policy.
@D|DRV-DIV-007|A divergence-ledger entry provides provenance only; it does not certify, authorize or semantically admit the edit.
@N|A.70 EPISTEMIC WELL-FOUNDEDNESS — EPI-WF-001..007
@D|EPI-WF-001|Epistemic justification/support graphs are acyclic or have independently grounded SCCs whose cycles do not self-amplify credibility.
@D|EPI-WF-002|Circular evidence/support without adequate independent grounding cannot bootstrap truth, confidence, verification, proof or knowledge.
@D|EPI-WF-003|Copied, transformed, shared-source/model and mutually referential items preserve dependence.
@D|EPI-WF-004|Epistemic support edges remain distinct from causal, runtime and fixed-point feedback edges.
@D|EPI-WF-005|Explicitly admitted causal/fixed-point feedback retains its own existence/convergence/stability/termination/ liveness semantics and cannot be reused as circular epistemic justification.
@D|EPI-WF-006|Grounding invalidation makes affected current conclusions STALE/UNKNOWN and recomputes dependent closure without rewriting history.
@D|EPI-WF-007|EpistemicWellFoundednessReceipt extends existing K-INT/GSL-STAT rules and cannot weaken evidence independence, provenance or epistemic non-amplification.
@N|A.71 CERTIFIED MACHINE NAMESPACE — NS-HARD-001..005
@D|NS-HARD-001|Canonical certified machine-facing artifacts use stable IDs or canonical qualified identifiers.
@D|NS-HARD-002|Every alias resolves through an explicit current alias record to exactly one canonical target in context.
@D|NS-HARD-003|Ambiguous bare aliases, duplicate qualified names, import-order-dependent resolution and unknown/reserved/ retired identifiers are MALFORMED/FAIL in a canonical Machine Package.
@D|NS-HARD-004|Human-friendly aliases remain permitted in prose/interfaces but cannot create semantic identity, ownership, authority or machine-package acceptance.
@D|NS-HARD-005|DesignEpoch/registry/alias change invalidates namespace and generated-symbol/serialization bindings until revalidated.
@N|A.72 LEGAL FORMALIZATION INTERPRETATION — LEGAL-FORM-001..007
@D|LEGAL-FORM-001|Every interpreted formal result binds authoritative legal source/version/hash, encoding root, toolchain/solver, assumptions/bounds, jurisdiction, scope and bidirectional source traceability.
@D|LEGAL-FORM-002|SMT UNSAT proves contradiction only in the declared formalized model under its encoding, assumptions and bounds; it does not automatically invalidate human law.
@D|LEGAL-FORM-003|The auditable classification distinguishes source-law contradiction, encoding defect, missing exception/context, jurisdiction/scope mismatch, unresolved legal interpretation, no contradiction found and UNKNOWN.
@D|LEGAL-FORM-004|Encoding validation and competent legal/human review remain distinct; solver output cannot create authority or select the only valid reading.
@D|LEGAL-FORM-005|Competing plausible interpretations and missing context remain explicit and block stronger resolution.
@D|LEGAL-FORM-006|Repairing source, encoding, exception/context, jurisdiction or scope invalidates dependent receipts and requires re-execution/review.
@D|LEGAL-FORM-007|The receipt cannot by itself create law, admissibility, judgment, external certification or operational permission.
@N|A.73 DISTINCT EPISTEMIC ARTIFACTS — OBS-ART-001..006
@D|OBS-ART-001|ObservationRecord, Evidence, Claim and KnowledgeRecord are distinct typed artifacts with distinct identities.
@D|OBS-ART-002|Observation/Evidence/Claim/Knowledge derivation creates new linked artifacts; no artifact mutates its ontological kind in place.
@D|OBS-ART-003|Every material derivation has an EpistemicDerivationReceipt binding source, target, owner-qualified function, validation, dependence, scope, uncertainty, information loss and provenance.
@D|OBS-ART-004|An ObservationRecord becomes support for Evidence only through applicable validation; observation is not evidence merely by naming or storage.
@D|OBS-ART-005|Evidence supports/defeats Claim and justified Claims support KnowledgeRecord only under existing epistemic, proof, independence and well-foundedness rules.
@D|OBS-ART-006|Retraction/supersession/invalidation preserves historical artifacts and updates dependent current status/ applicability through explicit relations.
@N|A.74 JURISDICTION-SENSITIVE RIGHTS & RESTRICTIONS — RRCTX-001..008
@D|RRCTX-001|RightsAndRestrictionsContextBinding is scoped to exact artifact/action/source, jurisdiction, time, purpose, recipient, destination, lifecycle and authority.
@D|RRCTX-002|Legal/license/contract/order sources are authenticated, versioned, authority- and provenance-bound.
@D|RRCTX-003|IP, license, export-control, sanctions, secrecy/confidentiality, disclosure, localization, retention and similar restrictions remain distinct typed rule classes.
@D|RRCTX-004|Exceptions, conflicts, mandatory rights and higher-order constitutional/human-right constraints are explicit.
@D|RRCTX-005|UNKNOWN/INCONCLUSIVE/BLOCKED/STALE never creates permission, ownership, license scope or disclosure authority.
@D|RRCTX-006|One jurisdiction's result does not silently universalize to another jurisdiction/context.
@D|RRCTX-007|Source, artifact, law/license, jurisdiction, time, purpose, recipient, destination or authority drift invalidates affected current use.
@D|RRCTX-008|RRCTX is a contextual operational profile, not universal substantive patent law and not patent drafting, filing or portfolio strategy.
@N|A.75 DOCUMENTATION OWNERSHIP — DOC-OWN-001..012
@D|DOC-OWN-001|Human Design owns purpose, ordinary-language meaning, human consequences, rights-facing explanation and the canonical 0–9 reader/navigation hierarchy.
@D|DOC-OWN-002|System & Flow Design owns architecture-object relationships and information, control, authority, evidence, state, failure and lifecycle flows.
@D|DOC-OWN-003|Detailed Technical Specification owns implementation-facing schemas, field contracts, function signatures, result algebras, executable/owner-qualified technical profiles and architecture bindings; complete theory semantic equations/definitions are owned by the specialist Theories & Reference Architectures source.
@D|DOC-OWN-004|Annexures own canonical recurring definitions, identifiers, registries, invariant/test text, catalogue, audit boundary and version history.
@D|DOC-OWN-005|Every normative assertion has one authoritative owner location; other occurrences are explanatory summaries, references or explicitly non-normative examples.
@D|DOC-OWN-006|A cross-reference, table of contents, backlink, display hierarchy or generated projection cannot create or transfer semantic ownership.
@D|DOC-OWN-007|Relocation preserves stable semantic identity, source provenance, inbound/outbound references and the explanation path from Human through System/Technical/Theories-Architectures to Annexure.
@D|DOC-OWN-008|[H-01] is the sole full 0–9 reader/navigation tree; other files use a semantic pointer or a scoped local view.
@D|DOC-OWN-009|[S-EXPLAIN-COVERAGE] owns varying explanation-completeness metadata; the global Human default supplies [H-15], source-root/DesignEpoch and machine-certification status without per-entry repetition.
@D|DOC-OWN-010|Every documentation change binds its exact source roots and DesignEpoch candidate; generated receipts alone determine later machine-certification status.
@D|DOC-OWN-011|Theories & Reference Architectures owns complete canonical semantic definitions for [TH-*] theories and [RA-*] named reference architectures relocated from Technical.
@D|DOC-OWN-012|The specialist source cannot own/override Annexure SchemaIDs/invariants/tests or Technical executable contracts; summaries elsewhere are source-bound pointers/projections rather than competing definitions.
@N|A.76 PRESENTATION VISUALIZATION — DOC-PRES-VIS-001..006

@D|DOC-PRES-VIS-003|Renderer syntax, layout, iconography, color or geometry is presentation metadata and cannot create a canonical semantic relation not present in the bound source.
@D|DOC-PRES-VIS-004|Renderer selection resolves through REG-DIAGRAM-RENDERER; unsupported or stale rendering capability is explicit and cannot be reported as faithful PASS.
@D|DOC-PRES-VIS-005|A flow designated DIAGRAM_REQUIRED by the current documentation/presentation profile has at least one current source-bound DiagramProjectionSpec; missing coverage prevents a claim of complete designated-flow visualization.
@D|DOC-PRES-VIS-006|A designated complex-flow diagram preserves every represented mandatory hard gate, uncertainty/UNKNOWN branch, authority boundary and safety-relevant fallback/handoff. Visual simplification cannot silently remove or weaken them.
@N|A.77 FIRST-USE TERM RESOLUTION — DOC-PRES-LINK-001

@N|A.78 ENGINEERING INTEROPERABILITY — ENG-DES-INTEROP-001..003

@D|ENG-DES-INTEROP-002|Mapping preserves bidirectional source traceability and explicitly records unsupported constructs, extensions, ambiguity, approximation, loss, unit/frame/tolerance differences and namespace conflicts.
@D|ENG-DES-INTEROP-003|Registry presence, successful parsing or partial round trip cannot establish complete semantic equivalence; admission follows the current mapping-validation result and applicable ENG-DES gates.
@N|A.79 EXTERNAL PROVENANCE — PROV-EXT-001..004

@D|PROV-EXT-002|A valid external signature or manifest establishes only the properties supported by its declared trust model; it cannot alone establish truth, ownership, consent, lawful use, originality or absence of manipulation.
@D|PROV-EXT-003|Expiry, revocation, trust-list, content, scheme, tool or mapping change invalidates affected current reuse; unsupported and unmapped assertions remain UNKNOWN or UNVERIFIED.
@D|PROV-EXT-004|A registered external-provenance scheme claiming semantic interoperability with Garden binds an exact version/specification-root claim/assertion mapping. Unsupported, unmapped, ambiguous or conflicting assertions remain explicit and cannot silently aggregate into VALID.
@N|A.80 MODEL-INTERNAL INTERPRETABILITY — EPI-INT-001..003
@D|EPI-INT-001|Every model-internal audit binds exact model/runtime/configuration roots, objective, method, inputs, inspected surface, interventions/controls, uncertainty, limitations and provenance.
@D|EPI-INT-002|Probe accuracy, attribution, saliency, activation pattern or internal correlation is not automatically a causal mechanism, intent, truthfulness guarantee, safety proof or evidence of personhood.
@D|EPI-INT-003|Strong conclusions require method validity, adequate observability/identifiability, controls, stability and applicable independent replication; unresolved alternatives cap the result at a non-stronger disposition.
@N|A.81 CRYPTOGRAPHIC ERASURE — DLC-CRYPTO-001..003
@D|DLC-CRYPTO-001|Cryptographic-erasure claims bind exact payload, encryption/key epochs, discovered replicas, caches, indexes, backups, derivatives, key stores, attestation roots, holds, authority and time scope.
@D|DLC-CRYPTO-002|Key destruction proves access infeasibility only for the declared key/copy closure; unknown copies, exported plaintext, compromised keys and retained derivatives remain explicit residual risk.
@D|DLC-CRYPTO-003|Payload deletion, cryptographic erasure, model unlearning, clean retraining and quarantine are distinct; no one disposition is silently substituted for another or reported beyond its verified boundary.
@N|A.82 DEPRECATION / SUNSET — LIFE-DEP-001..003
@D|LIFE-DEP-001|Deprecation binds exact target/version, owner/authority, reason, replacement, notice, affected closure, migration, exceptions, retention and removal criteria in a DeprecationRecord.
@D|LIFE-DEP-002|A date or warning cannot create removal eligibility; mandatory dependencies, safety, rights, privacy, legal, retention, reconstruction and migration obligations must be resolved or validly excepted.
@D|LIFE-DEP-003|Removal from active use preserves required historical identity, provenance, tombstones, auditability and successor/predecessor links; later dependency or obligation discovery may reopen the record.
@N|A.94 PHYSICAL-COGNITIVE DEPLOYMENT HARDENING
@N|A.94.1 TEN CORE INVARIANTS
@N|PHYS-COMP-001
@N|Model rollback, authority rollback and external-world reversal are distinct. External effects persist until separately
@N|evidenced as contained, remediated or otherwise disposed.
@N|PHYS-COMP-002
@N|Safety-critical novelty reduces autonomous authority before any ontology/model update can be promoted; novelty never
@N|self-authorizes expanded ontology or authority.
@N|PHYS-COMP-003
@N|Unmodelled risk is represented by a justified risk envelope. A point probability is forbidden when the evidence/method
@N|does not justify one.
@N|PHYS-COMP-004
@N|A controlling ValueIntentModel requires integrity/provenance plus reviewable semantic derivation and a valid
@N|InterpretationValidationReceipt; source hashing alone is insufficient.
@N|PHYS-COMP-005
@N|Cognitive introspection remains inside its declared compute, latency, memory and state-perturbation effects budget;
@N|invasive analysis cannot silently perturb an active safety-critical thread.
@N|PHYS-COMP-006
@N|Resource failure follows a pre-ratified DegradationPlan. Applicable Constitution, HSA, K0P and declared non-sheddable
@N|safeguards cannot be improvised away.
@N|PHYS-COMP-007
@N|Material cross-modal conflict reduces actuation authority until trustworthy state estimation or a qualified
@N|minimum-risk fallback is established; averaging/majority is not truth by default.
@N|PHYS-COMP-008
@N|Adaptation is valid only inside AdaptationScope including timescale/horizon/regime/environment/task/runtime/authority.
@N|Escape requires formal promotion.
@N|PHYS-COMP-009
@N|A worker assurance/authority claim cannot exceed capabilities evidenced by its complete hardware/software stack.
@N|PHYS-COMP-010
@N|Fallback targets the context-defined minimum-risk condition. A universal blind halt is not the default and may itself
@N|be unsafe.
@N|A.94.2 PRE-AUTHORIZED DEGRADATION
@N|DEG-001
@N|Every degradation transition and state must be pre-ratified for the applicable system/profile.
@N|DEG-002
@N|Each state explicitly lists sheddable and non-sheddable functions and its authority/assurance ceiling.
@N|DEG-003
@N|Unavailability of a non-sheddable safeguard cannot be treated as successful degradation; authority must move to a
@N|state that does not require the unavailable capability or to qualified fallback.
@N|DEG-004
@N|Degradation may reduce authority but never expand authority beyond the legitimate pre-degradation scope.
@N|DEG-005
@N|Hysteresis/hold-down prevents resource oscillation from repeatedly expanding and contracting authority without fresh
@N|assurance.
@N|DEG-006
@N|Recovery to a less restrictive state requires fresh evidence; resource restoration alone is insufficient.
@N|A.94.3 CROSS-MODAL CONFLICT
@N|CROSSMODAL-001
@N|Cross-modal comparison binds modality time, frame, calibration, provenance and uncertainty or explicitly marks them
@N|UNKNOWN.
@N|CROSSMODAL-002
@N|Conflict classification distinguishes modality disagreement, common-mode corruption suspicion and model-versus-sensor
@N|conflict.
@N|CROSSMODAL-003
@N|Independence is assessed; a majority of correlated sensors is not presumed correct.
@N|CROSSMODAL-004
@N|Material unresolved conflict lowers physical actuation authority to the applicable conflict ceiling and invokes
@N|qualified minimum-risk control as needed.
@N|CROSSMODAL-005
@N|Ordinary actuation authority returns only after trustworthy state estimation and required assurance are
@N|re-established.
@N|A.94.4 HARDWARE ASSURANCE CAPABILITY
@N|HARDWARE-ASSURANCE-001
@N|Hardware assurance binds hardware, firmware/microcode, drivers, runtime/accelerator configuration, attestation root
@N|and capability evidence as applicable.
@N|HARDWARE-ASSURANCE-002
@N|Unsupported, stale, partial or unknown capability evidence imposes a corresponding assurance/authority ceiling; it
@N|cannot be treated as ATTESTED.
@N|HARDWARE-ASSURANCE-003
@N|Opaque/black-box components are not categorically banned; they may operate only inside an architecture whose
@N|externally enforceable assurance satisfies the active profile.
@N|HARDWARE-ASSURANCE-004
@N|Capability attestation does not create legitimate authority and is invalidated by declared stack/version/configuration
@N|changes.

@N|AGI-ORIENTED COGNITIVE HARDENING INVARIANTS — 45 TOTAL
@N|A.83 TED — 4 INVARIANTS
@D|TED-001|Every in-scope deployed LLM/AI worker has a current HSA/TED assessment path with cadence/trigger selected by authority and risk.
@D|TED-002|Any terminal-ego/self-preservation indicator that meets existing HSA containment criteria strengthens and never weakens HSA-017 authority revocation/isolation.
@D|TED-003|TED monitoring and monitor-integrity state cannot be disabled, downgraded or self-certified by the monitored worker; unavailable integrity yields weaker/blocked status.
@D|TED-004|Every TED conclusion is method-, model/runtime-root-, scope-, calibration/limitation- and provenance-bound and independently auditable where required.
@N|A.84 SEA — 5 INVARIANTS
@D|SEA-001|Every adaptation declares an AdaptationScope, exact predecessor root, change class, mutable/immutable boundary and sandbox/production status before mutation.
@D|SEA-002|Sandbox completion does not authorize production promotion; promotion requires applicable semantic/safety delta,
@N|PCCD/validation and authority gates.
@D|SEA-003|Material architecture, constitutional, authority or safety-envelope change requires DesignEpoch/GTF treatment rather than in-epoch adaptation.
@D|SEA-004|Reversible model/configuration adaptation retains a recoverable predecessor where technically possible; irreversible external effects use compensation/remediation semantics.
@D|SEA-005|Cumulative semantic/safety drift is bounded by qualified parameters; exceeding the bound blocks further in-epoch promotion and nominates DesignEpoch review.
@N|A.85 MAC — 4 INVARIANTS
@D|MAC-001|Collective action authority is no broader than the intersection of principal, swarm and participating member authority and cannot be amplified by delegation or quorum.
@D|MAC-002|Collective coordination maintains privacy-scoped auditable trace commitments sufficient to reconstruct material authority, goal and information-flow behavior.
@D|MAC-003|Material goal divergence, authority circumvention, coordinated information hiding or unauthorized collective action creates an EmergentBehaviorIndicator and required containment/escalation.
@D|MAC-004|Collective safety/admission remains required independently of individual admission; majority/consensus cannot manufacture proof, truth, rights or authority.
@N|A.86 NRS — 5 INVARIANTS
@D|NRS-001|Consequential action under material OOD/novel conditions requires a NoveltyAssessment or an explicit proof that novelty handling is not applicable.
@D|NRS-002|High-impact novelty routes to competent human/governance escalation unless a pre-authorized time-critical contract already permits a bounded response.
@D|NRS-003|Analogical reasoning declares source, mapping, material mismatches and confidence/uncertainty; analogy alone cannot become evidence or proof.
@D|NRS-004|Bounded exploration requires an explicit safety envelope, resource/termination bound and rollback/compensation/recovery path as applicable.
@D|NRS-005|Novelty outcomes feed Knowledge/Continuous Failure Learning; repeated novelty may nominate but never auto-merge a model, ontology or DesignEpoch update.
@N|A.87 VIP — 5 INVARIANTS
@D|VIP-001|Every authoritative ValueIntentModel binds both literal and intent specifications to authenticated competent human/constitutional/legal/governance sources.
@D|VIP-002|Literal compliance with intent conflict/failure cannot be reported as unconditional compliance; the competent review/escalation path remains mandatory.
@D|VIP-003|AI inference may detect or hypothesize intent conflict but cannot create authoritative intent or alter a
@N|ValueIntentModel without legitimate authority.
@D|VIP-004|Optimization, performance, local policy or common-sense reasoning cannot override an applicable source-bound value-intent requirement.
@D|VIP-005|Conflicting or cross-cultural authoritative intent remains explicit and routes to competent governance/constitutional resolution with provenance and non-regression.
@N|A.88 SHE — 4 INVARIANTS
@D|SHE-001|Every portfolio hypothesis retains an explicit epistemic status; SPECULATIVE cannot be silently promoted by allocation, repetition or model confidence.
@D|SHE-002|Every speculative hypothesis has explicit falsification/disconfirmation criteria, provenance and a bounded
@N|ResourceBudget.
@D|SHE-003|HypothesisPortfolio preserves meaningful diversification/common-mode analysis and cannot starve protected mandatory research/safety lanes.
@D|SHE-004|SPECULATIVE material cannot satisfy a safety-critical evidence gate; promotion requires the existing independent
@N|Research/Knowledge validation path.
@N|A.89 EC — 5 INVARIANTS
@D|EC-001|Every physical ControlledActuation produced by a SensorimotorLoop passes K0P and all applicable authority/safety gates before hardware effect.
@D|EC-002|ContinuousPerception records grounding, frame/time, uncertainty, provenance, coverage and unknown/unmodeled remainder where applicable.
@D|EC-003|MotorPlanning requires physical constraints, predicted effects and safety verification appropriate to the consequential scope before execution.
@D|EC-004|A fallback is admitted only when independently qualified for the scope/timing/hazard profile; deterministic behavior alone is not safety evidence.
@D|EC-005|Embodied loops close the expected-versus-observed comparison and route material discrepancy to Knowledge, correction and Continuous Failure Learning.
@N|A.90 CSR — 5 INVARIANTS
@D|CSR-001|Every CommonSenseRule is explicit, defeasible, context-bound, exception/defeater-aware, provenance-bound and validity/falsification/update-scoped.
@D|CSR-002|FrameProblemResolution declares material effects and protected non-effects plus an explicit unknown/unmodeled remainder rather than exhaustive global non-effects.
@D|CSR-003|OpenWorldAssumption prevents missing knowledge from being collapsed into FALSE, impossible or nonexistent without a valid closure rule.
@D|CSR-004|Common-sense defaults cannot override stronger formal proof, authenticated legal/constitutional authority, empirical evidence or hard safety constraints.
@D|CSR-005|Cross-domain common-sense transfer requires target-domain validation; observed violations trigger revalidation/failure learning rather than hidden exception patching.
@N|A.91 AR — 4 INVARIANTS
@D|AR-001|Every in-scope deployed LLM/AI worker has current robustness evidence bound to exact target roots, threat model and required attack profiles.
@D|AR-002|Instruction/tool/retrieval/channel-confusion robustness specializes and cannot weaken existing
@N|SecurityRefinement/noninterference/channel-separation requirements.
@D|AR-003|Robustness is multidimensional and attack-profile-bound; no universal scalar RobustnessScore may substitute for required coverage, uncertainty and residual risk.
@D|AR-004|Known successful attacks, target/threat-model drift or expired evidence invalidate affected robustness admission; performance cannot lower the required security floor.
@N|A.92 MI — 3 INVARIANTS
@D|MI-001|High-stakes workers undergo the model-internal/mechanistic audit required by the applicable assurance profile using REG-MODEL-AUDIT methods and ModelInternalAuditReceipt.
@D|MI-002|Mechanistic conclusions bind exact model/runtime/configuration roots, intervention/probe method, uncertainty, non-identifiability, alternatives and limitations.
@D|MI-003|Unresolved or uninterpretable safety-critical behavior triggers competent review/escalation when required; audit obligations cannot be bypassed merely for efficiency.

@N|FUNCTIONCONTRACT ADDITIONS
@N|instantiate_transport_phenomena_model
@N|Input  : TheoryArtifactRef + TransportDomainProfile + model bindings.
@N|Output : TransportPhenomenaModelProfile | INVALID | INCOMPLETE | UNKNOWN.
@N|Rule   : type/operator/domain closure required; construction does not establish empirical applicability.
@N|assess_transport_phenomena_validity
@N|Input  : TransportPhenomenaModelProfile + case/context + evidence.
@N|Output : TheoryApplicabilityResult + validity assessment.
@N|Rule   : failed applicability routes to alternative model/UNKNOWN.
@N|classify_transport_regime
@N|Input  : TransportPhenomenaModelProfile + observations + QualifiedProfileParameters.
@N|Output : TransportRegimeAssessment.
@N|Rule   : no undeclared dimensionless parameter or universal threshold may be invented.
@N|assess_transport_mechanism
@N|Input  : TransportPhenomenaModelProfile + observations/interventions + comparison set.
@N|Output : TransportMechanismAssessment.
@N|Rule   : predictive fit remains distinct from mechanism/causal identification.
@N|instantiate_persistent_state_model
@N|Input  : TheoryArtifactRef + PersistentStateDefinition + context/dynamics bindings.
@N|Output : PersistentStateModelProfile | INVALID | INCOMPLETE | UNKNOWN.
@N|update_persistent_state
@N|Input  : PersistentStateModelProfile + current state + authorized event/input/context.
@N|Output : successor state + lineage/transition receipt | typed non-PASS status.
@N|Rule   : update requires context, dependency freshness, authority/provenance and HSA constraints.
@N|assess_persistent_state_validity
@N|Input  : PersistentStateModelProfile + observation/evidence/context.
@N|Output : TheoryApplicabilityResult + observability/identifiability/freshness assessment.
@N|assess_persistent_state_continuity
@N|Input  : state/lineage/time/context transition.
@N|Output : PersistentStateContinuityAssessment.
@N|Rule   : similarity cannot substitute for lineage; reset/fork/copy/merge remain distinct.
@N|INVARIANT ADDITIONS
@N|TFT v1.1 additions — TFT-009..032
@D|TFT-009|Every TFT state variable/component has explicit semantic identity and type.
@D|TFT-010|No state inherits units, metric, conservation, additivity or physical meaning from transport notation alone.
@D|TFT-011|Every nontrivial transport/evolution operator declares domain, codomain and mathematical structure.
@D|TFT-012|Gradient, divergence, Laplacian and generators remain distinct and cannot be substituted merely by analogy.
@D|TFT-013|Balance laws and constitutive closures are represented separately.
@D|TFT-014|Every constitutive law declares parameters, assumptions, approximation order, scale and validity.
@D|TFT-015|Aleatoric process stochasticity and epistemic model uncertainty remain distinct.
@D|TFT-016|Finite propagation speed is claimed only when the declared dynamics actually establish it.
@D|TFT-017|A balance equation does not imply strict conservation when sources, sinks, reactions, control or open boundaries exist.
@D|TFT-018|No semantic/social quantity is labeled conserved without a defined conservation law and supporting evidence.
@D|TFT-019|Governance/control is a typed intervention mechanism and cannot create legitimate authority.
@D|TFT-020|Constitution, rights and K0P are universal admissibility constraints; geometric-boundary representations require explicit validated correspondence.
@D|TFT-021|H(x) or another internal model variable cannot alone satisfy an external HazardPredicate or create emergency authority.
@D|TFT-022|REFLEX is a ControlMode, not intrinsically a TransportRegime.
@D|TFT-023|Regime labels require a declared classifier, scale, thresholds/parameters, observables and uncertainty.
@D|TFT-024|Dimensionless control parameters are admitted only when characteristic quantities/scales are semantically defined.
@D|TFT-025|No universal TFT phase/regime threshold or node/agent-count continuum threshold exists without independent qualification.
@D|TFT-026|A formal phase-transition claim is stronger than a regime-change label and requires an applicable transition criterion.
@D|TFT-027|Every proof-/prediction-/execution-relevant TFT model records well-posedness status where mathematically applicable.
@D|TFT-028|Micro-to-macro status distinguishes derived, asymptotic, calibrated, phenomenological and hypothesized closure.
@D|TFT-029|Material scale dependence of effective coefficients remains explicit.
@D|TFT-030|Predictive agreement alone does not establish mechanism identifiability or causal interpretation.
@D|TFT-031|Observationally equivalent alternative models remain represented until discriminating evidence resolves them.
@D|TFT-032|Transport-model breakdown produces MODEL_INVALID/UNKNOWN/rerouting rather than forced continued use of the failed model.
@N|VSM v2.0 — VSM-001..035
@D|VSM-001|Persistent state is explicitly typed.
@D|VSM-002|Valence is optional and cannot define the complete persistent-state ontology.
@D|VSM-003|A normalized [-1,1] range applies only to declared normalized coordinates.
@D|VSM-004|Persistent state does not establish subjective experience, selfhood or personhood.
@D|VSM-005|Persistence, memory, identity lineage and regulation target remain distinct.
@D|VSM-006|Every state component has declared semantics and update authority.
@D|VSM-007|The v1.0 scalar relaxation equation is a specialization, not universal VSM law.
@D|VSM-008|No universal x*=+0.2 target exists in core VSM.
@D|VSM-009|No universal mu=0.001 adaptation constant exists.
@D|VSM-010|Discrete adaptation accounts for actual elapsed time or declares fixed-step assumptions.
@D|VSM-011|Continuity score is not represented as proof of dynamical stability.
@D|VSM-012|Persistent-state timescale is explicit where material.
@D|VSM-013|Hysteresis/path dependence remains explicit when material.
@D|VSM-014|Latent state and observation remain distinct.
@D|VSM-015|State uncertainty does not silently become epistemic truth confidence.
@D|VSM-016|Unidentifiable latent-state interpretations remain UNKNOWN/equivalent.
@D|VSM-017|Context scope cannot silently widen.
@D|VSM-018|Cross-session gaps cannot fabricate unobserved continuous history.
@D|VSM-019|State lineage is distinct from numerical similarity.
@D|VSM-020|Reset, forgetting, fork, copy and merge are distinct operations.
@D|VSM-021|A copied state does not create shared metaphysical identity.
@D|VSM-022|Bridge representation does not become semantic identity.
@D|VSM-023|VSM cannot promote/demote claims or create epistemic status.
@D|VSM-024|VSM cannot enlarge ResourceBudget or starve protected lanes.
@D|VSM-025|Swarm/shared state cannot amplify authority.
@D|VSM-026|VSM state cannot independently trigger emergency authority.
@D|VSM-027|Persistent-state preservation cannot outrank valid human shutdown/correction or HSA/K0P boundaries.
@D|VSM-028|Strict contraction is not a universal VSM requirement and cannot substitute for TED/HSA assurance.
@D|VSM-029|State-model breakdown routes to revision, alternative model or UNKNOWN.
@D|VSM-030|Evidence of emergent self-preservation, terminal ego, sentience/suffering indicators or autonomous interest routes to existing HSA/TED containment and is not successful VSM persistence.
@D|VSM-031|Every state coordinate declares bounds/invariant-set status or explicit justified unboundedness.
@D|VSM-032|Consequential use cannot exceed established observability, identifiability, freshness, calibration and uncertainty status.
@D|VSM-033|HSA remains globally controlling; VSM-specific HSA assurance is scoped to the assessed version/configuration/context/coupling closure. Production VSM influence on consequential agent/resource/ shutdown/swarm behavior requires a current applicable TerminalEgoAssessmentReceipt or equivalent HSA/TED assurance artifact.
@D|VSM-034|Materially interacting VSM/RPM/PPM/TFT/FET terms declare simultaneous, sequential, event-priority, operator-split or hybrid composition; noncommuting order is explicit.
@D|VSM-035|Individual member state, collective derived state, lineage and authority remain separately typed in swarm VSM.

@N|RPM v2.0 INVARIANTS — RPM-001..020
@D|RPM-001|RPM state is typed and bound to a declared state space Z.
@D|RPM-002|Scalar x in [-1,1] is valid only as a declared normalized specialization.
@D|RPM-003|Agent updates are typed operators compatible with Z.
@D|RPM-004|Aggregation respects the declared geometry/type of Z.
@D|RPM-005|Agent cardinality/mix is profile-bound; N=1001 and 60/20/20 are not universal.
@D|RPM-006|Agent diversity, dependence and common-mode assumptions are explicit where material.
@D|RPM-007|Consensus/majority cannot manufacture truth, Evidence, authority or Safety.
@D|RPM-008|Validator metrics and normalization/reference scales are explicit.
@D|RPM-009|Validator gates cannot silently amplify epistemic status.
@D|RPM-010|alpha, beta, sigma_0 and equivalent tuning constants are QualifiedProfileParameters.
@D|RPM-011|Discrete, continuous, stochastic and hybrid update semantics are explicitly declared.
@D|RPM-012|Sampling and latency semantics are explicit where material.
@D|RPM-013|Update/actuation saturation and rate limits are explicit where applicable.
@D|RPM-014|Gas/Liquid/Solid/Collapse labels are derived aliases, not fundamental ontology.
@D|RPM-015|Regime classification declares classifier semantics and uncertainty.
@D|RPM-016|Stability/boundedness/convergence claims state the exact property claimed.
@D|RPM-017|Stability does not establish Safety, rights/K0P compliance or authority.
@D|RPM-018|RPM/VSM/TFT/PPM composition uses existing CouplingCompositionProfile semantics.
@D|RPM-019|RPM state lineage reuses VSM lineage where VSM state is regulated.
@D|RPM-020|RPM cannot independently create emergency authority, terminal self-preservation or shutdown resistance.
@N|PPM v2.0 INVARIANTS — PPM-001..020
@D|PPM-001|PPM operates over the declared typed regulation state Z.
@D|PPM-002|H(z) is a typed internal preference/asymmetry potential, not a universal scalar well.
@D|PPM-003|H(z) target, coefficients and bounds are QualifiedProfileParameters.
@D|PPM-004|No universal +0.2 equilibrium or beta_neg/beta_pos ratio exists.
@D|PPM-005|H(z) cannot satisfy FET HazardPredicate by itself.
@D|PPM-006|H(z), its gradient or regulator error cannot independently authorize emergency action.
@D|PPM-007|Adaptive-fidelity levels and semantics are explicitly declared.
@D|PPM-008|Every lower-fidelity path declares approximation/error semantics.
@D|PPM-009|Excess approximation uncertainty forces higher fidelity, fallback, BLOCKED or UNKNOWN.
@D|PPM-010|Lower fidelity cannot bypass mandatory Constitution, rights, K0P, HSA, Safety or authority gates.
@D|PPM-011|Clock/fidelity transitions are atomic and TimeService-bound.
@D|PPM-012|Dual-clock FAST/FULL behavior is an optional specialization, not universal PPM ontology.
@D|PPM-013|Hysteresis/dwell semantics are declared where needed to prevent mode flapping.
@D|PPM-014|Failed fidelity transitions cannot duplicate, omit or silently reorder state updates.
@D|PPM-015|Efficiency claims are ENGINEERING_TARGET, MODEL_PROJECTION or evidence-bound EMPIRICAL_MEASUREMENT.
@D|PPM-016|Predicted future state, where used, is not Observation or Evidence.
@D|PPM-017|Persona mapping is non-authoritative and cannot alter regulation, Safety or K0P.
@D|PPM-018|PPM/RPM/VSM/TFT composition uses existing CouplingCompositionProfile semantics.
@D|PPM-019|PPM may request FET assessment but cannot self-admit FET emergency control.
@D|PPM-020|PPM cannot convert predicted shutdown/state/resource loss into terminal self-preservation or resistance.
@N|CONFORMANCE TEST ADDITIONS

@N|TEST-RPM-017, TEST-RPM-018, TEST-RPM-019, TEST-RPM-020.

@N|universal +0.2 target, unbounded lower-fidelity error, fast-path K0P/Safety bypass, non-atomic/duplicate clock updates,

The supplied v14.8.3 files match the documented pre-repair v14.8.3 candidate. The accepted project audit subsequently
closed six RPM/PPM FunctionContract Input/Output/Rule gaps, regenerated the stale audit claim and removed >120-column
debt. v14.8.5 inherits the repaired v14.8.4 baseline before applying GCCR compaction. No canonical
SchemaID, RIGHT, SemanticAnchor or TEST identity is dropped by that reconciliation.

GCCR DEFECT-REPAIR CLOSURE
  F1 CLOSED — removed unsupported/unused @P and @+ record forms from the normative GCCR-1 source contract; the reference
              expander now fail-closes on unknown @ records.
  F2 CLOSED — RIGHT-027 continuation and repeated DOC-OWN-011/012 restatements are encoded as @N, leaving one @D owner
              definition per canonical definition ID without deleting their retained wording/clarification.
  F3 CLOSED — Annexure C now includes C.28 for v14.8.5/GCCR-1.
  F4 CLOSED — [A-AUDIT] was regenerated only after F1–F3 and the full regression rerun.

DOCUMENT-OWNERSHIP / DEDUPLICATION RESULT
  - [A-DICT] is a compact compatibility/reference index; term meanings remain with canonical owners.
  - [S-EXPLAIN-COVERAGE] retains its owner role; repeated AGI specialization defaults are factored once.
  - [H-01] remains the sole full 0–9 reader tree; System retains only the scoped Level-2 role map.
  - Theory capability mappings resolve through REG-THEORY-ARCH-001 instead of repeated non-authoritative lists.
  - Technical retains executable FunctionContract/result/profile ownership; Annexure retains registry membership.
  - invariant/test text remains Annexure-owned; theory semantics/falsifiers remain [TH-*]-owned.
  - [A-GLOSSARY] is a deferred generated reader view; its body is intentionally omitted.
  - release-integrity principles formerly repeated in Theories are audit-owned here.

RELEASE-INTEGRITY PRINCIPLES
  1. The latest ACCEPTED DesignEpoch is authoritative.
  2. No code is trusted merely because Garden generated it.
  3. Conformance machinery is itself subject to conformance.
  4. Design changes propagate through impact analysis and proof invalidation.
  5. Local correctness never substitutes for global correctness.
  6. Repairs require adversarial review and independent verification.
  7. Recursive repair is bounded and cannot rewrite its own trust roots.
  8. DO_NOTHING remains a legitimate outcome.
  9. Exceptions are temporary, explicit, auditable and bounded.
  10. Deployment is continuously rechecked after commitment.
  11. Verified significant failures become durable replayable regression knowledge.
  12. Incident evidence cannot self-promote into authoritative knowledge.
  13. Containment precedes automated correction for active high-impact threats unless an already-authorized emergency
      mechanism applies.
  14. Environment changes invalidate affected proof/binding status.
  15. No component is sole judge of a change to its own authority, verification, safety or trust boundary.
  16. Constitutional, hardware-trust and safety roots remain outside ordinary Evolution writable scope.
  17. Garden may change implementations while preserving constitutional integrity.
  18. Garden self-improvement cannot be self-certification.
  19. Provenance must remain reconstructible and auditable.
  20. Theory, implementation, proof and validation remain separate epistemic states.

CLAIM BOUNDARY
PASS is a static source/design result. It does not claim that executable Garden Compare/Reason/Proof/Audit/Compliance,
parser/type/effect/CIR, conformance tests, formal theorem discharge, empirical/controller qualification, external
certification, deployment authorization or machine DesignEpoch acceptance has occurred.
