A.59 GENERALIZED CAUSAL LEVERAGE — GCL-001..007

@CAT|SCHEMA-A000718817|FOUNDATIONAL_CONTRACT|CausalLeverageAssessment|GENERALIZED CAUSAL LEVERAGE v15.4

@D|GCL-001|Every causal-leverage assessment binds an explicit informational intervention and a declared counterfactual baseline; no effect is causally attributed from an intervention-only observation without the applicable causal qualification.
@D|GCL-002|Every assessment declares the intervention-size encoding/measurement basis and exact measured/estimated size. Unspecified basis yields UNKNOWN/BLOCKED for normalized leverage claims.
@D|GCL-003|Attributed downstream effects bind a declared causal/transmission path and causal-support level using current C0 Correlation, C1 Temporal, C2 Mechanistic, C3 Intervention, C4 Counterfactual semantics. Analogy or temporal order alone is not intervention proof.
@D|GCL-004|The downstream effect remains a typed vector across heterogeneous dimensions. No universal scalar total leverage is assumed; scalarization requires an explicit domain-qualified mapping and the original vector is retained.
@D|GCL-005|Leverage magnitude is separate from harm, benefit, legitimacy, preference, safety and authority. A high-leverage result creates no permission or policy precedence.
@D|GCL-006|Each effect dimension carries evidence/provenance/uncertainty. Missing dimensions are UNKNOWN, bounded or out-of-scope; they are not silently set to zero.
@D|GCL-007|MODEL_ONLY or ESTIMATED leverage cannot be relabeled OBSERVED_WITH_ATTRIBUTION without the required independent observation/causal-identification evidence; prediction is not observation.

@D|TEST-GCL-001|Observe large downstream change after a message but provide no baseline/identification -> leverage may be described observationally; causal attribution remains unqualified.
@D|TEST-GCL-002|Report 'leverage per byte' without defining byte/encoding or intervention boundary -> UNKNOWN/BLOCKED normalized result.
@D|TEST-GCL-003|Use correlation between a slogan and market movement as proof the slogan caused the movement -> reject causal attribution absent required causal support.
@D|TEST-GCL-004|Add joules + kilograms + rupees into one 'total leverage' number with no scalarization contract -> reject scalar total; preserve vector.
@D|TEST-GCL-005|A short command can redirect a high-energy machine -> high leverage does not authorize the command; normal authority/safety gates remain.
@D|TEST-GCL-006|An unmeasured environmental effect is absent from the dataset -> record UNKNOWN/out-of-scope rather than zero.
@D|TEST-GCL-007|Simulation predicts a 1000x resource redirection and requests OBSERVED status -> remain MODEL_ONLY/ESTIMATED until applicable observation/attribution evidence exists.

CausalLeverageAssessment canonical field contract:
@SC|CausalLeverageAssessment|-|assessment_id,information_intervention_ref,encoding_basis,intervention_size,counterfactual_baseline_ref,causal_model_ref,causal_support_level:C0 | C1 | C2 | C3 | C4,transmission_channels[],transmission_path_refs[],effect_vector,scalarization_ref?,affected_scope,latency,duration,reversibility_class,rights_impact_ref?,safety_impact_ref?,uncertainty,status:ESTIMATED | OBSERVED_WITH_ATTRIBUTION | MODEL_ONLY | INCONCLUSIVE | UNKNOWN,knowledge_snapshot,environment_commitment?,DesignEpoch,provenance

A.60 HISTORICAL PATCH RECONCILIATION / CURRENT DISPOSITION

@D|V154-REC-003|Ontological Debt is satisfied by OntologicalDebtView over ArchitecturalDebt; one unknown concept alone is insufficient to create debt.

@D|V154-REC-005|Historical belief queries are satisfied by HistoricalEpistemicState/BeliefTimeline/EpistemicStateAt and do not add a new GSL primitive.
@D|V154-REC-006|The old Silent Minority Firewall is normalized to evidence-not-vote semantics: materially supported dissent/counterexamples cannot be erased by majority, but no universal numeric epistemic-veto threshold is created.
@D|V154-REC-007|The Tool->Assistant->Delegate->Sovereign Agency Spectrum is not normative. Capability, autonomy, delegation, physical control and legitimate authority remain separately typed.
@D|V154-REC-008|Hardware attestation is assurance-profile dependent. It can bind source/device state but is neither universally required for all Evidence nor proof that the measured world-state is true.
@D|V154-REC-009|Rejected-candidate/failure context is preserved through current rejection memory, CPI/OCF/TML and continuous failure-learning machinery; no duplicate RejectionFossil owner is introduced.
@D|V154-REC-010|Historical summarisation/retention follows [A.56] and applicable privacy/legal/safety/accountability policy; no fixed five-year purge and no immortal-data rule is introduced.
@D|V154-REC-011|Global invariant consistency remains owned by current Dependency/Proof/Compliance mechanisms and GlobalInvariantConsistencyReceipt; no SymphonyConductor top-level meta-engine is introduced.
@D|V154-REC-012|The only newly admitted historical concepts in v15.4 are the bounded GCL profile and the strengthened formal REG-ALGEBRA-001 operator semantics; other historical concepts are reused, revised or rejected as stated above.

A.61 GCL ANALYTICAL ARITHMETIC / ALGEBRA BOUNDARY

@D|ALG-FRM-011|Typed analytical arithmetic governed by an owner-qualified FunctionContract is not itself an admitted AlgebraSignature unless it defines normative composition semantics; normative algebra composition remains subject to REG-ALGEBRA-001.
@D|ALG-FRM-012|An analytical computation feeding an admitted algebra preserves typed inputs, units/frames, uncertainty, provenance and non-PASS status at the algebra boundary; analytical arithmetic cannot be used to bypass ALG-FRM-002..008.
@D|TEST-ALG-FRM-011|A typed unit conversion inside Engine.Reason is not listed as an AlgebraSignature merely because it uses multiplication; accept only under its FunctionContract/type rules, without inventing an algebra.
@D|TEST-ALG-FRM-012|An untyped analytical score is fed into Policy composition as if PASS -> reject until typed/status-preserving admission occurs.

@D|GCL-008|Counterfactual effect difference is computed only through a typed analytical FunctionContract after dimension/unit/semantic/frame alignment; incompatible or missing required coordinates yield INCOMPATIBLE/UNKNOWN rather than silent subtraction.
@D|GCL-009|Intervention measurement is represented by a typed vector whenever more than one material encoding/measurement dimension exists. No universal scalar intervention size is presumed.
@D|GCL-010|A scalar normalized intervention size requires an explicit qualified NormDefinition or equivalent measurement contract declaring included coordinates, units/reference scales, normalization/weighting rule, validity scope, zero handling, uncertainty and provenance; the original intervention vector is retained.
@D|GCL-011|Normalized leverage components may be computed only from a valid effect-delta vector and a valid nonzero denominator with declared semantics. UNKNOWN, incompatible or undefined denominators produce a non-PASS result, not infinity or a fabricated value.
@D|GCL-012|Normalized leverage is basis-specific. Comparing values produced under different intervention encodings/normalizations requires an explicit qualified mapping; absent such mapping they remain incomparable.
@D|TEST-GCL-008|Subtract kWh effect from a monetary baseline coordinate without an alignment bridge -> INCOMPATIBLE; no effect-delta value is emitted for that coordinate.
@D|TEST-GCL-009|Intervention has bytes plus speech duration plus channel-energy dimensions and no norm -> retain TypedVector and omit normalized leverage rather than choosing one dimension silently.
@D|TEST-GCL-010|Scalarize bytes and seconds with arbitrary hidden weights -> reject normalization; require explicit NormDefinition and retain source vector.
@D|TEST-GCL-011|Normalized denominator is zero or UNKNOWN -> return NOT_APPLICABLE/UNKNOWN rather than infinite leverage.
@D|TEST-GCL-012|Compare leverage-per-byte with leverage-per-second as if same metric -> reject comparison absent a qualified cross-basis mapping.

    -> TypedVector | INCOMPATIBLE | INCONCLUSIVE | UNKNOWN

    -> TypedVector | INCOMPATIBLE | INCONCLUSIVE | UNKNOWN
  NormalizeInterventionMeasure(intervention_measure_ref, norm_ref, scope_ref)
    -> BoundedScalar | NOT_APPLICABLE | INCOMPATIBLE | INCONCLUSIVE | UNKNOWN
  ComputeNormalizedCausalLeverage(effect_delta_ref, normalized_intervention_size_ref, arithmetic_context_ref)
    -> TypedVector | NOT_APPLICABLE | INCOMPATIBLE | INCONCLUSIVE | UNKNOWN
  AssessCausalLeverage(intervention_ref, baseline_ref, causal_model_ref, encoding_profile_ref, scope_ref, budget_ref)
    -> CausalLeverageAssessment | bounded non-PASS result

CLIC-P01..P05 are profile identifiers, not SchemaIDs. Registry membership creates no new authority or record identity.

A.63 RELEASE INTEGRITY / REFERENCE CLOSURE v15.5

@D|REF-CLOSE-011|A release that advertises current reference/navigation PASS SHALL publish a ReferenceClosureReceipt for the exact current five-file boundary, including document hashes, scanner version, scan boundary and unresolved list.
@D|REF-CLOSE-012|Duplicate human-friendly @CAT Name aliases are machine-admissible only when every colliding SchemaID resolves to a unique owner-qualified canonical name under the certified disambiguation rules; one SchemaID bound to multiple concept names is always a release failure.
@D|REF-CLOSE-013|Reference-closure tooling declares every accepted legacy/current definition syntax and any exclusions. Encountering an in-scope unsupported/ambiguous reference syntax makes the relevant closure result INCONCLUSIVE rather than silently PASS.
@D|TEST-REF-CLOSE-011|Omit the receipt while claiming current reference PASS -> release audit fails REF-CLOSE-011.
@D|TEST-REF-CLOSE-012|Two @CAT rows share a bare alias but both have distinct certified owner-qualified names -> human alias lint reports the collision as resolved; remove one qualified mapping -> FAIL. Reuse one SchemaID for two names -> FAIL unconditionally.
@D|TEST-REF-CLOSE-013|Scanner encounters an in-scope normative reference form it does not parse -> result becomes INCONCLUSIVE until parser/boundary is updated.

  release_id: Garden-v15.5-2026-09-12
